Herbal Formulas 方劑學格式範例 v3：01 · Calming Shen 安神劑

v4 新增：① 左上角加了繁體／簡體切換（透過 OpenCC 引擎即時轉換全頁中文，需要網路連線）；② 字體縮放（A−／重設／A+）；③ 每組最後加入本組方劑比較總表，把該組所有方劑的證型、病機、功效、辨證重點、特別提醒濃縮成一張表方便總複習；④ 左側目錄維持深色分類卡片＋搜尋框（中/英/拼音皆可搜）。

內容忠實度原則（已依你的要求調整）：凡原文有英文，一律同時呈現英文＋中文，能左右對照就左右對照；若原文某段落只有中文（是你當時上課的筆記，完全沒有對應英文），我會直接保留成中文筆記呈現，不會擅自「翻譯」出一段英文假裝是原文——如果我有加註輔助翻譯或推測用詞（例如原文用字跟教科書標準寫法不同、或原文語意不夠明確），會用小字/括號明確標示這是我加的說明，不會不加註記地更動或美化你的原始筆記。

01 · Calming Shen 安神劑

▍組別總覽 Group Overview — 本組 4 方劑速記

English中文
Zhu Sha An Shen Wan — cinnabar is heavy, so it sinks and calms the spirit; treats acute Excess Heart Fire; short-term use only.
硃砂安神丸——硃砂質重，故能下沉安神，專治急性心火實證；只能短期使用（唯一「攻」性安神方）。
Suan Zao Ren Tang — sour enters the liver, nourishes liver blood; treats insomnia from Liver blood deficiency (liver blood ↓ → heart blood material ↓ → heart blood deficiency insomnia).
酸棗仁湯——酸入肝、補肝血，治肝血不足之失眠（肝血不足 → 心血原料不足 → 心血不足而失眠）。
Tian Wang Bu Xin Dan — commonly used for insomnia; Kidney yin deficiency fails to control heart's empty fire, causing insomnia — core strategy is actually to tonify kidney water.
天王補心丹——常用於失眠；腎陰虛致無法制約心之虛火，心虛火旺而失眠——重點其實在補腎水。
Gan Mai Da Zao Tang — not a five-zang deficiency pattern; specifically for Zang-Zao (menopausal/emotional crying) — deficiency on the "spirit" level.
甘麥大棗湯——非五臟虛實證，而是專治臟躁（更年期情緒性哭泣）——精神層面之血不足。

▍失眠方劑比較表 Comparison of Insomnia Formulas（含跨組方劑）

English中文
Zhu Sha An Shen Wan — excess heart fire, attacking method
硃砂安神丸——實火上炎，攻邪為主
Suan Zao Ren Tang — liver blood deficiency; nourish liver blood + clear heat + relieve irritability; safe, no major contraindication
酸棗仁湯——肝血不足；養肝血＋清熱＋除煩；安全無明顯禁忌
Tian Wang Bu Xin Dan — kidney yin deficiency with empty heart fire; treat root by nourishing kidney water
天王補心丹——腎陰虛心虛火旺；治本在補腎水
Gui Pi Tang (see Group 17 · Tonifying) — heart-spleen dual deficiency from overuse of the mind; insomnia + poor memory
歸脾湯 （見 17·補益劑）——用腦過度耗傷心脾兩虛；失眠兼記憶力下降
Ren Shen Yang Rong Tang (see Group 17 · Tonifying) — qi and blood deficiency + calm spirit
人蔘養榮湯 （見 17·補益劑）——氣血兩虛兼安神
Gan Mai Da Zao Tang — Zang-Zao; calms spirit / resolves visceral agitation (atypical five-zang pattern)
甘麥大棗湯——臟躁；寧神、緩解臟躁（非典型五臟虛實證）

▍安神常用單味藥 Key Herbs for Calming Shen

English中文
Cinnabar (Zhusha) — sweet, cold, heavy; enters the Heart channel specifically; cold descends fire, heaviness settles fright.
硃砂——甘寒質重，專入心經；寒能降火、重能鎮怯。
Dragon's Bone (Longgu) — subdues yang and calms spirit; for yin deficiency with yang rising: irritability, palpitations/insomnia, dizziness, mania/fright-epilepsy.
龍骨——潛陽安神，用於陰虛陽亢：煩躁易怒、心悸失眠、頭暈目眩、癲狂驚癇。
Wild Jujube Seed (Suanzaoren) — enters Heart and Liver channels; nourishes heart yin, benefits heart and liver blood to calm the spirit.
酸棗仁——入心、肝經，能養心陰，益心、肝之血而安神。
Arborvitae Seed (Baiziren) — moistens the intestines; nourishes heart and calms spirit; for heart yin deficiency / heart-kidney disharmony palpitations-insomnia; also for yin-blood deficiency failing to nourish the spirit.
柏子仁——潤腸；養心安神；用於心陰虛及心腎不交之心悸失眠，亦用於陰血不足心神失養。
Polygala Root (Yuanzhi) — opens heart qi to settle the spirit, and connects kidney qi to strengthen memory; for heart-kidney disharmony with restlessness, fright palpitations, insomnia, forgetfulness; also resolves phlegm misting the heart orifices (mania/epilepsy), enters the lung to resolve phlegm and stop cough, and disperses toxic swellings.
遠志——開心氣而寧心安神，又能通腎氣強志不忘；用於心腎不交之心神不寧、驚悸、失眠健忘；亦可袪痰開竅（痰阻心竅、癲癇發狂）、入肺袪痰止咳、消散癰腫。
Albizia Bark (Hehuanpi) — relieves depression, harmonizes blood, calms the heart, disperses toxic swellings; enters Heart and Liver channels.
合歡皮——解鬱、和血、寧心、消癰腫；入心、肝二經。

▍失眠病機 Pathogenesis of Insomnia（心／肝兩軸）

〔Heart 心〕English中文
Heart fire — the most direct, simple cause, usually from yin insufficiency; treat by purging fire first.
心火——最直接單純的病因，多因陰不足所致，治療先以瀉火為主。
Heart yin deficiency — empty heat + kidney yin insufficiency; treat with clearing heat + nourishing yin.
心陰虛——虛熱＋腎陰不足，治以清熱＋滋陰。
Heart blood deficiency — two causes: ① overuse of the mind (as in Gui Pi Tang pattern) ② liver blood deficiency as root cause, eventually leading to heart blood deficiency (liver stores blood, heart uses blood).
心血虛——成因有二：① 過度耗神（如歸脾湯證）② 肝血不足為主因，最終導致心血不足（肝藏血、心用血）。
📝 課堂筆記（原文僅中文）
神門（HT7）主治「神」的門戶不穩；內關（PC6）主治「神」在胸口悸動跳躍感。
〔Liver 肝〕English中文
Liver blood deficiency — from yin insufficiency.
肝血虛——陰不足所致。
Liver yang rising — kidney yin insufficiency causing liver yang to rise.
肝陽上亢——腎陰不足導致肝陽上亢。
Liver fire — emotions (anger) triggering liver fire.
肝火——情緒（怒）引動肝火。
⚠ 原稿 ＊＊ 重點提醒：肝相關方劑速記表
Liver qi stagnation invading the Spleen → Xiao Yao San
肝氣滯犯脾 → 逍遙散
Liver fire invading the Stomach → Zuo Jin Wan
肝火犯胃 → 左金丸
Liver blood deficiency with empty heart fire → Suan Zao Ren Tang
肝血虛而心虛火 → 酸棗仁湯
Liver fire reverse-insulting the Lung → Ke Xue Fang
肝火旺反侮肺 → 咳血方
▍EXAM FOCUS — 本組出題重點
  • 四方鑑別是最常考型式：同樣是「失眠 insomnia」，但根本證型完全不同——心火實證（硃砂安神丸）／肝血虛（酸棗仁湯）／腎陰虛心火旺（天王補心丹）／臟躁（甘麥大棗湯），務必能依症狀組合反推證型與方劑。
  • 硃砂安神丸是本組唯一「攻」法方劑，其餘皆偏補——考題常以「哪一方不宜久服／哪一方含礦物藥」測試。
  • 硃砂安神丸之脾胃虛弱者禁用、毒性須靠炮製降低，是常見的禁忌考點。
  • 酸棗仁湯的君臣佐使（酸棗仁君、知母/茯苓臣、川芎佐、甘草使）常被單獨考「君藥為何」。
  • 天王補心丹雖名為「補心」，但病機重點其實是腎陰虛——常考「治本在何臟」的陷阱題。
  • 甘麥大棗湯只有 3 味藥（甘草、小麥、大棗），常考「藥味數目最少的安神方」與「臟躁」定義（多見於婦人，因憂思過度、心陰受損、肝氣失和）。
Zhu Sha An Shen Wan ⚠ 有毒 Toxic
硃砂安神丸【急性有毒／心火＋陰血虛】
Cinnabar Pill to Calm the Spirit
組成
Ingredients
  • Cinnabar (Zhusha) 15 g — chief herb; calms spirit (★ toxic, per original notes)
  • Coptis (Huanglian) 18 g — clears heat
  • Fresh Rehmannia (Shengdihuang) 8 g — cools blood, nourishes blood
  • Chinese Angelica (Danggui) 8 g — nourishes blood
  • Licorice (Gancao) 8 g — harmonizes the formula
  • 硃砂 15 g — 君藥；安神（★ 原稿標記有毒）
  • 黃連 18 g — 清熱
  • 生地黃 8 g — 涼血、補血
  • 當歸 8 g — 補血
  • 甘草 8 g — 調和諸藥
功效
Functions
Tranquilize the mind and purge heart fire.
鎮定安神、瀉心火。
📝 課堂筆記（原文僅中文）
唯一比較有攻擊性的安神藥，治療急性失眠，一般的安神藥都是補居多（長期失眠）。
主治
Indication
Disturbance of the mind by the flaring up of heart fire with yin and blood deficiency.
因陰血虛而心火上炎所致的神志不安——屬陰虛火旺型的急性失眠。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Insomnia (acute)
  • Dreaminess
  • Severe palpitations with fright
  • Irritability
  • Red tongue tip
  • Weak and rapid pulse
  • 急性失眠
  • 多夢
  • 心悸、易受驚
  • 煩躁易怒
  • 舌尖紅
  • 脈虛數（陰虛火旺之象）
加減
Modification
  • Chest distress + greasy tongue coating (phlegm-heat) → add Gualou, Zhuru
  • Severe palpitations/insomnia → add Longgu (Dragon's bone), Muli (Oyster shell), Cishi (Magnetite)
  • Caution: toxic — avoid large dose/prolonged use; contraindicated in spleen-stomach deficiency
  • 痰熱而胸悶舌苔膩：加栝蔞、竹茹
  • 心悸失眠嚴重：加龍骨、牡蠣、磁石
  • ⚠ 使用禁忌：硃砂有毒，不宜大量或久服（炮製可降低毒性）；脾胃虛弱者禁用
Suan Zao Ren Tang
酸棗仁湯【肝血虛】
Sour Jujube Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Chief — Wild Jujube Seed (Suanzaoren) 15 g (sour enters the liver)
  • Deputy — Anemarrhena Rhizome (Zhimu) 10 g
  • Deputy — Poria (Fuling) 10 g
  • Assistant — Chuanxiong Rhizome (Chuanxiong) 5 g
  • Envoy — Licorice (Gancao) 3 g
  • 君 — 酸棗仁 15 g （酸入肝）
  • 臣 — 知母 10 g — 清熱、滋陰
  • 臣 — 茯苓 10 g — 寧心、滲濕
  • 佐 — 川芎 5 g — 行氣活血
  • 使 — 甘草 3 g — 調和諸藥
功效
Functions
Nourish the liver blood and soothe the mind; clear heat and relieve irritability.
養肝血以安神；清熱降煩躁。
主治
Indication
Disturbance of the mind due to liver blood deficiency.
治療長期性肝血不足導致的心神不寧。屬安全性高、無明顯禁忌的方劑。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Insomnia
  • Palpitations
  • Irritability
  • Dizziness
  • Vertigo
  • Dry throat and mouth
  • Reddened tongue
  • Wiry and thready pulse
  • 失眠
  • 心悸
  • 煩躁
  • 頭暈
  • 眩暈
  • 咽乾口燥
  • 舌紅
  • 脈弦細
加減
Modification
  • Severe yin deficiency with internal heat → add Baishao (White peony root), Shengdihuang
  • Severe night sweating → add Wuweizi (Schisandra fruit)
  • Severe anxiety from liver blood deficiency → add Longgu, Muli
  • 陰虛火旺明顯：加白芍、生地黃（涼血養陰）
  • 夜汗嚴重：加五味子（斂汗）
  • 肝血虛而焦慮嚴重：加龍骨、牡蠣
Tian Wang Bu Xin Dan
天王補心丹【心腎不交，腎陰虧而心虛火】
Emperor of Heaven's Pill to Tonify the Heart
組成
Ingredients
  • Chief (nourish heart/liver yin, clear heat, calm spirit) — Fresh Rehmannia (Shengdihuang) 20 g
  • Deputy (assist Shengdi to nourish yin/clear heat) — Scrophularia (Xuanshen) 15 g, Asparagus Root (Tianmendong) 20 g, Ophiopogon Root (Maidong) 20 g, Red Sage Root (Danshen) 15 g
  • (guide the formula, boost qi to calm spirit) — Chinese Angelica Root (Danggui) 20 g, Ginseng (Renshen) 15 g, Poria (Fuling) 15 g
  • Assistant (nourish and settle the heart) — Schisandra Fruit (Wuweizi) 15 g, Wild Jujube Seed (Suanzaoren) 20 g, Polygala Root (Yuanzhi) 15 g, Biota Seed (Baiziren) 20 g
  • (carries the formula upward into the heart) — Platycodon Root (Jiegeng) 15 g
  • 君（滋心肝之陰、清熱安神）— 生地黃 20 g
  • 臣（助生地滋陰清熱）— 玄參 15 g、天門冬 20 g、麥門冬 20 g、丹參 15 g
  • （導藥性、益氣安神）— 當歸 20 g、人參 15 g、茯苓 15 g
  • 佐（養心寧神）— 五味子 15 g、酸棗仁 20 g、遠志 15 g、柏子仁 20 g
  • （載藥上浮、引藥入心）— 桔梗 15 g
功效
Functions
Replenish yin and nourish blood; tonify the heart and soothe the mind.
滋陰養血；補心安神。
主治
Indication
Mental derangement due to yin deficiency and interior heat.
腎陰虛無法掌控心之虛火，心虛火旺而失眠——治療重點其實在補腎水；證屬心腎陰虧血少、虛火內動（心腎不交）。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Insomnia
  • Palpitations with restlessness, scanty sleep
  • Mental fatigue
  • Nocturnal emissions
  • Amnesia
  • Feverish sensation in the heart
  • Night sweat
  • Palms and soles (原文僅寫「palms and soles」，未寫明「熱」— 對應中文欄之「手足心熱」為臨床推測，非逐字直譯，供參考)
  • Ulcer in tongue and mouth
  • Reddened tongue with little coating
  • Thready and rapid pulse
  • 失眠
  • 心煩少寐
  • 神疲
  • 遺精
  • 健忘
  • 五心煩熱
  • 夜間盜汗
  • 手足心熱 （原文僅寫「palms and soles 手足」，「熱」為對應臨床推測，非逐字直譯）
  • 口舌生瘡
  • 舌紅少苔
  • 脈細數
加減
Modification
  • Mouth/tongue ulcers → add Huanglian, Mudanpi
  • Constipation → add Zhishi, Houpo
  • Dizziness → add Chuanxiong, Baizhi
  • Restlessness → add Longyanrou, Yejiaoteng
  • Seminal emission/forgetfulness → add Jinyingzi, Qianshi
  • Dry mouth/throat → add Shihu, Lianzixin
  • Excessive worry → combine with Xiao Yao San
  • Gum swelling/pain → combine with Qing Wei San
  • 口舌生瘡：加黃連、牡丹皮
  • 大便秘結：加枳實、厚朴
  • 頭暈目眩：加川芎、白芷
  • 心神不寧：加龍眼肉、夜交藤
  • 夢遺健忘：加金櫻子、芡實
  • 口燥咽乾：加石斛、蓮子心
  • 思慮過度：合逍遙散
  • 牙齦腫痛：合清胃散
Gan Mai Da Zao Tang
甘麥大棗湯【安神降臟燥】 （原文用字如此；標準醫學名詞為「臟躁」）
Licorice, Wheat and Jujube Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Licorice (Gancao) 20 g — sweet, harmonizes the middle
  • Wheat (Xiaomai) 100 g — tonifies middle qi, nourishes heart yin
  • Jujube (Dazao) 20 g — tonifies middle qi, nourishes heart yin

Only 3 herbs — one of the shortest formulas in the whole database; commonly tested as "name the 3 herbs."

  • 甘草 20 g — 甘緩和中
  • 小麥 100 g — 補中益氣、調養心陰
  • 大棗 20 g — 補中益氣、調養心陰

全方僅 3 味藥，是全書藥味最少的方劑之一，考試常直接考「這方有哪三味藥」。

功效
Functions
Nourish the heart and calm the mind; harmonize the middle and relieve urgency, settle spirit and calm agitation; tonify middle qi and nourish heart yin, while easing the urgency of liver qi.
養心安神；和中緩急、寧神安躁；補中益氣、調養心陰，並可緩肝氣之急。
主治
Indication
Zang-Zao (visceral agitation / "hysteria"): depression, uncontrollable crying, etc. — commonly seen in women, usually from excessive worry damaging heart yin and disharmonizing liver qi; treat by nourishing the heart, calming the mind, regulating the liver, harmonizing the middle.
臟燥（標準寫法：臟躁）——多見於婦人，多因憂思過度，心陰受損、肝氣失和所致，故治宜養心安神、調肝和中。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Absent-mindedness / mental derangement
  • Sorrowful, uncontrollable crying
  • Frequent yawning
  • 精神恍惚
  • 悲傷欲哭、不能自主
  • 呵欠頻頻
加減
Modification
（原始資料未列加減內容）
（原始資料未列加減內容）

02 · Astringing 固澀劑

▍組別分類 Classification of Astringent Formulas（原文結構）

English中文
a. For consolidating the exterior and arresting sweating.
a. 止汗——固表止汗
b. For astringing the intestines and stopping diarrhea.
b. 止瀉——澀腸止瀉
c. For astringing essence and stopping seminal emissions and enuresis.
c. 止遺精遺尿——固精縮尿
d. For relieving metrorrhagia and leukorrhagia.
d. 止崩漏帶下

▍本組 8 方劑速記 Group Overview

止汗 SweatingEnglish中文
Sweating can come from yin deficiency (night sweat), qi deficiency (spontaneous daytime sweat), or profuse sweating triggered by any movement.
防止出汗的三種成因：陰虛（夜汗）、氣虛（白天自發出汗）、一動就大出汗。
Yu Ping Feng San — strengthens Wei qi; treats continuous spontaneous sweating during the day.
玉屏風散——增強衛氣，治白天持續自發流汗。
Dang Gui Liu Huang Tang — treats night sweating.
當歸六黃湯——治夜汗。
Mu Li San — usable for excessive sweating regardless of day or night; its main function is simply to stop sweating (symptomatic).
牡蠣散——不論白天晚上，只要過度流汗都可用，主要功能是止汗（治標）。
止瀉 DiarrheaEnglish中文
For severe diarrhea, also consider tonifying Spleen qi with Shen Ling Bai Zhu San, or warming the middle burner with Li Zhong Tang.
嚴重腹瀉：也可考慮補脾氣（蔘苓白朮散）、溫中焦（理中湯）。
Si Shen Wan — severe diarrhea in the early morning, caused by Kidney yang deficiency failing to rise.
四神丸——早晨嚴重腹瀉，是因為腎陽虛造成，腎陽虛無法上升。
Zhen Ren Yang Zang Tang — acute, extremely severe diarrhea; if not stopped can cause fatal dehydration (e.g. cholera). Usually needs emergency care. The only formula that uses opium (as the chief herb).
真人養臟湯——急性非常嚴重腹瀉，不止住會造成脫水死亡，例如霍亂。通常需要送急診，唯一用鴉片(opium)的藥方（君藥）。
遺精／早洩 EmissionsEnglish中文
For controlling urination and premature emission —
控制排尿、早洩——
Jin Suo Gu Jing Wan — cannot hold urine/essence in, due to Kidney qi deficiency.
金鎖固精丸——無法止住尿，腎氣虛。
Sang Piao Xiao San — Kidney yin deficiency, water cannot control fire, so the Heart also becomes affected.
桑螵蛸散——腎陰虛，水止不住火，心會出問題。
崩漏 MetrorrhagiaEnglish中文
Gu Chong Tang — targets Chong Vessel deficiency with Kidney deficiency; presents with a lot of lower-back pain; involves the female reproductive system.
固衝湯——針對衝脈虛腎虛，會有很多下背痛症狀，女性的生殖系統。

▍相似方劑比較 Compare Similar Formulas

桂枝湯——外感風寒伴隨大流汗（有表證）
Yu Ping Feng San — profuse sweating from Wei qi deficiency

Mu Li San — stops sweating directly, symptomatic only

Zhen Ren Yang Zang Tang — chronic diarrhea, even unable to get out of bed, with Spleen yang deficiency
真人養臟湯——長期嚴重腹瀉，甚至無法起床，脾陽虛

Sang Piao Xiao San — Kidney yin deficiency cannot control fire; a Heart-Kidney problem
桑螵蛸散——腎陰虛無法制火，心腎問題
止崩漏類English中文
Gu Chong Tang — targets Chong Vessel + Kidney deficiency; many lower-back-pain symptoms; female reproductive system
固衝湯——針對衝脈虛腎虛，會有很多下背痛症狀，女性的生殖系統
Gui Pi Tang (see Group 17 · Tonifying) — targets Spleen deficiency, prone to blood stasis, no lower-back pain
歸脾湯 （見 17·補益劑）——針對脾虛，容易瘀血，不會有下背痛

▍病機 Pathogenesis（原稿 col5「others」欄）

汗證English中文
Wei qi deficiency: spontaneous sweating, day or night
衛氣虛：不分日夜自汗
Yin deficiency: night sweating
陰虛：夜汗
瀉證English中文
Spleen deficiency: (see symptoms in formula cards)
脾虛：（症狀詳見各方劑卡片）
Kidney deficiency: (see symptoms in formula cards)
腎虛：（症狀詳見各方劑卡片）
遺精遺尿English中文
Kidney qi deficiency: (see symptoms in formula cards)
腎氣虛：（症狀詳見各方劑卡片）
Heart-Kidney disharmony: water cannot control fire, so Heart-related symptoms appear
心腎不交：水無法制火，所以會有心的問題
崩漏English中文
Spleen and Kidney: (see symptoms in formula cards)
脾腎：（症狀詳見各方劑卡片）
Chong and Ren vessels: (see symptoms in formula cards)
衝脈任脈：（症狀詳見各方劑卡片）
▍EXAM FOCUS — 本組出題重點
  • 玉屏風散不含甘草、人蔘——一般補氣方常有這兩味藥，本方沒有，是常見的陷阱考點；黃耆＋白朮是一組很好的補脾氣對藥。
  • 止汗四方鑑別：桂枝湯（有表證）／玉屏風散（衛氣虛，日汗）／當歸六黃湯（腎陰虛，夜汗）／牡蠣散（不分日夜，治標止汗）。
  • 真人養臟湯是全書唯二使用鴉片（罌粟殼）的方劑之一（另一方用於治療嚴重咳嗽，之後章節會遇到，屆時可互相對照）；止咳藥過量可能危及生命，是常見的安全性考點。
  • 止瀉二方鑑別：四神丸（清晨腹瀉，腎陽＋脾陽兩虛） vs 真人養臟湯（長期嚴重腹瀉，脾陽虛，甚至需要送急診）。
  • 止遺精二方鑑別：金鎖固精丸（單純腎氣虛，無陰陽問題） vs 桑螵蛸散（心腎不交，腎陰虛無法制火）。
  • 崩漏二方鑑別（跨組）：固衝湯（衝脈虛腎虛，有下背痛，女性生殖系統） vs 歸脾湯（脾虛，易瘀血，無下背痛）。
  • 四神丸「加減」欄原稿中文寫「升麻、丹蔘」，但英文寫 "Cimicifuga (Shengma) and Dangshen"（黨蔘）——你確認過以英文為準，故此處已統一為黨蔘 Dangshen（非丹蔘）。
Yu Ping Feng San
玉屏風散【止汗強衛氣】
Jade Windscreen Powder
組成
Ingredients
  • Chief — Astragalus Root (Huangqi) 18 g — tonifies Wei qi (used for those with severe qi deficiency)
  • Deputy — White Atractylodes (Baizhu) 6 g
  • Assistant — Ledebouriella Root (Fangfeng) 6 g — for "moving" (disperses)
⚠ ＊＊ 原稿重點
No Licorice (Gancao) and no Ginseng (Renshen) in this formula.
  • 君 — 黃耆 18 g — 補衛氣（氣虛嚴重的人用）
  • 臣 — 白朮 6 g
  • 佐 — 防風 6 g — 走竄（幫助行散）
⚠ ＊＊ 原稿重點
沒有甘草、人蔘
老師補充 ＆＆
黃耆＋白朮是很好的補脾氣對藥
功效
Functions
Replenish Wei qi and arrest spontaneous sweating. Main function: strengthen resistance, prevent colds; also prevents asthma from worsening.
補衛氣止汗（一般的補氣是補脾，用四君子湯，本方特別補的是衛氣）。主要功能：增強抵抗力，預防感冒；預防氣喘變嚴重。
主治
Indication
Wei qi deficiency. (Acupuncture note: ST36 + all Back-Shu points can be used to boost immunity.)
衛氣虛。針灸可用：增強免疫力，用 ST36＋全部的背俞穴。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Exterior deficiency with spontaneous sweating
  • Person prone to recurrent colds
  • Pale/whitish complexion, aversion to wind
  • Pale tongue with white coating
  • Floating, weak pulse
  • 表虛自汗
  • 虛人反覆感冒
  • 面色㿠白惡風
  • 舌淡苔白
  • 脈浮虛
加減
Modification
  • Severe spontaneous sweating → add Muli, Fuxiaomai
  • If exterior wind-cold present → combine with Guizhi Tang
  • Seasonal nasal allergy → add Xinyihua, Cangerzi
  • 嚴重的自汗：加牡蠣、浮小麥
  • 如有外感風寒：合桂枝湯
  • 季節性鼻子過敏：加辛夷花、蒼耳子
Dang Gui Liu Huang Tang
當歸六黃湯【陰虛夜汗】
Tangkuei and Six-Yellow Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Nourish yin and blood — Danggui (nourishes/moves blood), Shu Di Huang (nourishes blood and essence), Sheng Di Huang (nourishes yin)
  • Strengthen Wei qi — Huangqi
  • Clear heat — Huangqin (clears upper-burner heat, e.g. lung heat cough), Huanglian (clears middle-burner heat), Huangbai (clears lower-burner heat)
  • 滋陰養血 — 當歸（補血走血）、熟地黃（補血、補精）、生地黃（滋陰）
  • 強衛氣 — 黃耆
  • 清熱 — 黃芩（清上焦熱，如肺熱咳嗽）、黃連（清中焦熱）、黃柏（清下焦熱）
功效
Functions
Tonify yin and arrest night sweating. (Sweating occurring only at night is more likely related to hormonal influence.)
滋陰止夜汗。只有晚上的流汗比較是因為賀爾蒙影響。
主治
Indication
Night sweating due to yin deficiency and empty heat.
因陰虛內熱所致的夜間盜汗。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Night sweating
  • Fever with flushed face
  • Dry mouth and lips
  • Restlessness with reddish urine
  • Dry, hard stool
  • Red tongue, rapid pulse
  • Also treats spontaneous (daytime) sweating
  • 盜汗
  • 發熱面赤
  • 口乾唇燥
  • 心煩尿赤
  • 大便乾結
  • 舌紅脈數
  • 亦治自汗
加減
Modification

Mu Li San
牡蠣散【全天持續出汗】
Oyster Shell Powder
組成
Ingredients
  • Oyster Shell (Muli) 30 g — stops sweating
  • Astragalus Root (Huangqi) 30 g — tonifies Wei qi
  • Ephedra Root (Mahuanggen) 30 g — (Ephedra herb promotes sweating for exterior wind-cold; the ROOT has the opposite effect and stops sweating)
  • Light Wheat (Fuxiaomai) 30 g
  • 牡蠣 30 g — 止汗
  • 黃耆 30 g — 補衛氣
  • 麻黃根 30 g — 麻黃（草本部分）助出汗，治外感風寒；麻黃根（根部）有相反功效，可止汗
  • 浮小麥 30 g
功效
Functions
Replenish qi and arrest spontaneous sweating; preserve yin and arrest night sweating. (Sweating occurs day or night; excessive sweating damages yin, which in turn affects the Heart and Shen.)
補氣止自汗；養陰止夜汗。不論白天或晚上都在流汗，並且過度流汗傷到陰，因此會影響心與神。
主治
Indication
Spontaneous sweating and night sweating that damage the Heart.
汗流過多會傷血，進而損到心。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Constant sweating, especially while asleep at night
  • Palpitations
  • Susceptibility to fright
  • Shortness of breath
  • Lassitude
  • Reddish tongue
  • Thready and feeble pulse
  • 整日出汗，睡覺時尤其嚴重
  • 心悸
  • 易受驚嚇
  • 氣短
  • 倦怠乏力
  • 舌偏紅
  • 脈細弱
加減
Modification
  • Yang deficiency with severe spontaneous sweating → add Baizhu, Fuzi (Chinese notes also add Ganjiang, Renshen — "to warm the person up")
  • Yin deficiency with severe night sweating → add Baishao, Gandihuang (fresh Rehmannia), Suanzaoren
  • Severe blood deficiency → add Shudi, Ejiao
  • Severe qi deficiency → add Dangshen, Baizhu, Huangqi
  • 陽虛，自汗嚴重：加白朮、附子（原文中文另提乾薑、人蔘，讓他暖起來）
  • 陰虛，夜汗嚴重：加白芍、生地、酸棗仁
  • 嚴重血虛：加熟地、阿膠
  • 嚴重氣虛：加黨蔘、白朮、黃耆
Zhen Ren Yang Zang Tang ⚠ 含鴉片成分 Opium
真人養臟湯【腹瀉霍亂／罌粟】
True Man's Decoction to Nourish the Organs
組成
Ingredients
  • Stop diarrhea — Poppy Capsule / Papaver somniferum (Ying Su Ke): stops pain and cough, causes constipation — only used in severe cases; Terminalia Chebula (Ke Zi)
  • Tonify Spleen — Ren Shen; Bai Zhu
  • Warm Spleen and Kidney — Rou Dou Kou
  • Harmonize — Mu Xiang; Bai Shao; Gan Cao
⚠ ＊＊ 原稿重點
Overdosing on antitussive (cough-suppressant) herbs can be life-threatening.
  • 止瀉 — 罌粟殼 Ying Su Ke（Papaver somniferum，即鴉片來源）：止痛、止咳，會造成便祕，只有嚴重症狀時才可用此藥；訶子 Ke Zi
  • 補脾 — 人蔘；白朮
  • 溫脾腎 — 肉豆蔻
  • 調和 — 木香；白芍；甘草
⚠ ＊＊ 原稿重點
止咳的藥過量都有可能危及生命。
功效
Functions
Warm the Spleen and Kidney; astringe the intestines and stop diarrhea.
溫脾腎；澀腸止瀉。
主治
Indication
Chronic, severe diarrhea (e.g. cholera) — even unable to get out of bed, requiring diapers. Acute, extremely severe diarrhea that if not stopped can cause fatal dehydration (e.g. cholera) — usually requires emergency care. One of only two formulas in the entire formulary that use opium (the other treats severe cough).
長期嚴重的腹瀉，例如霍亂，甚至無法起床上廁所，需要穿尿布。急性非常嚴重腹瀉，不止住會造成脫水死亡，例如霍亂，通常需要送急診——全書唯二使用鴉片的藥方之一（另一方治嚴重咳嗽）。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Chronic / protracted diarrhea
  • Fecal incontinence
  • Abdominal pain relieved by warmth
  • Cold limbs
  • Fatigue
  • Pale tongue with whitish coating
  • Deep, slow pulse
  • 長期腹瀉／持續腹瀉
  • 大便失禁
  • 腹痛得溫則減
  • 四肢冰冷
  • 疲勞
  • 舌淡苔白
  • 脈沉遲
加減
Modification
（原始資料未列加減內容）
（原始資料未列加減內容）
Si Shen Wan
四神丸【清晨腹瀉腎脾陽虛】
Four-Miracle Pill
組成
Ingredients
  • Chief — Psoralea Fruit (Buguzhi) 120 g — tonifies kidney
  • Deputy — Nutmeg (Roudoukou) 60 g (stops diarrhea); Evodia Fruit (Wuzhuyu) 30 g (warms); Schisandra Fruit (Wuweizi) 60 g (astringent)
  • Assistant — Fresh Ginger (Shengjiang) 120 g; Jujube (Dazao) 100 pieces
  • 君 — 補骨脂 120 g — 補腎
  • 臣 — 肉豆蔻 60 g（止瀉）；吳茱萸 30 g（溫）；五味子 60 g（收澀）
  • 佐 — 生薑 120 g；大棗 100 枚
功效
Functions
Warm the Spleen and Kidney; astringe the intestines and stop diarrhea.
溫脾腎；澀腸止瀉。
主治
Indication
Diarrhea before dawn ("five-more diarrhea" / cockcrow diarrhea).
清晨腹瀉（五更瀉／雞鳴瀉）。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Diarrhea in the early morning, or protracted diarrhea
  • Abdominal pain relieved by warmth
  • Soreness about the waist, cold limbs
  • Fatigue and lassitude
  • Pale, enlarged tongue with thin whitish coating
  • Deep, slow, feeble pulse
  • 早晨腹瀉，或持續性腹瀉
  • 腹痛得溫則減
  • 腰部痠軟、四肢冰冷
  • 疲勞倦怠
  • 舌淡胖，苔薄白
  • 脈沉遲無力
加減
Modification
  • Rectal prolapse → add Astragalus Root (Huangqi), Cimicifuga (Shengma), Pilose Asiabell Root (Dangshen)
  • Severe lower-back soreness and cold limbs → add Prepared Aconite Root (Fuzi), Cinnamon Bark (Rougui)
  • 脫垂（如：直腸脫垂）：加黃耆、升麻、黨蔘
  • 腎虛下背痛嚴重：加附子、肉桂
Jin Suo Gu Jing Wan
金鎖固精丸【腎氣虛遺精】
Metal Lock Pill to Stabilize the Essence
組成
Ingredients
  • Chief — Flattened Milkvetch Seed (Shayuanjili) 60 g
  • Deputy — Euryale Seed (Qianshi) 60 g; Lotus Seed (Lianzi) 60 g
  • Assistant — Lotus Stamen (Lianzixu) 60 g; Dragon's Bone (Longgu) 30 g; Oyster Shell (Muli) 30 g
  • 君 — 沙苑子 60 g
  • 臣 — 芡實 60 g；蓮子 60 g
  • 佐 — 蓮鬚 60 g；龍骨 30 g；牡蠣 30 g
功效
Functions
Reinforce the Kidney and astringe the essence. (No yin/yang issue here — purely a Kidney qi deficiency problem; all herbs work together to stop the "leakage.")
補腎固精。沒有陰陽的問題，只有腎氣虛的問題；全部的藥一起作用在止漏。
主治
Indication
Nocturnal and spontaneous seminal emissions due to Kidney qi deficiency.
因腎氣虛所致的夜間或自發性遺精。

  • 耳鳴
  • 倦怠
  • 舌淡苔白
  • 脈細弱
加減

  • 下背痛：加杜仲、續斷、桑寄生
  • 陽痿：加淫羊藿

Gu Chong Tang
固衝湯【衝脈虛腎虛／下背痛／女生殖】
Decoction to Consolidate the Chong Meridian
組成
Ingredients
  • Chief — Astragalus Root (Huangqi) 18 g; White Atractylodes (Baizhu) 30 g
  • Deputy — Dragon's Bone (Longgu) 24 g; Oyster Shell (Muli) 24 g; Dogwood Fruit (Shanzhuyu) 24 g; Cuttlefish Bone (Haipiaoxiao) 12 g; Gallnut (Wubeizi) 15 g
  • Assistant — White Peony Root (Baishao) 12 g; Rubia Root (Qiancao) 9 g
  • 君 — 黃耆 18 g；白朮 30 g
  • 臣 — 龍骨 24 g；牡蠣 24 g；山茱萸 24 g；海螵蛸 12 g；五倍子 15 g
  • 佐 — 白芍 12 g；茜草 9 g
功效
Functions
Tonify qi and strengthen the Spleen; consolidate the Chong Meridian and control blood. (Targets Chong Vessel deficiency, the female reproductive system.)
補氣健脾；固衝脈、攝血。針對衝脈虛，女性的生殖系統。
主治
Indication
Debility of the Chong Vessel due to Spleen-qi deficiency.
脾氣虛導致衝脈失固。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Metrorrhagia, or profuse but thin, light-colored menstruation
  • Palpitations
  • Shortness of breath
  • Lower back pain
  • Pale tongue
  • Weak pulse
  • 崩漏，或經量多而質稀色淡
  • 心悸
  • 氣短
  • 下背痛
  • 舌淡
  • 脈弱
加減
Modification
  • Cold sensation (listlessness, pale complexion, cold limbs, weak pulse) → add Ginseng (Renshen), Prepared Aconite Root (Fuzi)
  • 感覺身冷（神疲、面色蒼白、四肢冰冷、脈弱）：加人蔘、附子

03 · Regulating Qi 理氣劑

▍理氣常用藥 Herbs for Regulating Qi（子類：Qi Circulation 行氣）

English中文
Tangerine Peel (Chenpi) — Spleen; promotes digestion, increases appetite. Points: SP9, CV6.
陳皮——入脾，促進消化功能、增加食慾。穴位：SP9、CV6。
Immature Bitter Orange (Zhishi) — Spleen; breaks up qi, for something "stuck"; promotes movement. Point: ST25.
枳實——入脾，破氣，用於有東西卡住；促進蠕動。穴位：ST25。
Costus Root (Muxiang) — Spleen; stops pain, for bloating/gastrointestinal pain. Points: CV12 + ST36.
木香——入脾，止痛，用於腸胃脹痛。穴位：CV12＋ST36。
Cyperus Tuber (Xiangfu) — Spleen; the #1 herb for "moving qi," also enters the Liver channel (the #1 herb for "moving blood" is Chuanxiong); for rib-side pain. Points: LR3, LR13; the "Four Gates" combo: bilateral LI4 + bilateral LR3.
香附——入脾，走氣第一用藥，也走肝經（走血的第一用藥是川芎），治肋脅痛。穴位：LR3、LR13；配穴「開四關」：雙合谷＋雙太衝。
Macrostem Onion (Xiebai) — Heart; for chest pain (classic herb-pair Gualou-Xiebai); can also use PC6 / CV17 (Danzhong).
薤白——入心，治胸痛，對藥「栝樓薤白」；也可用 PC6、CV17（膻中）。
📝 課堂筆記（原文僅中文）
心經比較偏管心氣，心包絡經比較偏管心血。

▍行氣方劑速記 Formulas for Qi Circulation

English中文
Yue Ju Wan — the most general-purpose formula, similar in role to Si Junzi Tang.
越鞠丸——最 general 的用藥，類似四君子湯。
Ban Xia Hou Po Tang — treats Plum-Pit Qi (a sensation of something stuck in the throat with nothing actually there); qi stagnation at the throat with phlegm.
半夏厚朴湯——治梅核氣（喉嚨卡著東西但實際沒東西），咽喉氣滯夾痰。
Zhi Shi Xie Bai Gui Zhi Tang — treats chest pain, chest tightness, Bi-syndrome; qi stagnation at the chest.
枳實薤白桂枝湯——治胸痛、胸緊、痹症，氣滯於胸。
Tian Tai Wu Yao San — treats hernia, genital pain; qi stagnation at the intestine.
天台烏藥散——治疝氣、陰部疼痛，氣滯於腸。

▍氣病病機 Pathogenesis of Abnormal Qi Circulation

氣滯 Qi StagnationEnglish中文
→ Liver: rib-side pain, irregular menstruation, headache
→肝：肋脅痛，月經不調，頭痛
→ Spleen: bloating
→脾：脹氣
→ Heart: chest pain, chest oppression
→心：胸痛、胸悶
氣逆 Qi ReversalEnglish中文
→ Lung: cough, wheeze, shortness of breath
→肺：咳嗽、喘、氣短
→ Stomach: vomiting, bloating
→胃：嘔吐、脹氣

▍相似方劑比較（跨組，哮喘／嘔吐） Compare Similar Formulas

肺腎虛哮喘鑑別English中文
Su Zi Jiang Qi Tang — treats Lung + Kidney together (if Lung + Spleen, use Liu Junzi Tang instead); the upper Lung is blocked by phlegm causing excess, while the Kidney is also too deficient to "grasp" Lung qi.
蘇子降氣湯——同時治肺＋腎（如果是肺＋脾則要用六君子湯）；是因肺的上半部卡痰導致實證，腎也虛，抓不住肺氣。
Xiao Qing Long Tang (see Group 10 · Exterior Wind-Cold) — Taiyang cold; cold causing poor water metabolism; treats the Lung only.
小青龍湯 （見 10・辛溫解表）——太陽寒，有寒造成水液代謝不良，只治肺。
Shen Qi Wan (see Group 17 · Tonifying) — Kidney yang deficiency, but only tonifies the Kidney — does not stop cough; suitable for long-term treatment of Kidney yang deficiency.
腎氣丸 （見 17・補益劑）——腎陽虛，但只補腎，不止咳，適合長期治腎陽虛。
寒熱咳喘鑑別English中文
Ding Chuan Tang — cough/asthma from exterior wind-cold invasion, with heat, yellow phlegm
定喘湯——因外感風寒而引起的咳跟哮喘，黃痰有熱
Xiao Qing Long Tang — cough/asthma from exterior wind-cold invasion, with cold, white phlegm
小青龍湯——因外感風寒而引起的咳跟哮喘，白痰有寒
Su Zi Jiang Qi Tang — mainly shortness of breath + phlegm, from Kidney deficiency causing Lung phlegm blockage; common in the elderly
蘇子降氣湯——主要氣短＋痰，腎虛引起的肺痰卡住，常見老人
Ma Xing Shi Gan Tang (see Group 14 · Clearing Heat) — mainly shortness of breath + phlegm, but purely a Lung infection
麻杏石甘湯 （見 14・清熱劑）——主要是氣短＋痰，只有肺部感染
哮喘方劑總覽English中文
Su Zi Jiang Qi Tang / Ding Chuan Tang / Ma Huang Tang / Ma Xing Shi Gan Tang / Xiao Qing Long Tang — five key asthma formulas across the whole formulary; see individual chapters for each pattern.
蘇子降氣湯／定喘湯／麻黃湯／麻杏石甘湯／小青龍湯——全書五個治哮喘的關鍵方劑，各自證型詳見所在章節。
嘔吐方劑鑑別English中文
Xuan Fu Dai Zhe Tang — Stomach deficiency with hiccup, turbid phlegm rebelling upward
旋覆代赭湯——胃虛打嗝痰濁上逆
Ju Pi Zhu Ru Tang — yin deficiency with Stomach heat causing dry heaving
橘皮竹茹湯——陰虛胃熱而乾嘔
Wu Zhu Yu Tang (see Group 15 · Warming Interior) — Jueyin vertex headache + vomiting
吳茱萸湯 （見 15・溫裡劑）——厥陰頭頂痛＋嘔吐
Zuo Jin Wan (see Group 14 · Clearing Heat) — Liver fire invading the Stomach, acid reflux
左金丸 （見 14・清熱劑）——肝火犯胃，胃酸
Ban Xia Xie Xin Tang (see Group 13 · Harmonizing) — vomiting and diarrhea occurring together
半夏瀉心湯 （見 13・和解劑）——同時嘔吐腹瀉
▍EXAM FOCUS — 本組出題重點
  • 越鞠丸治「六鬱」（氣、血、火、濕、食、痰）但方中沒有直接去痰的藥——因為「痰是濕氣的一種」，去濕（蒼朮）即間接治痰，是常見的推理題。
  • 枳實薤白桂枝湯的「胸痹 Chest Bi」跟心臟疾病無關，是經絡層次的症狀（跟理中丸治的陽虛胸痛要區分）；治療重點在「胸悶」而非「胸痛」。
  • 降氣五方哮喘鑑別是必考重點：蘇子降氣湯（肺腎兩虛，老人）／定喘湯（外感風寒＋黃痰熱咳）／小青龍湯（外感風寒＋白痰寒咳）／麻杏石甘湯（單純肺部感染）／麻黃湯（見辛溫解表章）。
  • 旋覆代赭湯是全書中唯一「向下」的花類藥（旋覆花）——一般花皆主升浮向上，是常考的特例。
  • 嘔吐方劑鑑別（跨組）：胃虛痰濁（旋覆代赭湯）／陰虛胃熱（橘皮竹茹湯）／厥陰頭痛（吳茱萸湯，另章）／肝火犯胃（左金丸，另章）／嘔吐兼腹瀉（半夏瀉心湯，另章）。
Yue Ju Wan
越鞠丸【Basic・開四關】
Escape Restraint Pill
組成
Ingredients
  • [Qi] Cyperus Tuber (Xiangfu) 15 g — regulates qi; enters the Liver channel to move qi throughout the whole body
  • [Blood] Chuanxiong Rhizome (Chuanxiong) 15 g — invigorates blood and moves qi, the "qi-herb within blood," able to "regulate menstruation below, open constraint in the middle"
  • [Fire] Capejasmine Fruit (Zhizi) 15 g — purges fire, clears heat; enters the Triple Burner channel
  • [Damp] Atractylodes Rhizome (Cangzhu) 15 g — removes dampness
  • [Food] Medicated Leaven (Shenqu) 15 g
  • 【氣】香附 15 g——理氣，走肝經而行全身之氣
  • 【血】川芎 15 g——活血行氣，為「血中氣藥」，能「下調經水，中開鬱結」
  • 【火】梔子 15 g——去火、清熱，走三焦經
  • 【濕】蒼朮 15 g——去濕氣
  • 【食】神麯 15 g
老師補充 ＆＆
蒼朮去濕氣＆行氣，但無健脾之功（穴位 ST40）。白朮健脾化痰濕（穴位 SP9）。神曲治療沒胃口。（這邊沒有去痰的藥，因為痰是濕氣的一種）
功效
Functions
Promote qi circulation. A very basic, general formula — similar in role to Si Junzi Tang, functioning like "opening the Four Gates." (Most basic treatment approach: cupping + needling the Back-Shu points, then opening the Four Gates on the front + Yintang.)
促進氣的循環。很 basic、general 的藥，類似四君子湯，作用就像開四關。最 basic 的治療＝拔罐＋下針背後俞穴，接著正面開四關＋印堂。
主治
Indication
Stagnation of qi, blood, fire, dampness, food, and phlegm (the "Six Stagnations").
氣、血、火、濕、食、痰六種鬱滯（六鬱）。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Chest distress and fullness
  • Epigastric and abdominal distension and pain
  • Acid regurgitation, vomiting, and indigestion
  • 胸悶脹滿
  • 胃脘及腹部脹痛
  • 吞酸、嘔吐、消化不良
加減
Modification
  • Severe qi stagnation → add Muxiang, Chenpi (point CV12)
  • Severe blood stagnation → add Taoren, Honghua, Danshen, Danggui
  • Severe fire → add Huanglian, Lianqiao
  • Severe dampness → add Fuling, Zexie, Cheqianzi, Yiyiren
  • Food retention → add Shanzha, Maiya, Jineijin
  • Excess phlegm → add Gualou, Chenpi, Banxia
  • 氣滯：加木香、陳皮（CV12）
  • 走氣（血瘀）：加桃仁、紅花、丹蔘、當歸
  • 實火旺：加黃連、連翹
  • 濕氣重：加茯苓、澤瀉、車前子、薏苡仁
  • 食物滯留：加山楂、麥芽、雞內金
  • 痰多：加栝樓、陳皮、半夏
Ban Xia Hou Po Tang
半夏厚朴湯【梅核氣】
Pinellia and Magnolia Bark Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Pinellia Tuber (Banxia) 12 g
  • Magnolia Bark (Houpo) 9 g
  • Poria (Fuling) 12 g
  • Fresh Ginger (Shengjiang) 9 g — stops vomiting
  • Perilla Leaf (Zisuye) 6 g — envoy herb; relaxes the throat, can stop vomiting
  • 半夏 12 g
  • 厚朴 9 g
  • 茯苓 12 g
  • 生薑 9 g——止嘔
  • 紫蘇葉 6 g——使藥，放鬆喉嚨，可止嘔
功效
Functions
Promote qi and remove nodules; conduct qi downward and resolve phlegm.
行氣散結；降氣化痰。
主治
Indication
Globus hystericus (Plum-Pit Qi).
梅核氣。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Sensation of an object in the throat that can neither be coughed up nor swallowed down
  • Chest distress
  • Coughing or vomiting
  • Greasy, whitish tongue coating
  • Wiry, smooth pulse
  • 咽喉有異物感，咯之不出、嚥之不下
  • 胸悶
  • 咳嗽或嘔吐
  • 舌苔白膩
  • 脈弦滑
加減
Modification
（原始資料未列加減內容）
（原始資料未列加減內容）
Zhi Shi Xie Bai Gui Zhi Tang
枳實薤白桂枝湯【Bi・胸痛】
Unripe Bitter Orange, Garlic, and Cinnamon Twig Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Release chest — Trichosanthes Fruit (Gualou) 12 g (herb-pair Gualou-Xiebai, point PC6); Macrostem Onion (Xiebai) 9 g (same pair, point PC6)
  • Descending qi — Immature Bitter Orange (Zhishi) 9 g (unblocks); Magnolia Bark (Houpo) 9 g (sends qi downward)
  • Dispersing — Cinnamon Twig (Guizhi) 9 g — enters Heart (chest pain) and Lung (sweating) channels, promotes circulation
  • 開胸 — 栝樓 12 g（對藥「栝樓薤白」，PC6）；薤白 9 g（同對藥，PC6）
  • 降氣 — 枳實 9 g（通利）；厚朴 9 g（使氣下降）
  • 發散 — 桂枝 9 g——走心（胸痛）、肺（發汗），促進循環
功效
Functions
Activate yang and resolve stagnation in the chest (point PC6 can also be needled); treats Bi-syndrome (a channel-level symptom, unrelated to internal organ disease — acupuncture is the main treatment for this); mainly treats chest oppression, not chest pain.
通陽散結（可下針 PC6）；治痹症（經絡症狀，跟內臟無關，針灸主要治療這個）；主要是治療胸悶，非胸痛。
主治
Indication
Chest stifling (Xiong Bi) — unrelated to heart disease — due to obstruction of yang qi in the chest caused by turbid phlegm. (Compare to Li Zhong Wan, which treats yang-deficiency chest pain.)
胸痹（跟心臟疾病無關），因濁痰阻遏胸中陽氣所致。（對比理中丸：是治療陽虛症狀）
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Stifling sensation over the chest
  • Dyspnea
  • Cough with sputum
  • Shortness of breath
  • Greasy, whitish tongue coating
  • Deep and wiry, or tense pulse
  • 胸中痞悶
  • 呼吸困難
  • 咳嗽有痰
  • 氣短
  • 舌苔白膩
  • 脈沉弦或緊
加減
Modification
  • Severe angina pectoris → add Danshen, Chishao, Honghua, Chuanxiong
  • Severe cold → add Dried Ginger
  • Blockage by turbid phlegm → add Banxia, Fresh Ginger
  • 心絞痛：加丹蔘、赤芍、紅花、川芎
  • 寒重：加乾薑
  • 濁痰阻滯：加半夏、生薑
Tian Tai Wu Yao San
天臺烏藥散【疝氣、陰縮痛】
Top-Quality Lindera Powder
組成
Ingredients
  • Move Liver qi, disperse cold — Lindera Root (Wuyao) 12 g; Fennel Fruit (Xiaohuixiang) 6 g
  • Promote qi circulation — Green Tangerine Peel (Qingpi) 6 g; Costus Root (Muxiang) 6 g; Galanga (Gaoliangjiang) 9 g
  • Release pain, act on Liver — Sichuan Chinaberry (Chuanlianzi) 12 g; Areca Seed (Binlang) 9 g
  • 行肝氣，散寒 — 烏藥 12 g；小茴香 6 g
  • 行氣 — 青皮 6 g；木香 6 g；高良薑 9 g
  • 止痛，作用於肝 — 川楝子 12 g；檳榔 9 g
功效
Functions
Promote Liver qi circulation and dispel cold to stop pain. (These herbs enter the Liver channel; the Liver channel runs to the reproductive system — the Liver channel has two point types: the Connecting point, used for excess conditions, and the Source point, used to tonify — both are on LR.)
行肝氣、散寒止痛。走肝經的藥，肝經走生殖系統（肝經分兩種：一種是絡穴，可解決實證，用 LR；一種是原穴，可用來補虛，用 LR）。
主治
Indication
Hernia and contracting pain in the genitals.
疝氣及陰部拘急疼痛。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Lower abdominal pain radiating to the testicles
  • Swelling and pain on one side of the testicle
  • Pale tongue with whitish coating
  • Deep-slow or wiry pulse
  • 少腹疼痛牽引睪丸
  • 單側睪丸腫痛
  • 舌淡苔白
  • 脈沉遲或弦
加減
Modification
  • Especially severe cold → add Evodia Fruit (Wuzhuyu), Cinnamon Bark (Rougui)
  • Dysmenorrhea → add Chuanxiong Rhizome, Safflower (Honghua), Chinese Angelica Root (Danggui)
  • 特別寒：加吳茱萸、肉桂
  • 經痛：加川芎、紅花、當歸
Su Zi Jiang Qi Tang
蘇子降氣湯【長期肺＋腎虛哮喘】
Perilla Fruit Decoction for Directing Qi Downward
組成
Ingredients
  • For excess in the upper Jiao — Perilla Seed (Suzi) 9 g; Pinellia Tuber (Banxia) 9 g; Peucedanum Root (Qianhu) 6 g; Magnolia Bark (Houpo) 6 g
  • For deficiency in the lower Jiao — Cinnamon Bark (Rougui) 3 g; Chinese Angelica Root (Danggui) 6 g; Baked Licorice (Zhigancao) 6 g
  • 治上焦實證 — 蘇子 9 g；半夏 9 g；前胡 6 g；厚朴 6 g
  • 治下焦虛證 — 肉桂 3 g；當歸 6 g；炙甘草 6 g
老師補充 ＆＆
煎藥另加生薑二片、大棗一枚、紫蘇葉五片。
功效
Functions
Descend qi, resolve phlegm, and soothe asthma. Treats long-term/chronic asthma due to Kidney qi deficiency. (Upper body excess, lower body deficiency: the Kidney qi cannot "hold" the Lung qi down, so breathing is shallow and cannot go deep; the upper Lung has phlegm that locks up Lung function.)
降氣化痰、平喘。治療長期性哮喘，因為腎氣虛。上身實、下身虛：因腎氣無法拉住肺氣讓其往下降，無法深呼吸、呼吸淺；上半部的肺因為有痰，造成肺的機能鎖住。
主治
Indication
Cough with sputum and asthma due to phlegm blocking the Lung and Kidney deficiency.
因痰阻肺及腎虛所致的咳嗽有痰、氣喘。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Cough/asthma with white sputum
  • Fullness and choking sensation in the chest
  • Lower back ache
  • Or edema of the limbs
  • White, slippery or greasy tongue coating
  • Wiry, smooth pulse
  • 咳喘痰白
  • 胸中滿悶、有窒息感
  • 腰痠
  • 或四肢水腫
  • 舌苔白滑或白膩
  • 脈弦滑
加減
Modification
  • Severe edema → add Poria (Fuling), White Atractylodes (Baizhu), Alismatis Rhizome (Zexie)
  • Exterior symptoms with chills and fever → add Ephedra (Mahuang), Apricot Seed (Xingren)
  • 水腫嚴重：加茯苓、白朮、澤瀉
  • 外感風寒往來寒熱：加麻黃、杏仁
Ding Chuan Tang
定喘湯【氣喘黃痰】
Arrest Wheezing Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Disperse/astringe Lung qi — Ephedra (Mahuang) 9 g; Ginkgo Seed (Baiguo) 9 g
  • Resolve phlegm, descend qi — Perilla Seed (Suzi) 6 g; Apricot Seed (Xingren) 9 g; Pinellia Tuber (Banxia) 9 g; Tussilago Flower (Kuandonghua)
  • Purge Lung heat — Mulberry Bark (Sangbaipi) 9 g; Scutellaria Root (Huangqin) 6 g
  • Licorice (Gancao) 3 g
  • 宣降肺氣（散＋澀並用） — 麻黃 9 g；白果 9 g
  • 化痰降氣 — 蘇子 6 g；杏仁 9 g；半夏 9 g；款冬花
  • 清瀉肺熱 — 桑白皮 9 g；黃芩 6 g
  • 甘草 3 g
功效
Functions
1. Descend and disperse Lung qi. 2. Resolve heat-phlegm and soothe asthma. (Large amount of phlegm: white phlegm/white coating = cold [Xiao Qing Long Tang]; yellow phlegm/yellow coating = heat [Ding Chuan Tang].)
1. 宣降肺氣。2. 清熱化痰、平喘。有大量的痰，白痰苔白是寒（小青龍湯），黃痰苔黃是熱（定喘湯）。
主治
Indication
Asthma due to heat-phlegm (yellow phlegm, usually bacterial infection). Clinical note: many asthma patients now use inhalers, which can turn the phlegm white — no longer the traditional yellow — but the tongue coating is often still yellow. Historically a very commonly used hot-cough asthma formula, but its usage has decreased with the popularity of inhalers.
因熱痰（黃痰，通常是細菌感染）所致的氣喘。現在許多氣喘常用吸入性藥劑，使得痰變白，不再像傳統上氣喘的痰是黃的，但苔仍常是黃的；原本是很常使用的熱咳氣喘藥，但因為吸入式藥劑的普及，使得定喘湯使用率變少。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Asthma, chest fullness
  • Slight aversion to wind-cold
  • Greasy, yellowish tongue coating
  • Smooth, rapid pulse
  • 氣喘、胸悶
  • 輕微惡風寒
  • 舌苔黃膩
  • 脈滑數
加減
Modification
  • Severe heat-phlegm → add Houttuynia (Yuxingcao), Capejasmine Fruit (Zhizi), Trichosanthes Fruit (Gualou), Arisaema with Bile (Dannanxing)
  • Severe stifling sensation in the chest → add Bitter Orange (Zhiqiao), Citrus Peel (Jupi), Magnolia Bark (Houpo)
  • Severe chills → add Perilla Leaf (Zisuye)
  • 嚴重熱痰：加魚腥草、梔子、栝樓、膽南星
  • 胸部刺痛感：加枳殼、橘皮、厚朴
  • 發寒：加紫蘇葉
Xuan Fu Dai Zhe Tang
旋覆代赭湯【胃虛打嗝痰濁上逆】
Inula and Hematite Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Descend qi — Chief: Inula Flower (Xuanfuhua) 9 g — sends qi downward (all flowers normally float upward — Xuanfuhua is the only one that sends qi down); Hematite (Daizheshi) 3 g; Pinellia Tuber (Banxia) 9 g (resolves phlegm); Fresh Ginger (Shengjiang) 12 g (stops vomiting)
  • Tonify Stomach qi — Ginseng (Renshen) 6 g; Baked Licorice (Zhigancao) 6 g; Jujube (Dazao) 4 pieces
  • 降氣 — 【君藥】旋覆花 9 g——讓氣下降（所有花都是向上，只有旋覆花向下）；代赭石 3 g；半夏 9 g（去痰）；生薑 12 g（止嘔）
  • 補胃氣 — 人參 6 g；炙甘草 6 g；大棗 4 枚
老師補充 ＆＆
旋覆花消痰降逆，代赭石重鎮降逆，半夏、生薑化痰止嘔，人參、大棗、甘草益氣和胃。
功效
Functions
For Stomach qi weakness with turbid phlegm obstructing internally, epigastric fullness and hardness, and belching that does not resolve.
胃氣虛弱，痰濁內阻，心下痞硬，噫氣不除者。
主治
Indication
Frequent belching and regurgitation due to Stomach qi deficiency.
因胃氣虛所致的頻繁噫氣、反胃。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Stomach deficiency with turbid phlegm rebelling upward
  • Frequent belching
  • Nausea/vomiting with epigastric fullness
  • White, slippery tongue coating
  • 胃虛痰濁上逆
  • 噫氣頻作
  • 嘔惡脘痞
  • 舌苔白滑
加減
Modification
  • Severe Stomach cold → add Dried Ginger, Clove
  • Excess phlegm → add Chenpi, Fuling
  • Stomach yin deficiency → add Maidong, Shihu
  • Food retention → add Shanzha, Neijin
  • Signs of heat, slightly yellow coating → add Huanglian, Huangqin
  • Epigastric fullness → add Zhishi, Jiegeng
  • 胃寒甚：加乾薑、丁香
  • 痰多：加陳皮、茯苓
  • 胃陰虛：加麥冬、石斛
  • 食滯：加山查、內金
  • 有熱象苔微黃：加黃連、黃芩
  • 心下痞滿：加枳實、桔梗
Ju Pi Zhu Ru Tang
橘皮竹茹湯【陰虛胃熱而乾嘔】
Tangerine Peel and Bamboo Shavings Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Citrus Peel (Jupi) 12 g
  • Nourish yin and clear heat — Bamboo Shavings (Zhuru) 12 g (bamboo-type herbs nourish yin); Fresh Ginger (Shengjiang) 9 g
  • Ginseng (Renshen) 3 g; Jujube (Dazao) 5 pieces; Licorice (Gancao) 6 g
  • 橘皮 12 g
  • 滋陰清熱 — 竹茹 12 g（竹類藥材能滋陰）；生薑 9 g
  • 人參 3 g；大棗 5 枚；甘草 6 g
功效
Functions
Because yin deficiency causes Stomach heat, which causes dry heaving. Regulates qi and descends rebellion; benefits the Stomach and clears heat. For those weak from chronic illness, or Stomach deficiency with heat after vomiting/purging.
因陰虛造成胃熱，造成乾嘔。理氣降逆，益胃清熱。久病體弱，或吐下後胃虛有熱。
主治
Indication
Frequent vomiting due to Stomach heat.
因胃熱所致的頻繁嘔吐。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Qi rebellion failing to descend
  • Hiccup or vomiting
  • Tender, reddish tongue
  • Weak, rapid pulse
  • 氣逆不降
  • 呃逆或嘔吐
  • 舌嫩紅
  • 脈虛數
加減
Modification
（原始資料未列加減內容）
（原始資料未列加減內容）
Ding Xiang Shi Di Tang
丁香柿蒂湯【胃寒而打嗝】
Clove and Persimmon Calyx Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Clove (Dingxiang) 6 g
  • Persimmon Calyx (Shidi) 6 g
  • Ginseng (Renshen) 3 g
  • Fresh Ginger (Shengjiang) 6 g
  • 丁香 6 g
  • 柿蒂 6 g
  • 人參 3 g
  • 生薑 6 g
功效
Functions
Because Stomach cold causes hiccups. The key formula for descending rebellion and stopping hiccup, for hiccup/vomiting/abdominal distension/poor appetite/pale tongue with white coating/deep-weak pulse due to chronic debility with Stomach deficiency-cold.
因胃寒造成打嗝。體虛久病、胃中虛寒所致之呃逆、嘔吐、腹脹、納呆、舌淡苔白、脈沈虛等症，為降逆止呃之要方。
主治
Indication
Frequent hiccup due to Stomach cold.
因胃寒所致的頻繁打嗝。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Hiccup
  • Epigastric/abdominal distension
  • Poor appetite
  • Pale tongue with white coating
  • Deep, slow, feeble pulse
  • 呃逆
  • 脘腹脹
  • 納呆
  • 舌淡苔白
  • 脈沈遲無力
加減
Modification
  • Unremitting vomiting → add Banxia, Fuling

  • Poor appetite → add Shanzha, Maiya
  • 嘔吐不止：加半夏、茯苓
  • 有熱象者：加黃連、竹茹
  • 寒甚挾痰：加橘紅、沈香、半夏
  • 胃痛：合安中散
  • 納呆：加山查、麥芽

04 · Regulating Blood 理血劑

▍止血方劑速記 Formulas for Bleeding（子類：Stop Bleeding）

English中文
Ke Xue Fang — Liver fire invading the Lung; coughing with blood caused by an anger-related emotional trigger.

▍相似方劑比較 Compare Similar Formulas

咳血鑑別English中文
Ke Xue Fang — Liver fire invading the Lung, causing Lung heat
咳血方——肝火犯肺，導致的肺熱
Da Bu Yin Wan (see Group 17 · Tonifying) — empty heat due to Kidney yin deficiency
大補陰丸 （見 17・補益劑）——因腎陰虛導致的虛熱
血便鑑別English中文
Huang Tu Tang — Spleen yang deficiency, with cold limbs; dark-colored blood indicates yang deficiency.
黃土湯——治脾陽虛，伴隨四肢冷，血深色是陽虛。
Gui Pi Tang (see Group 17 · Tonifying) — Spleen qi deficiency, no cold limbs; pale-colored blood indicates qi deficiency.
歸脾湯 （見 17・補益劑）——治脾氣虛，不會四肢冷，血淡色是氣虛。
Li Zhong Wan (see Group 15 · Warming Interior) — Spleen yang deficiency, Taiyin-pattern abdominal pain relieved by pressure/warmth; mainly treats pain, may include blood in stool.
理中丸 （見 15・溫裡劑）——治脾陽虛，太陰症狀會肚子痛（按或溫暖會緩解），主要治療痛，可能會有血便。
Huai Hua San — intestinal excess heat causing blood in stool; bright-red blood.
槐花散——治腸實熱造成的血便，血鮮紅色。
▍EXAM FOCUS — 本組出題重點
  • 血府逐瘀湯是桃紅四物湯＋四逆散兩個名方的合方，考試常考「這方是哪兩個方組成的」；使藥牛膝（引血下行）與桔梗（引藥上行）一升一降是特色。
  • 補陽還五湯的黃耆用量極大（120g），與當歸的比例是 20:1，重點在「大補氣以行血」而非直接活血，與當歸補血湯（同樣重用黃耆）常被拿來比較。
  • 桃核承氣湯其實是調胃承氣湯加桃仁、桂枝，考試常考底方為何。
  • 血便三方（跨組）鑑別是必考：黃土湯（脾陽虛，四肢冷，血色深）／歸脾湯（脾氣虛，血色淡，無四肢冷）／槐花散（腸實熱，血色鮮紅）。
  • 失笑散只有兩味藥（蒲黃、五靈脂），且五靈脂不可過量久服，常與其他方劑合用而非單獨使用。
  • 十灰散全方十味藥皆為炒炭使用（“十灰”之意），是全書中特殊的「炭藥止血」代表方。
Xue Fu Zhu Yu Tang
血府逐瘀湯【血瘀的胸痛】
Drive Out Stasis in the Mansion of Blood Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • [Tao Hong Si Wu Tang] — Peach Seed (Taoren) 12 g; Safflower (Honghua) 9 g; Chinese Angelica Root (Danggui) 9 g; Chuanxiong Rhizome (Chuanxiong) 5 g; Fresh Rehmannia Root (Shengdihuang) 9 g
  • [Si Ni San] — Red Peony (Chishao) 6 g; Bupleurum Root (Chaihu) 3 g; Bitter Orange (Zhiqiao) 6 g; Licorice (Gancao) 3 g
  • Envoy — Achyranthes (Niuxi) 9 g (moves qi down); Platycodon Root (Jiegeng) 5 g (moves qi up)
  • 【桃紅四物湯】— 桃仁 12 g；紅花 9 g；當歸 9 g；川芎 5 g；生地黃 9 g
  • 【四逆散】— 赤芍 6 g；柴胡 3 g；枳殼 6 g；甘草 3 g
  • 使藥 — 牛膝 9 g（引血下行）；桔梗 5 g（引藥上行）
功效
Functions
Promote qi and blood circulation to remove blood stasis. (For heart disease caused by blood stasis.)
行氣活血、化瘀止痛。血瘀造成的心臟病。
主治
Indication
Chest pain due to blood stasis — persistent chest pain suggests Qi Stagnation, sharp/stabbing chest pain suggests Blood Stagnation. (Bi-syndrome symptoms can be caused by wind-damp, or by blood stasis.)
因血瘀所致的胸痛——持續胸痛偏氣滯，尖銳刺痛偏血瘀。（痹症症狀可以是風濕，或是血瘀造成）
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Protracted chest pain and headache at a fixed location
  • Or palpitations
  • Insomnia
  • Irritability
  • Hectic fever
  • Dark purple lips or blackish eyelids
  • Deep red tongue with purple spots
  • Uneven or wiry and tense pulse

（症狀組合對應：血瘀、氣滯、熱、虛）

  • 胸痛、頭痛，痛處固定
  • 或心悸
  • 失眠
  • 煩躁
  • 骨蒸潮熱
  • 唇色紫暗或眼瞼發黑
  • 舌深紅有瘀斑
  • 脈澀或弦緊
加減
Modification
  • Original formula = Tao Hong Si Wu Tang + Si Ni San (the "Four Gates" function)
  • Hypochondriac pain (Liver) → Ge Xia Zhu Yu Tang: add Yujin, Xiangfu
  • Lower abdominal pain (dysmenorrhea with cold and clots) → Shao Fu Zhu Yu Tang: add Rougui, Xiaohuixiang (warms the Kidney)
  • Body itching (rheumatoid arthritis, fibromyalgia) → Shen Tong Zhu Yu Tang: add Qianghuo (whole-body/upper-body pain), Qinjiao (joint pain)
  • Headache, concussion → Tongqiao Huoxue Tang: add Shexiang (musk)
  • 原方即開四關：桃紅四物湯＋四逆散
  • 肋脅痛（走肝）：隔下逐瘀湯，加鬱金、香附
  • 下腹痛（經痛，有寒有血塊）：少腹逐瘀湯，加肉桂、小茴香，走腎暖起來
  • 身癢（風濕關節炎、纖維肌痛）：身痛逐瘀湯，加羌活（全身痛、上身痛）、秦艽（關節痛）
  • 頭痛、腦震盪：通竅活血湯，加麝香
Bu Yang Huan Wu Tang
補陽還五湯【氣虛血瘀中風後半身癱】
Tonify the Yang to Restore Five (Tenths) Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Chief — Astragalus (Huangqi) 120 g — massively tonifies qi

  • Earthworm (Dilong) 3 g — clears and unblocks the channels

Ratio Huangqi : Danggui = 20 : 1.

  • 君 — 黃耆 120 g — 大補氣
  • 當歸 6 g；川芎 3 g；赤芍 6 g；桃仁 3 g；紅花 3 g
  • 地龍 3 g — 清通經脈

黃耆：當歸＝20：1。

老師補充 ＆＆

補氣、活血、通經絡。

  • 口眼歪斜
  • 流涎

  • 脈弱而遲
加減

  • 多痰：加半夏、生薑

  • 頭痛暈眩（中風前後都有）：加鉤藤、菊花
  • 下肢癱瘓：加杜仲、牛膝

05 · Dryness 治燥劑

▍燥證病機 Pathogenesis of Dryness

外燥（實）External Dryness (Excess)English中文
Cold: mild wind-cold + dryness (dry throat, wiry pulse, etc.)
涼燥：輕微風寒＋燥（咽乾、脈弦等）
Heat: mild wind-heat + dryness (dry cough, dry nose and throat...)
溫燥：輕微風熱＋燥（乾咳、鼻乾咽乾等）
Advanced dryness with qi and yin damaged.
燥證進一步發展，氣陰兩傷。
內燥（虛）Internal Dryness (Deficiency)English中文
Upper Jiao: Lung yin deficiency (chronic cough...)
上焦：肺陰虛（慢性咳嗽等）
Middle Jiao: Stomach yin deficiency (diabetics...)
中焦：胃陰虛（消渴等）
Lower Jiao: Kidney and Large Intestine (constipation...)
下焦：腎與大腸（便秘等）

▍外燥方劑速記 Formulas for External Dryness

English中文
Xing Su San — contains Apricot Kernel; treats the earliest stage of dryness; the mildest of the three.
杏蘇散——有杏仁，最初期的燥證，最溫和。
Sang Xing Tang — contains Mulberry Leaf.
桑杏湯——有桑葉。
Qing Zao Jiu Fei Tang — for qi deficiency + yin deficiency.
清燥救肺湯——氣虛＋陰虛。

▍內燥方劑速記 Formulas for Internal Dryness

English中文
Bai He Gu Jin Tang / Mai Men Dong Tang / Yu Ye Tang / Zeng Ye Tang — the four representative internal-dryness formulas, each targeting a different organ/Jiao (see cards below).
百合固金湯／麥門冬湯／玉液湯／增液湯——四個代表性的內燥方劑，各自對應不同臟腑／三焦（詳見以下各方劑卡片）。

▍慢性咳嗽方劑比較 Compare Formulas for Chronic Cough

English中文
Bai He Gu Jin Tang — Lung + Kidney yin deficiency with empty heat
百合固金湯——肺＋腎陰虛之虛熱
Qing Zao Jiu Fei Tang — qi + yin deficiency from warm-dryness damaging the Lung
清燥救肺湯——溫燥傷肺，氣陰兩傷
Ke Xue Fang (see Group 04 · Regulating Blood) — Liver fire invading the Lung
咳血方 （見 04・理血劑）——肝火犯肺
Xie Bai San (see Group 14 · Clearing Heat) — Lung heat with wheezing/cough
瀉白散 （見 14・清熱劑）——肺熱喘咳
▍EXAM FOCUS — 本組出題重點
  • 外燥三方是燥證嚴重程度的階梯：杏蘇散（涼燥初期，痰稀白）→ 桑杏湯（溫燥，痰轉濃稠，各種乾）→ 清燥救肺湯（溫燥傷肺最重，氣陰兩傷）——常考「哪一方最輕/最重」。
  • 桑杏湯與桑菊飲鑑別：桑菊飲是溫病初期單純咳嗽（無明顯燥象），桑杏湯則有明顯「乾」的燥證表現。
  • 清燥救肺湯 vs 麻杏石甘湯：兩者都治肺熱喘咳，但清燥救肺湯是氣陰兩傷的虛證，麻杏石甘湯是肺部實熱高燒——虛實鑑別是常考重點。
  • 內燥四方各自對應：百合固金湯（肺腎陰虛虛熱，兼咳血）／麥門冬湯（肺胃陰虛，肺痿）／玉液湯（消渴症，氣陰兩虛型）／增液湯（大腸津虧便秘，陰虛不可用承氣湯者）。
  • 麥門冬湯與炙甘草湯的比較：氣＋陰虛用百合固金湯／麥門冬湯，陰＋陽虛（虛勞）則用炙甘草湯（見溫裡劑或補益劑相關章節）。
  • 增液湯的臨床意涵：專為「陰虛不可用承氣湯」的便秘患者設計（增水行舟），與麻子仁丸（潤下，含油脂類藥材）為不同的潤腸策略。
Xing Su San
杏蘇散【咳嗽＋清痰】
Apricot Kernel and Perilla Leaf Powder
組成
Ingredients
  • Dispersing wind-cold — Perilla Leaf (Zisuye): dispels exterior wind-cold
  • Nourishing Lung — Apricot Kernel (Xingren): moistens the Lung
  • Resolve cold-phlegm — Pinellia Tuber (Banxia): removes internal phlegm; Peucedanum Root (Qianhu): removes external phlegm; Platycodon Root (Jiegeng)
  • 散風寒 — 紫蘇葉：去外感風寒
  • 潤肺 — 杏仁：潤肺
  • 化寒痰 — 半夏：除內部的痰；前胡：除外部的痰；桔梗
功效
Functions
For cool-dryness attacking externally with Lung qi failing to disperse — an exterior pattern; the mildest formula, used at the very earliest stage before phlegm has thickened.
涼燥外襲、肺氣不宣之外感證；最初期的，痰還沒變濃稠；溫和的藥。
主治
Indication
Exterior dryness (cold type); exterior cold + dryness.
外燥（涼燥型）；外寒＋燥。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Fever and aversion to cold
  • Headache without sweating
  • Cough with thin, white phlegm
  • Nasal congestion, dry throat
  • Thin, white tongue coating
  • Floating or wiry pulse
  • 發熱惡寒
  • 頭痛無汗
  • 咳嗽痰稀而白
  • 鼻塞咽乾
  • 苔白薄
  • 脈浮或弦

（原始資料未列加減內容）
（原始資料未列加減內容）
Sang Xing Tang
桑杏湯【咳嗽＋濃痰】
Mulberry Leaf and Apricot Kernel Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Dispersing wind-heat — Mulberry Leaf (Sangye)
  • Nourishing Lung — Apricot Kernel (Xingren)
  • Nourishing Lung (additional, ＆＆) — Glehnia Root (Shashen); Tendrilled Fritillary Bulb (Beimu); Pear Peel (Lipi)
  • 散風熱 — 桑葉
  • 潤肺 — 杏仁
  • 潤肺（老師補充 ＆＆）— 沙蔘；貝母；梨皮
功效
Functions
For autumn-contracted warm-dryness scorching Lung fluids: body not very hot, dry cough without phlegm, dry throat and thirst, red tongue with thin white dry coating, large rapid pulse on the right. (This is the second stage — phlegm has become thick, with a general sensation of dryness throughout; still a mild formula.) Clears and disperses dry-heat; moistens the Lung and stops cough.
秋感溫燥，灼傷肺津，身不甚熱，乾咳無痰，咽乾口渴，舌紅，苔薄白而燥，右脈數大者。第二階段，痰變濃稠，感覺各種乾；溫和的藥。清宣燥熱，潤肺止咳。
主治
Indication
Exterior dryness (heat type).
外燥（溫燥型）。
📝 課堂筆記（原文僅中文）
桑菊飲——溫病的第一階段，單純咳嗽。
臨床表現
Manifestation

  • Dry throat and thirst
  • Red tongue, thin white and dry coating
  • Large, rapid pulse on the right
  • 乾咳無痰
  • 咽乾口渴
  • 舌紅，苔薄白而燥
  • 右脈數大者

Qing Zao Jiu Fei Tang
清燥救肺湯【氣虛＋陰虛】
Eliminate Dryness and Rescue the Lung Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • [Sang Xing Tang base] — Mulberry Leaf (Sangye); Apricot Kernel (Xingren)

  • Tonify qi for Lung and Spleen — Ginseng (Renshen)
  • Donkey Hide Gelatin (Ejiao); Loquat Leaf (Pipaye)
  • 【桑杏湯底】— 桑葉；杏仁
  • 清肺熱 — 石膏
  • 補肺陰 — 麥門冬
  • 補肺脾之氣 — 人蔘
  • 阿膠；枇杷葉
功效
Functions
For qi deficiency + yin deficiency; used when warm-dryness has damaged the Lung, injuring both qi and yin.
氣虛＋陰虛；用於溫燥傷肺，氣陰兩傷之證。
主治
Indication
Severe heat-dryness damaging the Lung with qi and yin deficiency (dry throat, thirsty, empty pulse); warm-dryness Lung-damage pattern.
溫燥傷肺，氣陰兩傷之證。

Bai He Gu Jin Tang
百合固金湯【肺陰＋腎陰虛的虛熱】
Lily Bulb Decoction to Preserve the Metal
組成
Ingredients
  • [Kidney — nourish yin, clear heat] (the Kidney must "grasp" qi and pass it to the Lung) — Fresh Rehmannia Root (Shengdihuang) 9 g; Prepared Rehmannia Root (Shudihuang) 9 g
  • [Lung yin — nourish yin, moisten Lung, generate fluids] — Lily Bulb (Baihe) 9 g; Glehnia Root (Shashen) 6 g; Ophiopogon Root (Maidong) 6 g; Scrophularia (Xuanshen) 6 g — benefits the throat; Tendrilled Fritillary Bulb (Beimu) 3 g — clears Lung, resolves phlegm, stops cough
  • [Tonify blood — nourish blood and yin] — Chinese Angelica Root (Danggui) 6 g; White Peony Root (Baishao) 6 g
  • Platycodon Root (Jiegeng) 3 g — clears Lung, resolves phlegm, stops cough
  • 【腎——滋陰清熱】（需腎抓住氣傳給肺）— 生地黃 9 g；熟地黃 9 g
  • 【肺陰——養陰潤肺生津】— 百合 9 g；沙參 6 g；麥門冬 6 g；玄蔘 6 g——利咽喉；貝母 3 g——清肺化痰止咳
  • 【養血益陰】— 當歸 6 g；白芍 6 g
  • 桔梗 3 g——清肺化痰止咳
功效
Functions
Addresses Lung disorders, treats pulmonary tuberculosis; Lung yin + Kidney yin deficiency with empty heat: dry, flushed cheeks and afternoon fever. Nourishes yin and clears heat; moistens the Lung and resolves phlegm.
處理肺的問題，治療肺結核；肺陰＋腎陰虛的虛熱：臉頰乾燥潮熱＋下午發燒。養陰清熱、潤肺化痰。
主治
Indication
Empty heat due to Lung and Kidney yin deficiency; Lung and Kidney yin deficiency with empty fire flaring, causing cough with blood-streaked phlegm.
因肺陰虛＋腎陰虛造成的虛熱；肺腎陰虧、虛火上炎所致咳嗽痰血。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Dry, sore throat
  • Chronic cough, wheezing
  • Blood in sputum
  • Hot flashes
  • Afternoon fever
  • Feverish sensation in palms and soles
  • Red tongue with little coating
  • Thin, rapid pulse
  • 咽喉乾燥、疼痛
  • 慢性咳嗽、氣喘
  • 咳痰帶血
  • 潮紅
  • 下午發燒
  • 手足心熱
  • 舌紅少苔
  • 脈細數
加減
Modification
  • Chest/hypochondriac distension and fullness → add Zhishi, Chaihu
  • Loss of voice, sore throat → add Tiandong, Zhimu
  • Cough with wheezing → add Xingren, Houpo, Zhiqiao
  • Pulmonary tuberculosis → add Baiji, Baibu, Xiakucao
  • Copious, thick yellow phlegm → add Gualou, Zhuru
  • Cough with blood-streaked sputum → add Baimaogen, Xianhecao
  • Hemoptysis with chest oppression → combine with Qi Li San
  • Bronchiectasis with hemoptysis → add Qingdai
  • 胸脅脹滿：加枳實、柴胡
  • 失聲咽痛：加天冬、知母
  • 咳嗽氣喘：加杏仁、厚朴、枳殼
  • 肺結核：加白芨、百部、夏枯草
  • 痰多黃稠：加瓜蔞、竹茹
  • 咳痰帶血：加白茅根、仙鶴草
  • 咯血胸悶：合七厘散
  • 支氣管擴張咯血：加青黛
Mai Men Dong Tang
麥門冬湯【肺陰＋胃陰虛／肺失功能】
Ophiopogonis Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Chief — Ophiopogon Root (Maidong) 35 g — used in a large dose to nourish Lung and Stomach and clear/descend empty fire, as the chief herb
  • Assistant — Pinellia Tuber (Banxia) 5 g — descends rebellion and resolves phlegm
  • Deputy — Ginseng (Renshen) 5 g — boosts qi and generates fluids
  • [Three envoy herbs: benefit Stomach qi, generate fluids] — Jujube (Dazao) 5 g; Polished Rice (Jingmi) 5 g; Licorice (Gancao) 3 g
  • 君 — 麥門冬 35 g——重用麥門冬滋養肺胃，清降虛火為君
  • 佐 — 半夏 5 g——降逆化痰
  • 臣 — 人蔘 5 g——益氣生津
  • 【三使藥：益胃氣，生津液】— 大棗 5 g；粳米 5 g；甘草 3 g
功效
Functions
Addresses Lung disorders — the Lung has lost function and cannot expel phlegm, e.g. pulmonary fibrosis; Lung atrophy (Fei Wei); Lung and Stomach fluids damaged, empty fire flaring upward — cough with facial flushing that is persistent/continuous. Nourishes and clears the Lung and Stomach, descends rebellious qi.
處理肺的問題；肺失去功能，無法排出痰，例如肺部纖維化；肺痿。肺胃津傷，虛火上炎；咳時顏面紅潮、連續不斷者。清養肺胃，降逆下氣。
主治
Indication
Lung atrophy (pulmonary fibrosis) due to yin deficiency and dryness in the Lung and Stomach. When Lung-Stomach qi and yin are restored, empty fire settles, rebellious qi descends, phlegm/saliva clears, the throat is comfortable, and the cough/wheeze resolves on its own.
因肺胃陰虛燥證所致的肺痿（肺纖維化）。使肺胃氣陰得復，則虛火平，逆氣降，痰涎清，咽喉利，咳喘自愈。
📝 課堂筆記（原文僅中文）
氣＋陰虛：百合固金湯（或本方）。陰＋陽虛（虛勞）：炙甘草湯。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Persistent cough with facial flushing
  • Dry mouth and throat
  • Rebellious, wheezing qi
  • Fluids depleted, empty fire flaring
  • 咳時顏面紅潮，連續不斷
  • 口咽乾燥
  • 氣逆而喘
  • 津傷，虛火上炎
加減

Yu Ye Tang
玉液湯【專治消渴】
Jade Fluid Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Tonify qi + yin — Chinese Yam (Shanyao); Astragalus (Huangqi)
  • Nourish yin, generate fluids — Pueraria (Gegen); Anemarrhena (Zhimu); Trichosanthes Root (Tianhuafen)
  • Promote digestion/appetite — Chicken Gizzard Lining (Jineijin); Schisandra Fruit (Wuweizi) — stops frequent urination
  • 補氣＋陰 — 山藥；黃耆
  • 滋陰生津 — 葛根；知母；天花粉
  • 促進消化、食慾 — 雞內金；五味子——止頻尿
功效
Functions
Specifically treats Xiao Ke (wasting-thirst / diabetes): Upper Jiao — easily thirsty; Middle Jiao — hunger (qi + yin deficiency); Lower Jiao — frequent urination. (The formula's effect travels through all Three Burners.) Boosts qi and nourishes yin; generates fluids and stops thirst.
專門治消渴症，上焦：易渴；中焦：飢餓（氣＋陰虛）；下焦：頻尿。（藥效走三焦）益氣養陰，生津止渴。
主治
Indication
Xiao Ke (diabetes) with Stomach qi deficiency and dryness.
消渴（糖尿病）兼胃氣虛而燥。
📝 課堂筆記（原文僅中文）
消渴症分兩種：氣虛＋陰虛：體型瘦小；很多濕氣：體型肥胖。

相似方劑：玉女煎——腎陰虛而虛熱，造成牙齦腫痛；六味地黃丸——腎陰虛。

06 · Wind 治風劑

▍風證病機概述 Pathogenesis of Wind

English中文
External wind — symptoms: skin rash, facial paralysis, pain (wind invading the channels)
外風——症狀：皮膚疹、顏面神經麻痺、疼痛（風犯經絡）
Internal wind — symptoms: Liver wind
內風——症狀：肝風

▍外風方劑速記 Formulas for External Wind

English中文
Chuan Xiong Cha Tiao San — external wind causing headache.
川芎茶調散——外風導致頭痛。
Xiao Feng San — red, swollen skin; a skin-specific formula.
消風散——皮膚紅腫，皮膚專用藥。
Cang Er Zi San — seasonal allergic rhinitis with yellow nasal discharge, a heat pattern. (Similar to Xiao Qing Long Tang, but that formula is for clear nasal discharge, a cold pattern.)
蒼耳子散——季節性過敏鼻炎，黃鼻涕，熱症。（跟小青龍湯相似，小青龍是清的鼻涕，寒症）
Qian Zheng San — facial nerve dysfunction (Bell's palsy), not a stroke; has a stronger preventive effect (against progression).
牽正散——顏面神經失調，不是中風，預防效果較強。
Xiao Huo Luo Dan — joint pain from wind + cold + dampness.
小活絡丹——關節疼痛，風＋寒＋濕。

▍相似方劑比較 Compare Similar Formulas

頭痛鑑別English中文
Chuan Xiong Cha Tiao San — no wind-cold exterior symptoms, but frequent headache
川芎茶調散——沒有風寒症狀，但常常頭痛
Jiu Wei Qiang Huo Tang (see Group 10 · Exterior Wind-Cold) — exterior wind-cold, various types of pain
九味羌活湯 （見 10・辛溫解表）——exterior 風寒，各種痛
顏面麻痺鑑別English中文
Qian Zheng San — targets only facial nerve dysfunction; does not address stroke
牽正散——只針對顏面神經失調，不處理中風
Bu Yang Huan Wu Tang (see Group 04 · Regulating Blood) — targets recovery from stroke
補陽還五湯 （見 04・理血劑）——針對中風的症狀調理

▍內風方劑速記 Formulas for Internal Wind

English中文
Ling Jiao Gou Teng Tang — from extreme excess heat causing severe wind symptoms; used to prevent wind-syndrome/epilepsy from forming during a period of extreme excess heat.
羚角鉤藤湯——因過度實熱造成極度的風症，在過度實熱時預防風症癲癇的形成。
Zhen Gan Xi Feng Tang — prevents stroke caused by Liver yang rising; used before a stroke. Pseudo-stroke / pre-stroke signs: high blood pressure, flushed face.
鎮肝熄風湯——預防肝陽上亢造成的中風，在中風前使用。類中風／先期中風症狀：高血壓、臉部潮紅。
Tian Ma Gou Teng Yin — a commonly seen "dizziness" formula.
天麻鉤藤飲——常見的暈眩用方。
Da Ding Feng Zhu — treats the hand-tremor wind-syndrome of Parkinson's disease.
大定風珠——治療帕金森氏症的手抖風症。
Di Huang Yin Zi — commonly seen in elderly patients unable to walk or speak, whole-body stroke, extreme Kidney qi deficiency. (Bu Yang Huan Wu Tang is for half-body stroke.)
地黃飲子——常見於無法行走跟說話的老人，全部的身體中風，極度腎氣虛。補陽還五湯是一半的身體中風。

▍內風病機 Pathogenesis of Internal Wind

English中文
Excess heat causing internal wind — high fever causing loss of consciousness
實熱造成內風——高燒造成失去意識
Liver yang rising causing internal wind
肝陽上亢造成內風
Yin deficiency
陰虛
Blood deficiency — (Liver yang rising + yin deficiency is the cause of the hand tremor in Parkinson's disease.)
血虛 ——肝陽上亢＋陰虛是帕金森氏症的手抖原因。

▍內風常用藥 Herbs for Internal Wind

English中文
Antelope's Horn (Lingyangjiao); Red Ochre / Hematite (Daizheshi); Oyster Shell (Muli)
羚羊角；代赭石；牡蠣
Uncaria Stem (Gouteng); Gastrodia Tuber (Tianma)
鉤藤；天麻
Scorpion (Quanxie); Earthworm (Dilong)
全蠍；地龍

▍三個滋陰熄風方 Three Yin-Nourishing Wind Formulas

English中文
Tian Ma Gou Teng Yin (Tianma, Gouteng, Baishao, etc.) — Indication: dizziness and headache due to Liver yang.
天麻鉤藤飲（天麻、鉤藤、白芍等）——主治：肝陽所致頭暈頭痛。
Da Ding Feng Zhu (Guiban, Biejia, Muli, etc.) — Indication: tremor due to yin deficiency.
大定風珠（龜板、鱉甲、牡蠣等）——主治：陰虛所致震顫。
Di Huang Yin Zi (Dihuang, Rougui, Shichangpu, etc.) — Indication: inability to speak and walk due to Kidney deficiency.
地黃飲子（地黃、肉桂、石菖蒲等）——主治：腎虛所致無法言語行走。

天麻鉤藤飲——專治肝陽上亢內風的頭痛
▍EXAM FOCUS — 本組出題重點
  • 川芎茶調散是頭痛部位定經絡用藥的經典方——川芎（少陽＋厥陰）、羌活（太陽，後腦痛）、白芷（陽明，額頭痛）、防風（少陽，頭側痛）、細辛（少陰，頭腦脹滿）——考試常考「哪個部位頭痛用哪味藥」。方中薄荷用量特別大（240g），因為薄荷性寒，須加大量以平衡全方辛溫之性。
  • 牽正散 vs 補陽還五湯：牽正散只治顏面神經失調（貝爾氏麻痺），不治中風；補陽還五湯才是中風後遺症專方。
  • 小活絡丹含川烏、草烏（皆有毒），只能急性期短期使用，與當歸四逆湯（溫和、可長期日常使用、無特定關節針對性）常被拿來對比。
  • 內風五方依病程/病機鑑別：羚角鉤藤湯（實熱極重，急性癲癇/高熱抽搐）／鎮肝熄風湯（肝陽上亢，中風前）／天麻鉤藤飲（肝陽頭痛暈眩）／大定風珠（陰虛，慢性手抖如帕金森氏症）／地黃飲子（腎虛，全身雙側中風，不能言語行走）。
  • 鎮肝熄風湯 vs 補陽還五湯：中風前預防（鎮肝熄風湯） vs 中風後治療（補陽還五湯），是常考的時間點鑑別。
Chuan Xiong Cha Tiao San
川芎茶調散【容易頭痛】
Chuanxiong Powder to Be Taken with Green Tea
組成
Ingredients
  • Stop pain — Chuanxiong Rhizome (Chuanxiong) 120 g — Shaoyang + Jueyin headache; Notopterygium Root (Qianghuo) 60 g — Taiyang, occipital headache; Angelica (Baizhi) 60 g — Yangming, frontal headache; Ledebouriella Root (Fangfeng) 45 g — Shaoyang, temporal headache; Asarum (Xixin) 30 g — Shaoyin, sensation of fullness deep in the head
  • [Herbs that travel to the head] (＆＆) — Schizonepeta (Jingjie) 120 g (spicy-warm); Mentha (Bohe) 240 g (spicy-cool — the only cold herb here, hence the larger dose to balance the formula's overall warm nature)
  • Licorice (Gancao) 60 g
  • 止痛 — 川芎 120 g（少陽＋厥陰頭痛）；羌活 60 g（太陽，後腦痛）；白芷 60 g（陽明，額頭痛）；防風 45 g（少陽，頭側頭痛）；細辛 30 g（少陰，感覺頭腦裡面脹滿）
  • 會往頭上走（老師補充 ＆＆）— 荊芥 120 g（辛溫）；薄荷 240 g（辛涼——只有薄荷是冷的，所以量要加大，以平衡藥性）
  • 甘草 60 g
功效
Functions
Dispel wind and relieve pain. For frequent headache, especially at the vertex; can be made into a tea bag for daily use.
祛風止痛。容易頭痛、頭頂痛；可做成茶包，每日飲用。
📝 課堂筆記（原文僅中文）
頭頂痛：DU20（近端）、LR2為瀉（遠端）／LR3為補（遠端）。前額痛：ST8＋印堂，可再加迎香。後腦痛：後背＋腳，BL10＝羌活（後腦痛）。頭側頭痛：走肝，GB20＝防風（枕小神經，會導致頭側頭痛）。CV4＋KD12可治療下腹痛。
主治
Indication
Headache due to invasion by exogenous pathogenic wind.
因外感風邪所致的頭痛。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Headache, or pain on the top of the head
  • Mild aversion to cold with fever
  • Thin, whitish tongue coating
  • Superficial pulse
  • 頭痛，或頭頂痛
  • 輕微惡寒發熱
  • 舌苔薄白
  • 脈浮
加減
Modification
  • Wind-heat headache with yellow tongue coating and rapid pulse → replace Xixin and Qianghuo with Chrysanthemum Flower (Juhua), Uncaria Stem (Gouteng)
  • Chronic headache → add White Stiff Silkworm (Jiangcan), Scorpion (Quanxie), Peach Seed (Taoren), Safflower (Honghua)

Similar to Jiu Wei Qiang Huo Tang (see Group 10).

  • 風熱頭痛（苔黃脈數）：以菊花、鉤藤取代細辛、羌活
  • 長期頭痛：加僵蠶、全蠍、桃仁、紅花

跟九味羌活湯相似（見第10組）。

Xiao Feng San
消風散【皮膚紅腫】
Eliminate Wind Powder
組成
Ingredients
  • Disperse wind — Ledebouriella Root (Fangfeng) 9 g; Cicada Slough (Chantui) 3 g; Schizonepeta (Jingjie) 9 g; Arctium Fruit (Niubangzi) 9 g
  • Clear heat and dampness — Gypsum (Shigao) 15 g; Atractylodes Rhizome (Cangzhu) 6 g; Flavescent Sophora Root (Kushen) 6 g; Clematis Stem (Mutong) 1.5 g; Cannabis Seed (Maziren) 9 g
  • Nourish yin and blood — Chinese Angelica Root (Danggui) 9 g; Fresh Rehmannia Root (Shengdihuang) 12 g; Anemarrhena Rhizome (Zhimu) 9 g; Licorice (Gancao) 1.5 g
  • 散風 — 防風 9 g；蟬蛻 3 g；荊芥 9 g；牛蒡子 9 g
  • 清熱祛濕 — 石膏 15 g；蒼朮 6 g；苦參 6 g；木通 1.5 g；麻子仁 9 g
  • 養陰血 — 當歸 9 g；生地黃 12 g；知母 9 g；甘草 1.5 g
功效
Functions
Dispel wind and nourish blood; clear heat-dampness. (Can treat urticaria; throat itchiness is because of Wind, so itchy that one cannot speak; blood deficiency and blood heat cause redness/swelling (hence nourish yin and blood), while itching is because of wind; damp-caused eczema tends to stay in a fixed location, while wind-caused urticaria tends to move around.)
祛風養血；清熱除濕。可治療蕁麻疹。喉嚨癢是因為有風，癢到無法說話。血虛跟血熱造成紅腫（所以要滋陰補血），癢是因為風。濕氣造成的濕疹較固定的地方，風造成的風疹比較到處跑。
📝 課堂筆記（原文僅中文）
去熱：LI11，SP8、SP9、LR2。補血時同時去血熱。
主治
Indication
Rubella and eczema, marked by an itchy, red skin rash or dots/spots over the whole body with pruritus, exudation of body fluid after scratching, a whitish or yellowish tongue coating, a superficial and rapid pulse.
風疹及濕疹，全身癢而紅之皮疹或點狀斑塊，搔抓後滲液，舌苔白或黃，脈浮數。
臨床表現
Manifestation
（症狀已列於主治欄）
（症狀已列於主治欄）
加減
Modification
  • Severe itching due to toxic wind → add Forsythia Fruit (Lianqiao), Honeysuckle Flower (Jinyinhua)
  • Severe heat in the blood → add Moutan Bark (Mudanpi), Red Peony (Chishao)
  • Severe damp-heat → add Plantain Seed (Cheqianzi), Spirodela (Fuping)
  • 風毒皮膚癢：加連翹、金銀花
  • 血熱：加牡丹皮、赤芍
  • 濕熱：加車前子、浮萍

Qian Zheng San
牽正散【顏面神經失調】
Lead to Symmetry Powder

功效

Eliminates wind-phlegm and stops spasm, unblocking the facial channels.
祛風化痰、通絡止痙。
主治
Indication
Facial paralysis (Bell's palsy) due to wind-phlegm attacking the meridians of the face — sudden facial deviation, occurring only on the face, acute onset. (Compare: Bu Yang Huan Wu Tang is specifically for stroke.)
因風痰侵襲面部經絡所致的顏面神經麻痺（貝爾氏麻痺）——突然臉部歪斜，只發生在臉，急性起病。（相較：補陽還五湯是中風專用）

Xiao Huo Luo Dan ⚠ 有毒 Toxic
小活絡丹【急性有毒／風寒濕關節痛】
Minor Invigorate the Channels Special Pill
組成
Ingredients
  • [Wind-cold-dampness] — Aconite Root (Chuanwu) 6 g (toxic); Kusnezoff Monkshood Root (Caowu) 6 g (toxic)
  • [Moving blood] — Frankincense (Ruxiang) 6 g; Myrrh (Moyao) 6 g; Arisaema Tuber (Tiannanxing) 6 g (wind-phlegm); Earthworm (Dilong) 6 g (guides the herbs into the channels)
  • 【風寒濕】— 川烏（有毒）6 g；草烏（有毒）6 g
  • 【行血】— 乳香 6 g；沒藥 6 g；天南星 6 g（風痰）；地龍 6 g（引藥進經絡）
功效
Functions
For acute wind-cold-damp symptoms — wind-cold invasion causing tightened channels and swollen, painful joints, where the affected area feels cold to the touch. (Compare with Dang Gui Si Ni Tang, essentially Guizhi Tang + Danggui: that formula treats chronic patterns, warming the channels gently, can be taken daily, with no specific site and not joint-specific. Xiao Huo Luo Dan is for acute conditions, strongly potent, targets joint pain specifically, and — being toxic — can only be used during the acute phase.)
急性的風寒濕症狀，因為風寒侵襲，導致經絡緊縮跟關節腫痛，並且痛的地方摸起來是冷的。相較當歸四逆湯（桂枝湯＋當歸）：當歸四逆湯是治療長期症狀，讓經絡暖起來，是溫和的藥可每天吃，沒有特定的位子也不針對關節；小活絡丹是急性，藥性強，針對關節疼痛，因有毒只能急性期使用。
📝 課堂筆記（原文僅中文）
血循環：最強的是動物性，如水蛭。次強是川芎、乳香、沒藥。
主治
Indication
Bi-syndrome due to wind-cold-dampness attacking the meridians.
因風寒濕侵襲經絡所致的痹症。
臨床表現
Manifestation
Case example: a patient with cold hands, unable to make a fist, with tight muscle near Laogong (PC8). Treatment: needling local points near Laogong + LI10 (Shousanli) + infrared lamp on the hand, plus Xiao Huo Luo Dan — recovered after one treatment. (If the hand were hot instead, a heat pattern would need to be treated instead.)
案例：病人手冷，且無法握緊，手掌近勞宮穴附近的肌肉緊繃。下針勞宮附近的 local point＋LI10 手三里＋紅外線燈照手，再開小活絡丹，一次治療後即恢復正常。（如果手是熱的，即要治療熱症）
加減
Modification
（原始資料未列加減內容）
（原始資料未列加減內容）
Zhen Gan Xi Feng Tang
鎮肝熄風湯【肝陽上亢中風前兆】
Sedate the Liver and Extinguish Wind Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Chief — Achyranthes (Niuxi) 30 g — a heavy substance that sends qi downward
  • Deputy (sedate Liver yang) — Hematite (Daizheshi) 30 g; Dragon's Bone (Longgu) 15 g; Oyster Shell (Muli) 15 g; Tortoise Plastron (Guiban) 15 g
  • Assistant (nourish Liver yin to pacify yang) — White Peony Root (Baishao) 15 g; Scrophularia (Xuanshen) 5 g; Asparagus Root (Tianmendong) 15 g
  • [Clear Liver heat and move Liver qi] — Oriental Wormwood (Qinghao) 6 g; Sichuan Chinaberry (Chuanlianzi) 6 g; Germinated Barley (Maiya) 6 g
  • Licorice (Gancao) 4.5 g
  • 君 — 牛膝 30 g——用重量重的東西，讓氣往下走
  • 臣（鎮肝陽）— 代赭石 30 g；龍骨 15 g；牡蠣 15 g；龜板 15 g
  • 佐（滋陰以安撫陽）— 白芍 15 g；玄蔘 5 g；天門冬 15 g
  • 【清肝熱、行肝氣】— 青蒿 6 g；川楝子 6 g；麥芽 6 g
  • 甘草 4.5 g
功效
Functions
Tranquilize the Liver and subdue endogenous wind. (If a stroke is of a heat type, the patient collapses with convulsion + trembling; if a deficiency type, the patient collapses with convulsion but without trembling. For Bi-pattern collapse: revive the brain and open the orifices. For Tuo-pattern [collapse of yang], rescue yang and reverse the reversal, using Ginseng.)
鎮肝熄風。中風如果是熱，會倒下驚攣＋抖動；中風如果是虛，會倒下驚攣但不會抖動。痹症：醒腦開竅。脫症：回陽救逆、用人蔘。
主治
Indication
Early signs of stroke — prevents stroke caused by Liver yang rising; used before stroke. Pseudo-stroke / pre-stroke signs: high blood pressure, flushed face.
中風前兆——預防肝陽上亢造成的中風，在中風前使用。類中風／先期中風症狀：高血壓、臉部潮紅。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Early signs of stroke: one side of the body tilts, tongue deviates to one side
  • Vertigo
  • Tinnitus
  • Feverish sensation in the head
  • Flushed face as if drunk
  • Gradually limited movement
  • Deviation of eyes and mouth
  • Or fainting in serious cases, failing to recover after waking
  • Wiry, forceful pulse
  • 身體一半會歪斜、舌頭歪一邊
  • 眩暈
  • 耳鳴
  • 頭部發熱感
  • 面赤如醉
  • 逐漸活動受限
  • 口眼歪斜
  • 或重症昏厥，醒後不能恢復
  • 脈弦有力
加減
Modification
  • Severe heat → add Gypsum (Shigao), Capejasmine Fruit (Zhizi) (clears Triple-Burner heat)
  • Severe eye pain → add Abalone Shell (Shijueming) or Uncaria Stem (Gouteng) and Juhua
  • Profuse sputum (can cause loss of consciousness) → add Arisaema (Dannanxing), Fritillary Bulb (Beimu)
  • Forceless pulse (extreme Liver-Kidney deficiency) → add Prepared Rehmannia Root (Shudihuang), Dogwood Fruit (Shanzhuyu) (tonify Liver and Kidney)
  • 有熱：加石膏、梔子，清三焦熱
  • 眼睛疼痛：加石決明或鉤藤、菊花
  • 痰多（會造成失去意識）：加膽南星、貝母
  • 脈無力（肝腎極度虛）：加熟地黃、山茱萸，補肝腎

Da Ding Feng Zhu
大定風珠【陰虛而肝風手抖】
Major Arrest Wind Pearl
組成
Ingredients
  • Nourish yin — Fresh Rehmannia (Shengdihuang), Ophiopogon (Maidong), White Peony (Baishao)
  • Sedate yang and wind — Tortoise Plastron (Guiban), Turtle Shell (Biejia), Oyster Shell (Muli)
  • Blood and fluids — Donkey Hide Gelatin (Ejiao), Cannabis Seed (Maziren), Schisandra Fruit (Wuweizi)
  • Licorice (Gancao)
  • 養陰 — 生地黃、麥門冬、白芍
  • 鎮陽熄風 — 龜板、鱉甲、牡蠣
  • 養血生津 — 阿膠、麻子仁、五味子
  • 甘草
功效
Functions
Nourishes yin and extinguishes wind; suppresses the tremor/twitching response of the skeletal muscles; treats the hand-tremor wind-syndrome of Parkinson's disease.
滋陰熄風；抑制骨骼肌的顫搐反應；治療帕金森氏症的手抖風症。
主治
Indication
Liver wind due to yin deficiency.
因陰虛所致的肝風。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Tremor, e.g. as in Parkinson's disease
  • Little tongue coating
  • Weak pulse

Original notes also mention this pattern being seen in meningitis, sepsis, and heart failure (as differential context, not as this formula's direct indications).

  • 震顫，例如帕金森氏症
  • 舌苔少
  • 脈弱

原稿另提及此類病機也可見於腦膜炎、敗血症、心力衰竭（作為鑑別脈絡，非本方直接主治）。

加減
Modification
（原始資料未列加減內容）
（原始資料未列加減內容）
Di Huang Yin Zi
地黃飲子【全身雙側中風】
Rehmannia Drink
組成
Ingredients
  • Tonify Kidney and Liver — Prepared Rehmannia (Shudihuang), Dogwood Fruit (Shanzhuyu)
  • Tonify Kidney — Prepared Aconite Root (Fuzi), Cinnamon Bark (Rougui), Morinda Root (Baji), Cistanche (Roucongrong)
  • Tonify yin — Ophiopogon (Maidong), Dendrobium (Shihu), Schisandra Fruit (Wuweizi)
  • Phlegm and Shen — Polygala (Yuanzhi), Poria with Wood Spirit (Fushen), Acorus (Shichangpu)
  • Harmonize — Mentha (Bohe), Jujube (Dazao), Fresh Ginger
  • 補腎肝 — 熟地黃、山茱萸
  • 補腎 — 附子、肉桂、巴戟天、肉蓯蓉
  • 補陰 — 麥門冬、石斛、五味子
  • 化痰安神 — 遠志、茯神、石菖蒲
  • 調和 — 薄荷、大棗、生薑
功效
Functions
Nourishes Kidney yin, tonifies Kidney yang, resolves phlegm and opens the orifices. (For cerebral thrombosis formation, syringomyelia.) Commonly seen in elderly patients unable to walk or speak — whole-body stroke, extreme Kidney qi deficiency. (Bu Yang Huan Wu Tang is for half-body stroke.)
滋腎陰，補腎陽，化痰開竅（腦血栓形成、脊髓空洞症）。常見於無法行走跟說話的老人，全部的身體中風，極度腎氣虛。（補陽還五湯是一半的身體中風）
主治
Indication
Paralysis due to Kidney deficiency; stroke, atrophy syndrome (Wei-syndrome).
因腎虛所致的麻痺；中風、痿證。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Failure to speak and walk
  • Peeled-off tongue coating
  • Dry mouth without desire to drink
  • Thready, weak, and slow pulse
  • 無法說話、行走
  • 舌苔剝落
  • 口乾不欲飲
  • 脈細弱

07 · Dampness 祛濕劑

▍利水方劑總覽 Summary for Formulas That Drain Dampness

English中文
Wu Ling San — Taiyang cold syndrome with water retention in the bladder.
五苓散——太陽寒證，水停膀胱。
Zhu Ling Tang — water retention with heat accumulation.
豬苓湯——水停兼熱。
Wu Pi San — general edema in the body due to Spleen deficiency.
五皮散——脾虛所致全身水腫。
Fang Ji Huang Qi Tang — acute edema/swelling due to Wei-qi deficiency.
防己黃耆湯——衛氣虛所致急性水腫。

▍濕證病機 Pathogenesis of Dampness

English中文
Dampness affecting the Spleen — bloating, poor appetite
濕困脾——脹氣、沒胃口
Water retention — edema
水停——水腫
Heat-dampness — jaundice
濕熱——黃疸
Cold-dampness — diarrhea, itchy skin
寒濕——腹瀉、皮膚癢
Wind-dampness — Bi-syndrome, rheumatoid arthritis
風濕——痹症、風濕關節炎
▍EXAM FOCUS — 本組出題重點（16方，分五類）
  • 本組依「濕證病機」分五類，跟前面病機表直接對應——考試常直接考「這個症狀屬於哪一類濕證，該用哪一方」。
  • 平胃散 vs 藿香正氣散：後者是平胃散的進階版，多了解表藥（治外感兼脾胃濕滯的「裡症」），並常與香薷散（治暑熱的「表症」）鑑別。
  • 五苓散 vs 豬苓湯：同樣治太陽腑證水停，但五苓散偏溫（無熱象）、豬苓湯偏涼（兼熱兼陰虛）——常考鑑別。
  • 「四飲」（金匱要略）定位是必考：痰飲（苓桂朮甘湯，中焦）／懸飲（十棗湯，脅下）／溢飲（五皮散，四肢體表）／支飲（小青龍湯、苓甘五味薑辛湯，上焦）。
  • 寒濕四方是同一病理演變的漸進系列：四逆湯（純溫陽）→真武湯（腎陽虛水腫，加化濕藥）→實脾飲（脾腎陽虛全身水腫，再加行氣藥）——常考排列順序與差異。
  • 獨活寄生湯（虛證，下背痛）vs 羌活勝濕湯（實證，上背痛）是風濕兩方鑑別重點，考試常考「虛實」與「上下背」兩個維度。
  • 八正散 vs 小薊飲子：兩者都治淋症，但八正散是純濕熱下焦（無血尿）、小薊飲子兼心火血尿。
Ping Wei San
平胃散【基本化濕氣】
Calm the Stomach Powder
組成
Ingredients
  • Chief — Atractylodes Rhizome (Cangzhu) 15 g — directly drains water, sends qi upward
  • Magnolia Bark (Houpo) 9 g — sends qi downward
  • Citrus Peel (Chenpi) 9 g — moves qi
  • Baked Licorice (Zhigancao) 4 g
  • 君 — 蒼朮 15 g——直接排水，讓氣往上走
  • 厚朴 9 g——讓氣往下走
  • 陳皮 9 g——行氣
  • 炙甘草 4 g
老師補充 ＆＆
白朮：是補脾以化濕（蒼朮與白朮是常見對藥）。
功效
Functions
Dry dampness, invigorate the Spleen, and harmonize the Stomach. A very basic dampness-transforming formula — only transforms dampness, does not tonify the Spleen (Spleen deficiency mainly presents with fatigue and poor appetite). Main symptoms: poor appetite, heavy dampness.
燥濕健脾、和胃。很基本的化濕氣藥方，只有化濕沒有補脾（脾虛主要會有疲勞、沒胃口）。主要症狀：沒胃口、濕氣重。
主治
Indication
Dampness retention in the Spleen and Stomach. (Pure dampness accumulation is usually caused by poor diet or external infection. A pale, swollen tongue coating indicates Spleen deficiency; a yellow, thick, greasy coating indicates excessive dampness.)
脾胃濕滯。單純濕氣累積的原因是因為錯誤飲食或外來感染。蒼白腫大的舌苔是脾虛，黃又厚膩的舌苔是濕氣過重。
📝 課堂筆記（原文僅中文）
四君子湯是因脾虛（主要會有疲勞、沒胃口）造成濕氣，平胃散是專門去除濕氣問題沒有補脾。健脾丸：四君子湯＋神曲、麥芽、山楂，促進食慾、促進消化、健脾。六君子湯：四君子湯＋木香、砂仁。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Abdominal fullness
  • Poor appetite
  • Reduced sense of taste (from Spleen deficiency)
  • Nausea and vomiting
  • Regurgitation
  • Heaviness of the body
  • Lassitude and sleepiness
  • Thick, greasy, whitish tongue coating
  • Slow or smooth pulse
  • 腹脹
  • 沒胃口
  • 味覺減退（脾虛所致）
  • 噁心嘔吐
  • 反胃
  • 身重
  • 倦怠嗜睡
  • 舌苔厚膩白
  • 脈遲或滑
加減
Modification
  • Food retention (Spleen-deficiency-based indigestion) → add Shenqu, Maiya, Shanzha
  • Severe abdominal distension → add Muxiang, Sharen
  • Diarrhea → add Fuling, Cheqianzi, Zexie
  • Cold abdominal pain → add Ganjiang, Rougui
  • Heat present → add Huanglian, Huangqin, Zhizi
  • Alternating chills and fever → add Chaihu, Huangqin
  • Constipation → add Dahuang, Laifuzi

Caution: contraindicated during pregnancy.

  • 積食：加神曲、麥芽、山楂（三藥組合用於脾虛型消化不良）
  • 腹脹：加木香、砂仁
  • 腹瀉：加茯苓、車前子、澤瀉
  • 腹寒痛：加乾薑、肉桂
  • 有熱：加黃連、黃芩、梔子
  • 往來寒熱：加柴胡、黃芩
  • 便祕：加大黃、萊菔子

⚠ 使用禁忌：孕婦禁用。

Huo Xiang Zheng Qi San
藿香正氣散【外感而脾胃濕氣】
Agastache Powder to Rectify Qi
組成
Ingredients
  • Release exterior and resolve dampness — Agastache (Huoxiang) 90 g; Perilla Leaf (Zisuye) 30 g; Angelica (Baizhi) 30 g
  • [Upgraded Ping Wei San — "Cangzhu" role] — Poria (Fuling) 30 g + White Atractylodes (Baizhu) 60 g (the two together play the role of Cangzhu)
  • ["Houpo" role] — Magnolia Bark (Houpo) 60 g; Pinellia Tuber (Banxia) 60 g (strengthens Houpo's effect)
  • ["Chenpi" role] — Platycodon Root (Jiegeng) 60 g (moves qi in the upper Jiao); Citrus Peel (Chenpi) 60 g (moves qi in the middle Jiao); Areca Peel (Dafupi) 30 g (moves qi in the lower Jiao)
  • Baked Licorice (Zhigancao) 75 g
  • 解表化濕 — 藿香 90 g；紫蘇葉 30 g；白芷 30 g
  • 【平胃散升級版——「蒼朮」角色】— 茯苓 30 g＋白朮 60 g（兩者結合＝蒼朮）
  • 【「厚朴」角色】— 厚朴 60 g；半夏 60 g（讓厚朴效果更強）
  • 【「陳皮」角色】— 桔梗 60 g（行氣上焦）；陳皮 60 g（行氣中焦）；檳榔皮（大腹皮）30 g（行氣下焦）
  • 炙甘草 75 g
功效
Functions
Release the exterior and resolve dampness; harmonize the Spleen and Stomach. An upgraded version of Ping Wei San, specifically for dampness (an interior pattern) caused by unusual diet or external pathogens — the vomiting and diarrhea resemble summer-heat (an exterior pattern, treated with Xiang Ru San), but this formula treats the interior pattern.
解表化濕；和中理氣。平胃散的進階版，專門處理特殊飲食或外來邪氣造成的濕氣（裡症），嘔吐跟腹瀉很像暑熱（是表症，用香薷散），但此方是治療裡症。
主治
Indication
Exterior wind-cold combined with interior dampness retention in the Spleen and Stomach.
外感風寒兼脾胃濕滯。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Abdominal pain
  • Vomiting and diarrhea
  • Headache
  • Aversion to cold
  • Greasy, whitish tongue coating
  • Superficial or soft pulse

Compare formula: Xiang Ru San — treats summer-heat, an exterior-releasing herb.

  • 腹痛
  • 嘔吐腹瀉
  • 頭痛
  • 惡寒
  • 舌苔白膩
  • 脈浮或濡

比較方劑：香薷散——治暑熱，是解表藥。

Wu Ling San
五苓散【水腫】
Five-Ingredient Formula with Poria
組成
Ingredients
  • Diuresis — Poria (Fuling) 9 g; Umbellate Pore-Fungus (Zhuling) 9 g; Alismatis Rhizome (Zexie) 15 g (the "urination combo": Fuling + Zhuling + Zexie)
  • White Atractylodes (Baizhu) 9 g — tonifies the Spleen (dampness); Cinnamon Twigs (Guizhi) 6 g — warms yang (dispersing)
  • 利水 — 茯苓 9 g；豬苓 9 g；澤瀉 15 g（排尿組合：茯苓、豬苓、澤瀉）
  • 白朮 9 g——健脾（化濕）；桂枝 6 g——暖陽（發散）
功效
Functions
Induce diuresis and eliminate dampness; warm yang and promote qi transformation. (Taiyang channel level, without sweating → Ma Huang Tang; Taiyang bladder level — dampness accumulation, disordered bladder qi transformation, unable to urinate. Wu Ling San is milder, mainly treats edema; Zhu Ling Tang is cooler, treats Taiyang excess. Edema can stem from Spleen or Kidney deficiency. Zhen Wu Tang is a modified variant of Wu Ling San.) Drains dampness through urination; warms yang to transform qi.
利水滲濕，溫陽化氣。太陽經：不出汗，用麻黃湯；太陽腑：濕氣累積、膀胱氣化失常，無法排尿。五苓散主要治水腫較溫和，豬苓湯較涼，治太陽症實證。水腫：脾虛、腎虛。真武湯從五苓散變方而來。
主治
Indication
Taiyang syndrome of fluid retention in the bladder, marked by edema, diarrhea, dysuria, a pale tongue with a glossy whitish coating, and a soft or floating pulse.
太陽蓄水證——水停膀胱，水腫、腹瀉、排尿困難、舌淡苔白滑、脈濡或浮。
📝 課堂筆記（原文僅中文）
小柴胡湯（見 13・和解劑）——少陽症，往來寒熱、口苦、脈弦。

Zhu Ling Tang
豬苓湯【積水伴隨熱】
Polyporus Decoction
組成

Wu Pi San
五皮散【脾氣虛＋滯長期水腫】
Five Peels Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Poria Peel (Fulingpi)
  • Citrus Peel (Chenpi)
  • Areca Peel (Dafupi)
  • Ginger Peel (Jiangpi)
  • Mulberry Bark (Sangbaipi)
  • 茯苓皮
  • 陳皮
  • 大腹皮
  • 生薑皮
  • 桑白皮
功效
Functions
Per the "Four Fluid-Retention Patterns" (Si Yin) from the Jin Gui Yao Lue: Phlegm-fluid, Suspended fluid, Overflowing fluid, Supported fluid. Xiao Qing Long Tang: phlegm-fluid affecting the upper Jiao — Supported fluid. (Formula for the sides of the body — Suspended fluid.) Wu Pi San: phlegm-fluid affecting the superficial layer of the limbs (fluid retained in the body surface and subcutaneous tissue) — Overflowing fluid.

Phlegm-fluid (Ling Gui Zhu Gan Tang) — in the middle Jiao, water moving through the intestines with a gurgling sound. Suspended fluid — fluid flows to the sides of the body, coughing and spitting cause pain. Overflowing fluid (Wu Pi San) — fluid flows into the limbs; sweating should occur but does not, and the body aches. Supported fluid (Ling Gan Wu Wei Jiang Xin Tang, Xiao Qing Long Tang) — in the upper Jiao, cough and dyspnea preventing lying flat, with a swollen appearance.
《金匱要略》四飲：痰飲、懸飲、溢飲、支飲。小青龍湯：痰飲影響上焦，屬支飲。（治身側的方劑）：痰飲影響身側，屬懸飲。五皮散：痰飲影響表層四肢（水液滯留於體表及皮下組織），屬溢飲。

痰飲（苓桂朮甘湯）：在中焦、水走腸間，瀝瀝有聲謂之。懸飲：飲後水流在脅下，咳唾引痛，謂之懸飲。溢飲（五皮散）：飲水流行，歸於四肢，當汗出而不汗出，身體疼痛，謂之溢飲。支飲（苓甘五味薑辛湯、小青龍湯）：在上焦、咳逆倚息，短氣不得臥，其形如腫，謂之支飲。
主治
Indication
Chronic general edema in the body due to Spleen qi deficiency and stagnation.
因脾氣虛＋滯造成的長期全身水腫。
臨床表現
Manifestation
（原始資料此欄留空；典型表現為肢體水腫、皮膚繃緊發亮、小便短少）
（原始資料此欄留空；典型表現為肢體水腫、皮膚繃緊發亮、小便短少）

Ling Gui Zhu Gan Tang
苓桂朮甘湯（溫脾陽化胸口中焦水氣）
Poria, Cinnamon Twig, Atractylodes and Licorice Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Poria (Fuling) 12 g
  • Cinnamon Twigs (Guizhi) 9 g — warms
  • White Atractylodes (Baizhu) 6 g — tonifies the Spleen, helps drain dampness
  • Licorice (Gancao) 6 g
  • 茯苓 12 g
  • 桂枝 9 g——溫暖
  • 白朮 6 g——健脾，幫助排濕
  • 甘草 6 g
功效
Functions
Warm Spleen yang and resolve phlegm-fluid. (Caused by insufficient yang qi — thick tongue coating — water accumulates in the middle Jiao and chest. Difference from Wu Ling San: Wu Ling San is used purely to drain water and can treat edema.)
溫脾陽、化痰飲。因陽氣不足導致（舌苔厚），水積在中焦跟胸。跟五苓散差異：五苓散是用於單純排水可以治水腫。
📝 課堂筆記（原文僅中文）
痰飲（苓桂朮甘湯）：在中焦、水走腸間，瀝瀝有聲謂之。懸飲（十棗湯、滾痰丸）：飲後水流在脅下，咳唾引痛。溢飲（五皮散）：飲水流行歸於四肢，當汗出而不汗出，身體疼痛。支飲（苓甘五味薑辛湯、小青龍湯）：在上焦、咳逆倚息，短氣不得臥，其形如腫。
主治
Indication
Treats phlegm-fluid disease — from insufficient middle yang, the Spleen's transportive function failing, qi unable to transform water, accumulating as dampness; treat by warming and transforming to drain water. (Phlegm-fluid can affect the Heart-spirit.)
治痰飲病，乃中陽不足，脾運失職，氣不化水，聚濕而成，故治宜溫化利水。痰飲可以影響到心神。
📝 課堂筆記（原文僅中文）
小青龍湯（見第10組）。苓甘五味薑辛湯：水狀寒飲在胸口肺部，可以聽到水聲或氣泡聲。苓桂朮甘湯：治痰飲，痰濕累積在中焦。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Phlegm-fluid syndrome
  • Chest and abdominal fullness
  • Vertigo
  • Palpitations
  • Shortness of breath with cough
  • Glossy, whitish coating (yang deficiency)
  • Slippery pulse
  • 痰飲證
  • 胸腹脹滿
  • 眩暈
  • 心悸

  • 舌苔白滑（陽虛）
  • 脈滑

Zhen Wu Tang
真武湯（腎陽虛四肢水腫）
True Warrior Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • [Si Ni Tang — warm yang] — Prepared Aconite Root (Fuzi) 9 g; Fresh Ginger (Shengjiang) 9 g

Functions
Warm Kidney yang and eliminate water. (Leans toward a Kidney-yang / Shaoyin pattern; the whole body is cold, not just the hands and feet, with edema starting from the feet. A modified variant of Si Ni Tang.) Kidney yang declining, Spleen losing its transportive function — warm the Kidney yang, disinhibit water-qi.
溫腎陽、行水。偏向腎陽少陰症，全身都冷，不只手腳冷，從腳開始的水腫；是四逆湯的變方。腎陽衰微，脾失健運，溫腎陽，利水氣。
主治
Indication

因腎陽虛所致的水腫。

Functions
Dispel wind, cold, and dampness; relieve arthralgia; tonify the Liver and Kidney; replenish qi and blood. (Duhuo is heavier, so it can travel downward and treat lower-body problems.)
祛風寒濕、止痹痛、補肝腎、益氣血。獨活較重，所以可以往下走，治療下半身問題。

Bi-syndrome in the lower back with deficiency of both the Liver and Kidney and insufficient qi and blood. (A deficiency-type Bi-syndrome, presenting as lower-back pain.)

📝 課堂筆記（原文僅中文）

  • 腰膝疼痛
  • 關節活動受限或麻木
  • 惡寒喜溫
  • 心悸氣短
  • 舌淡苔白
  • 脈細弱
加減
Modification
  • Severe cold invasion → add Fuzi
  • Severe dampness → add Fangji, Cangzhu
  • Severe pain → add Dilong, Honghua
  • Mild qi and blood deficiency → omit Renshen, Gandihuang, Baishao
  • 寒邪重：加附子
  • 濕重：加防己、蒼朮
  • 痛甚：加地龍、紅花
  • 氣血虛輕微：去人蔘、乾地黃、白芍
Qiang Huo Sheng Shi Tang
羌活勝濕湯【上背痛外感風濕表症】
