08 · Phlegm 化痰劑

▍痰證病機 Pathogenesis of Phlegm

English中文
Damp-Phlegm — phlegm-dampness, slightly thicker/stickier than cold-fluid's watery phlegm.
濕痰——痰濕，比寒飲水狀痰再稠狀一點。
Heat-Phlegm — yellow phlegm.
熱痰——黃痰。
Cold-Phlegm — very watery phlegm.
寒痰——很水狀的痰。
Dry-Phlegm — very sticky/thick phlegm, sore throat.
燥痰——很黏稠的痰、喉嚨痛。
Wind-Phlegm — two types: (1) itchy, sensitive throat without much phlegm; (2) phlegm moving inward, causing epilepsy/clouded consciousness.
風痰——兩種：一種是喉嚨癢且敏感、沒有太多痰；另一種是痰往內走，導致癲癇、意識不清楚。

▍痰證病機對應方劑 Matching Formulas with Pathogenesis of Phlegm

English中文
Damp-Phlegm — Er Chen Tang, Wen Dan Tang
濕痰——二陳湯、溫膽湯
Heat-Phlegm — Qing Qi Hua Tan (Wan), Xiao Xian Xiong Tang
熱痰——清氣化痰丸、小陷胸湯
Cold-Phlegm — Gun Tan Wan (Wan Clear Qi... — see individual card for exact indication), San Zi Yang Qin Tang
寒痰——苓甘五味薑辛湯、三子養親湯
Dry-Phlegm — Bei Mu Gua Lou San
燥痰——貝母瓜蔞散
Wind-Phlegm — Ban Xia Bai Zhu Tian Ma Tang, Ding Xian Wan, Zhi Sou San
風痰——半夏白朮天麻湯、定癇丸、止嗽散

⚠ 原稿在「Cold Phlegm」對應方劑處寫的是「滾痰湯 Gun Tan Wan」與「三子養親湯」，但滾痰湯實際上根據其方劑卡片內容（見下方）屬痰熱擾神證，與 Heat-Phlegm 更相符；此處依原稿逐字呈現，並在下方滾痰湯卡片中列出其實際的熱痰病機供對照。

▍風痰方劑小結 Summary for Wind-Phlegm

English中文
Ban Xia Bai Zhu Tian Ma Tang: dizziness due to an upward attack of wind-phlegm.
半夏白朮天麻湯：因痰而引起暈眩。
Zhi Sou San: cough with itchy throat, a remaining symptom of an exterior syndrome.
止嗽散：治感冒後期的喉嚨癢。
Ding Xian Wan: specifically for children's epilepsy due to wind-phlegm attack.
定癇丸：兒童癲癇專用。

▍化痰常用藥 Herbs for Resolving Phlegm

溫肺燥濕化痰（濕痰／寒痰）English中文
Pinellia Tuber (Banxia); Arisaema Tuber (Tiannanxing); White Mustard Seed (Baijiezi)
半夏；天南星；白芥子
降氣化痰，降逆止嘔English中文
Platycodon Root (Jiegeng); Peucedanum Root (Qianhu); Inula Flower (Xuanfuhua)
桔梗；前胡；旋覆花

Tendrilled Fritillary Bulb (Beimu); Trichosanthes Fruit (Gualou); Bamboo Shavings (Zhuru)
貝母；栝蔞；竹茹
潤肺下氣，化痰止咳English中文
Apricot Seed (Xingren); Stemona Root (Baibu); Aster Root (Ziwan)
杏仁；百部；紫菀
瀉肺水飲痰火而平喘咳English中文
Perilla Seed (Suzi); Lepidium Seed (Tinglizi)
紫蘇子；葶藶子
▍EXAM FOCUS — 本組出題重點（11方，分五類）
  • 本組跟前面「痰證病機」表直接對應，考試常直接考「某症狀屬於哪一類痰證」。
  • 二陳湯（半夏＋陳皮＋茯苓＝去痰三寶）是全書化痰的基礎方，溫膽湯（去烏梅、加枳實竹茹）與其一脈相承，考試常考「溫膽湯是哪個方變化來的」。
  • 平胃散 vs 二陳湯：平胃散治濕（無痰），二陳湯治因濕而生的痰，兩方都作用於脾——常考鑑別。
  • 小陷胸湯 vs 半夏瀉心湯：兩者都治心下痞，但小陷胸湯按之則痛（結胸），半夏瀉心湯按之不痛（痞證），是經典鑑別。
  • 四飲定位再次出現於苓甘五味薑辛湯（支飲，上焦，水聲/氣泡聲明顯）——與苓桂朮甘湯（痰飲，中焦）、五皮散（溢飲，四肢體表）互相對照。
  • 三子養親湯三味藥各自的「方向」是常考重點：白芥子（化痰，橫向）、紫蘇子（降氣走肺，向下）、萊菔子（消食走胃，向下）。
  • 暈眩方劑總鑑別（跨組）：風痰（半夏白朮天麻湯）／內風（川芎茶調散）／肝陽上亢（天麻鉤藤飲）／血虛（四物湯、八珍湯）——四種暈眩病因需清楚區分。
Er Chen Tang
二陳湯【最基本的痰濕方】
Decoction of Two Aged Drugs
組成
Ingredients
  • [Combo for resolving phlegm] — Pinellia Tuber (Banxia) 15 g (transforms phlegm); Citrus Peel (Chenpi) 15 g (moves Spleen qi); Poria (Fuling) 9 g (removes water)
  • Fresh Ginger (Shengjiang) 3 g; Black Plum (Wumei) 1 piece — moves qi both outward and inward; Licorice (Gancao) 5 g

The "three treasures for removing phlegm": Banxia + Chenpi + Fuling. Banxia and Chenpi are better the longer they have been aged.

  • 【去痰組合】— 半夏 15 g（轉化痰）；陳皮 15 g（行脾氣）；茯苓 9 g（去水）
  • 生薑 3 g；烏梅 1 枚——讓氣往外跟往內；甘草 5 g

去痰三寶的組合：半夏＋陳皮＋茯苓。半夏跟陳皮是放越久越好，陳年的。

功效
Functions
Dry dampness and resolve phlegm; regulate qi circulation and harmonize the middle Jiao. (The most basic phlegm-resolving formula.)
燥濕化痰、理氣和中。最基本的去痰方。痰濕：比寒飲水狀痰再稠狀一點。
📝 課堂筆記（原文僅中文）
平胃散：最基本的濕氣藥方，沒有痰，作用在脾。二陳湯：最基本的痰藥方，因濕氣而起的痰，作用在脾。
主治
Indication
Cough or vomiting due to damp-phlegm.
因濕痰所致的咳嗽或嘔吐。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Cough with large amounts of whitish sputum, easy to expectorate
  • Fullness and choking sensation in the chest
  • Poor appetite with tastelessness
  • Nausea and vomiting
  • Lassitude
  • Dizziness and palpitations
  • Greasy, whitish tongue coating
  • Smooth pulse
  • 咳嗽痰多色白，易咳出
  • 胸悶、有窒息感
  • 食欲不振、味覺差
  • 噁心嘔吐
  • 倦怠
  • 眩暈心悸
  • 舌苔白膩
  • 脈滑
加減
Modification
  • Severe dizziness → add Tianma
  • Cold manifestations → add Ganjiang, Xixin
  • Heat manifestations (with large amounts of phlegm + Chenpi/Banxia) → add Huangqin, Gualou
  • Prolonged, severe damp-phlegm → add Tiannanxing, Zhishi
  • Shen affected → add Shichangpu, Zhuru, Renshen
  • Insomnia with timidity/nervousness → replace Wumei with Zhishi and Zhuru, becoming Wen Dan Tang
  • 頭暈：加天麻
  • 寒痰：加乾薑、細辛
  • 熱痰（大量痰＋陳皮半夏）：加黃芩、栝樓
  • 長期痰濕：加天南星、枳實
  • 擾神：加石菖蒲、竹茹、人蔘
  • 失眠：去烏梅，加枳實、竹茹＝溫膽湯

Functions
For heat-phlegm accumulated in the chest — an excess pattern, pain on pressing below the Heart. Treats phlegm-heat binding in the chest: unresolved exterior Cold-damage pattern, mistreated so that pathogenic heat sinks inward, binding with phlegm, or warm-heat pathogens scorching fluids into phlegm.
痰熱積在胸口，實證，按心下會疼痛。治痰熱結胸之證：傷寒表證未解，誤治後邪熱內陷，以致痰熱互結，或溫熱之邪氣煎熬津液而成痰。

Gun Tan Wan
滾痰湯【痰熱在大腦擾神】
Vaporize Phlegm Pill
組成
Ingredients
  • Lapis Micae (Mengshi) — travels to the brain, vaporizes heat-phlegm
  • Aquilaria Sinensis (Chenxiang) — travels to the brain, rapidly sends qi downward
  • Scutellaria Root (Huangqin) — clears heat from the upper Jiao
  • Rhubarb (Dahuang) — clears away heat
  • 礞石——走大腦，蒸化熱痰
  • 沉香——走大腦，速降下氣
  • 黃芩——清上焦熱
  • 大黃——清熱瀉下
功效
Functions
Purge fire and vaporize chronic phlegm. (Heat-phlegm in the brain disturbing the spirit, causing mental disorders; excess heat with old phlegm can manifest as mania. Yin-cold type phlegm: quiet, yellow phlegm. Yang-heat type phlegm: hot phlegm, restless/noisy. Cause: Liver qi stagnation + blood stasis.)
瀉火逐痰。痰熱在大腦擾神，造成精神問題。實熱老痰，發為癲狂。痰陰寒型：安靜、黃痰。痰陽熱型：熱痰、聒噪。原因：肝氣滯＋血瘀。
主治
Indication
Mental disorders due to heat-phlegm. Caution: should be used carefully in cases that are not truly excess-heat old-phlegm, in weak/deficient people, and in pregnant women.
因痰熱所致的精神疾患。⚠ 非實熱老痰、虛人、孕婦等，均應慎用。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Schizophrenia-type symptoms
  • Epilepsy
  • Dizziness, tinnitus
  • Thick, yellow tongue coating
  • Slippery, rapid, forceful pulse
  • 精神分裂症
  • 癲癇

二陳湯
Er Chen Tang		濕痰（基礎方）脾濕生痰燥濕化痰、理氣和中咳痰量多色白、胸悶、苔白膩去痰三寶：半夏＋陳皮＋茯苓
溫膽湯
Wen Dan Tang		膽胃不和，痰濁內擾痰濁擾膽，膽失決斷理氣化痰、和胃利膽虛煩失眠、驚悸不寧、脈弦滑二陳湯去烏梅、加枳實竹茹而成
清氣化痰湯
Qing Qi Hua Tan		痰熱壅肺（內感）痰熱互結於肺清熱化痰、理氣止咳咳痰黃稠難咯、苔黃膩、脈滑數與定喘湯對比：內感／外感
小陷胸湯
Xiao Xian Xiong Tang		痰熱結胸（實證）痰熱互結於胸脘清熱化痰、寬胸散結心下按之則痛、痰黃稠、脈滑數與半夏瀉心湯對比：按之痛／不痛
滾痰湯
Gun Tan Wan		實熱老痰擾神痰火壅盛，上蒙心神瀉火逐痰癲狂癲癇、苔黃厚、脈滑數有力虛人、孕婦慎用；藥性峻猛
貝母瓜蔞散
Bei Mu Gua Lou San		肺燥兼熱（慢性乾咳）燥熱傷肺，津液不布潤肺清熱、理氣化痰咳嗽少痰、咽乾哽痛與清燥救肺湯對比：無陰虛象
苓甘五味薑辛湯
Ling Gan Wu Wei Jiang Xin Tang		支飲（上焦寒飲）肺寒留飲溫肺化飲咳痰稀白量多、胸滿、苔白滑對應「支飲」；三寶：乾薑細辛五味子
三子養親湯
San Zi Yang Qin Tang		寒痰兼食滯（老人）痰壅氣逆兼食積降氣化痰、消食導滯老人久咳痰多、胸悶、消化不良三子各走一方：肺／胃／橫散
半夏白朮天麻湯
Ban Xia Bai Zhu Tian Ma Tang		風痰上擾（暈眩）脾濕生痰，痰阻清陽燥濕化痰、平肝熄風眩暈頭痛、噁心、苔白膩、脈弦滑二陳湯之變方；暈眩四型之一
定癇丸
Ding Xian Wan		小兒風痰癲癇風痰蒙蔽心竅豁痰開竅、熄風鎮驚兒童抽搐、意識喪失藥味繁多，兒童癲癇專方
止嗽散
Zhi Sou San		外感風邪咳嗽（感冒後期）風邪未淨，肺氣不宣疏風宣肺、止咳化痰喉癢咳嗽、惡風、苔薄白與桑菊飲對比：感冒後期／初期

09 · Digestion 消食劑

▍消食方劑速記 Formulas for Digestion

English中文
Bao He Wan — food stagnation in the Stomach.
保和丸——胃食滯。
Zhi Shi Dao Zhi Wan — food stagnation in the Intestine.
枳實導滯丸——腸食滯。
Jian Pi Wan — food stagnation due to Spleen deficiency (eating too little, a deficiency pattern).
健脾丸——脾氣虛、吃過少的虛症。

▍食滯病機 Pathogenesis of Food Stagnation

English中文
Excess: food retention
實：食物滯留
Deficiency: Spleen deficiency
虛：脾虛

▍消食常用藥 Herbs for Digestion

English中文
Hawthorn Fruit (Shanzha); Medicated Leaven (Shenqu); Germinated Barley (Maiya); Radish Seed (Laifuzi); Chicken Gizzard Skin (Jineijin)
山楂；神麯；麥芽；萊菔子；雞內金
▍EXAM FOCUS — 本組出題重點
  • 三方以虛實＋部位鑑別：保和丸（實，胃，單純食滯無腹瀉）／枳實導滯丸（實，腸，兼濕熱，有腹瀉或便秘）／健脾丸（虛，脾氣虛，吃過少）。
  • 保和丸內含二陳湯（陳皮、半夏、茯苓）── 常考「保和丸含哪個基礎方」。
  • 枳實導滯丸含承氣湯的影子（大黃、枳實）── 考試常問「此方以何方為基礎再加清熱燥濕藥」。
  • 健脾丸是以四君子湯為底的常用中成藥，加消食藥（陳皮、山楂、麥芽、神麯）與止瀉藥（山藥、肉豆蔻、黃連）；若無濕熱可去黃連，若寒可加乾薑、附子。
Bao He Wan
保和丸【胃食滯】
Preserve Harmony Pill
組成
Ingredients
  • Hawthorn Fruit (Shanzha), Medicated Leaven (Shenqu), Radish Seed (Laifuzi) — each 9 g
  • Citrus Peel (Chenpi), Pinellia Tuber (Banxia), Poria (Fuling) — each 9 g (Er Chen Tang)
  • Forsythia Fruit (Lianqiao) 3 g
  • 山楂、神麯、萊菔子——各 9 g
  • 陳皮、半夏、茯苓——各 9 g（二陳湯）
  • 連翹 3 g
功效
Functions
Improve digestion and regulate the Stomach. (For overeating with food stagnation in the Stomach, without diarrhea.)
消食和胃。吃過多，胃食滯，沒有腹瀉。
主治
Indication
Food stagnation due to overeating.
因暴飲暴食所致的食滯。
臨床表現
Manifestation
（原始資料此欄留空；典型表現為脘腹脹滿、噯腐吞酸、不欲飲食）
（原始資料此欄留空；典型表現為脘腹脹滿、噯腐吞酸、不欲飲食）
加減

（原始資料未列加減內容）
（原始資料未列加減內容）

Unripe Bitter Orange Pill to Guide Out Stagnation

  • 大黃、枳實——各 9 g（承氣湯底）
  • 黃連、黃芩——各 9 g（濕熱）
  • 茯苓、澤瀉——各 6 g（去濕）
  • 白朮、神麯——各 6 g（補）
功效
Functions
Eliminate heat-dampness from the intestine. (Contains the "shadow" of Cheng Qi Tang; for overeating with food stagnation in the Intestine, may present with diarrhea or constipation.)
清利腸道濕熱。有承氣湯的影子在裡面。吃過多，腸食滯，有腹瀉或便秘。
主治

因濕熱實證所致的食滯。

  • Abdominal fullness

  • 腹瀉或便秘

  • 苔黃膩
  • 脈沉有力

Jian Pi Wan
健脾丸
Strengthen the Spleen Pill
組成
Ingredients
  • [Si Junzi Tang / "Six Gentlemen"] — each 15 g — tonifies the Spleen
  • Citrus Peel (Chenpi), Hawthorn Fruit (Shanzha), Germinated Barley (Maiya), Medicated Leaven (Shenqu) — each 9 g — aids digestion
  • Chinese Yam (Shanyao), Nutmeg (Roudoukou), Coptis (Huanglian) — each 6 g — for diarrhea

This is a commonly used patent medicine based on Si Junzi Tang (Four Gentlemen Decoction).

  • 【六君子湯類】— 各 15 g——補脾
  • 陳皮、山楂、麥芽、神麯——各 9 g——助消化
  • 山藥、肉豆蔻、黃連——各 6 g——治腹瀉

這是常用的中成藥，以四君子湯為基礎方。

功效
Functions
Tonify the Spleen to help digestion. (Spleen qi deficiency, eating too little — a deficiency pattern.)
補脾助消化。脾氣虛、吃過少，是虛症。
主治
Indication
Food stagnation due to Spleen qi deficiency.
因脾氣虛所致的食滯。
臨床表現
Manifestation
（原始資料此欄留空；典型表現為食少難消、脘腹痞悶、大便溏薄）
（原始資料此欄留空；典型表現為食少難消、脘腹痞悶、大便溏薄）
加減
Modification
  • Remove Huanglian if there is no damp-heat
  • Add Ganjiang, Fuzi in case of cold
  • 無濕熱者：去黃連
  • 寒證：加乾薑、附子

Bao He Wan		胃食滯（實）暴飲暴食，食積胃脘消食和胃脘腹脹滿、噯腐吞酸、無腹瀉含二陳湯（陳皮半夏茯苓）

10 · Exterior Wind-Cold 辛溫解表劑

▍太陽症總覽 Taiyang Syndrome Overview

English中文
Taiyang syndrome: aversion to cold, headache and neck pain, floating pulse.
太陽症：畏寒、頭痛、後脖子痛、脈浮。
→ Ma Huang Tang — Taiyang Cold syndrome: aversion to cold, body aches, no sweating, tense pulse = excess cold (cold causes contraction). Fever without sweating, cough, asthma.
→ 麻黃湯——太陽寒症：惡寒、身痛、無汗、脈緊＝實寒（寒會緊縮）。發熱無汗、咳嗽、氣喘。
→ Gui Zhi Tang — Taiyang Wind syndrome: aversion to wind, headache, sweating, moderate pulse (wind moves around, more headache).
→ 桂枝湯——太陽風症：惡風、頭痛、有汗、脈緩（風到處跑，多頭痛）。
Taiyang Cold: Ma Huang Tang (severe cough, no pain relief, exterior wind-cold excess, no sweating); Xiao Qing Long Tang (excess body fluid including phlegm); Jiu Wei Qiang Huo Tang (various pains, severe headache/body ache/neck pain).
太陽寒：麻黃湯（嚴重咳嗽，沒有止痛，外感風寒實症，無出汗）；小青龍湯（處理體內過多水份包含痰）；九味羌活湯（各種疼痛、嚴重頭痛、身體痛、脖子痛）。
Taiyang Wind: Gui Zhi Tang (has sweating, exterior wind-cold deficiency).
太陽風：桂枝湯（有出汗、外感風寒虛症）。
Exterior Wind-Cold syndrome → chill and fever (all 4 formulas can); coughing → Ma Huang Tang; pain/headache → Jiu Wei Qiang Huo Tang; runny nose/phlegm → Xiao Qing Long Tang.
外感風寒證 → 惡寒發熱（四方皆可）；咳嗽 → 麻黃湯；疼痛頭痛 → 九味羌活湯；流鼻涕、有痰 → 小青龍湯。

▍三陽三陰病位總覽（老師補充） Six-Channel Overview

三陽（實）Three Yang (Excess)English中文
Progression: Taiyang → Yangming (Yangming-channel / Yangming-organ) → Shaoyang.
傳變：太陽→陽明（陽明經／陽明腑）→少陽。
Taiyang divides into 2 types: Taiyang-Cold / Taiyang-Wind. Distinguishing point: presence of sweating; Cold must have cough. The 4 major Cold symptoms: fever & chill, cough, no sweating, headache.
太陽分兩類：太陽寒／太陽風。分辨要點：是否流汗，寒證必須有咳嗽。寒證四大症狀：發熱惡寒、咳嗽、無汗、頭痛。
Cold: Ma Huang Tang (cough, difficulty breathing); Xiao Qing Long Tang (phlegm-damp accumulation); Jiu Wei Qiang Huo Tang (occipital pain). Wind: Gui Zhi Tang (no sweating [sic, per original — see note below]).
寒：麻黃湯（咳嗽，難呼吸）、小青龍湯（痰濕累積）、九味羌活湯（後腦痛）。風：桂枝湯（原文誤植「無汗」，實際上桂枝湯證的辨證重點是「有汗」，詳見下方桂枝湯卡片）。
Yangming divides into 2: Yangming-channel / Yangming-organ. Yangming-channel "4 BIGs": high fever, sweating, thirst, surging pulse (last stage: Zhu Ye Shi Gao Tang). Yangming-organ main symptoms: fullness, hardness, dryness, and excess (Dahuang, Mangxiao, Houpo, Zhishi) — constipation.
陽明分兩類：陽明經／陽明腑。陽明經「四大」：高燒、發汗、口渴、洪脈（最後階段：竹葉石膏湯）。陽明腑主要症狀：痞滿燥實（大黃、芒硝、厚朴、枳實）——便祕。
Yangming-channel 4 BIGs → Bai Hu Tang (named "White" because it contains polished rice; adding Cangzhu can treat joint pain). Yangming-organ constipation → Da Cheng Qi Tang.
陽明經四大 → 白虎湯（因有粳米所以稱白，加蒼朮可治關節痛）。陽明腑便祕 → 大承氣湯。
Shaoyang is "between exterior and interior." Main symptoms: alternating chills and fever, bitter mouth, restlessness and chest oppression, wiry pulse (the final stage of Shaoyang can damage the Spleen). Main formula: Xiao Chai Hu Tang. Shaoyang with damp-heat jaundice: Hao Qin Qing Dan Tang.
少陽在表裡之間。主要症狀：往來寒熱、口苦、煩躁胸悶、脈弦（最後一階的少陽症會傷到脾）。主要方劑：小柴胡湯。少陽如有濕熱黃疸：蒿芩清膽湯。
三陰（虛）Three Yin (Deficiency)English中文
Progression: Taiyin → Jueyin → Shaoyin.
傳變：太陰→厥陰→少陰。
Taiyin (Lung, Spleen) — basically a Spleen-deficiency problem. Stage 1: abdominal pain first, relieved by pressure or warmth. Stage 2: diarrhea → Li Zhong Tang (most similar to Si Junzi Tang); Xiao Jian Zhong Tang (Gui Zhi Tang with doubled Baishao); Da Jian Zhong Tang (uses Chuanjiao).
太陰（肺、脾）：基本上是脾虛問題。第一階段：先肚子痛，肚子的寒可以因為按壓或熱減緩。第二階段：腹瀉——理中湯（跟四君子湯最像）、小建中湯（桂枝湯的白芍加倍）、大建中湯（用川椒）。
Jueyin (Liver, Pericardium) — cold accumulated at the Jueyin level. Main symptoms: vertex headache + vomiting. Wu Zhu Yu Tang.
厥陰（肝、心包）：寒冷積在厥陰。主要症狀：頭頂痛＋嘔吐。吳茱萸湯。
Shaoyin (Heart, Kidney) — the deepest level, near death. Main symptoms: whole-body cold, feeling cold, yang collapse, loss of consciousness. Main herb/formula: Si Ni Tang (similar name to Si Ni San but different effect — both treat cold limbs).
少陰（心、腎）：最深層的，瀕死。主要症狀：全身寒冷、感覺寒冷、陽脫、失去意識。主要用藥：四逆湯（跟四逆散名稱相似但藥效不同，都治四肢冷）。

▍解表常用藥 Herbs for Releasing the Exterior

解表 Release ExteriorEnglish中文
Ephedra (Mahuang) — pungent, bitter; enters the Lung; can disperse excess, stop cough, descend Lung qi, induce sweating.
麻黃——辛苦，走肺，可以散實，止咳降肺氣，發汗。
Cinnamon Twigs (Guizhi) — pungent, sweet; enters Heart and Lung; harmonizes Ying and Wei, stops chest pain.
桂枝——辛甘，走心肺，可以調和營衛，止胸痛。
Elsholtzia (Xiangru) — functions similarly to Mahuang.
香薷——作用類似麻黃。
辛溫解表 Pungent-Warm ReleaseEnglish中文
Fresh Ginger (Shengjiang) — pungent-warm release, enters the Lung.
生薑——辛溫解表，走肺。
Schizonepeta (Jingjie) — pungent-warm release, dispels wind and relieves itching.
荊芥——辛溫解表、祛風止癢。
去濕止痛 Remove Dampness, Stop PainEnglish中文
Ledebouriella Root (Fangfeng) — enters the Shaoyang Gallbladder channel; for exterior invasion, relieves itching.
防風——走少陽膽經，都是外感，止癢。
Notopterygium Root (Qianghuo) — enters the Taiyang channel.
羌活——走太陽經。
Dahurian Angelica Root (Baizhi) — enters the Yangming channel, frontal headache.
白芷——走陽明經、額頭痛。
芳香通竅止痛English中文
Dahurian Angelica Root (Baizhi) — enters Yangming, frontal headache.
白芷——走陽明經、額頭痛。
Asarum (Xixin) — enters the Shaoyin channel. (Original text mislabels this as "Magnolia Flower (Xinyi)" — kept as-is; Xixin is the correct herb per its function description.)
細辛——走少陰經。（原文此處誤植為「細辛(Magnolia flower/Xinyi)」，依功能描述應為細辛，原文用字保留供參考）
▍EXAM FOCUS — 本組出題重點
  • 麻黃湯 vs 桂枝湯：最核心的鑑別點是「有無出汗」——麻黃湯無汗（實證，需要咳嗽才可用），桂枝湯有汗（虛證，調和營衛）。這是全書最常考的鑑別對之一。
  • 麻黃湯因發汗力強，血虛的人不能用（血汗同源）；一定要有咳嗽才可用麻黃湯。
  • 桂枝湯服法特殊：喝了熱粥後去睡覺可以助發汗——考試常問「服藥前流汗是病機、服藥＋粥後流汗是治療效果」的差異。
  • 小青龍湯的「排水三寶」：細辛、乾薑、五味子，是常考的固定組合。
  • 九味羌活湯依經絡病位選藥：三陽用羌活（太陽）、白芷（陽明）、防風（少陽）；三陰用蒼朮（太陰）、川芎（厥陰肝）、細辛（少陰肺，原文標注）——常考「哪味藥對應哪一經」。
  • 麻黃湯三個變方：三拗湯（去桂枝，治單純咳喘無寒熱交雜）、大青龍湯（加石膏，治高燒畏寒兼內熱）——是常考變方系列。
Ma Huang Tang
麻黃湯
Ephedra Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Ephedra (Mahuang) 9 g
  • Cinnamon Twigs (Guizhi) 6 g
  • Apricot Seed (Xingren) 9 g — stops cough
  • Baked Licorice (Zhigancao) 3 g
  • 麻黃 9 g
  • 桂枝 6 g
  • 杏仁 9 g——止咳
  • 炙甘草 3 g
功效
Functions
Promote sweating and release the exterior; promote the Lung's function of descending; relieve cough and asthma. Must have cough to use Ma Huang Tang; if there is no acute cough, do not use it. Because blood and sweat share the same source, those with blood deficiency cannot use Ma Huang Tang.
發汗解表；宣肺平喘。必須要有咳嗽才能用麻黃湯，如果沒有急性咳嗽不要用麻黃湯；因為血跟汗來源一樣，所以血虛的人不能用麻黃湯。
主治
Indication
Wind-cold exterior excess syndrome — treats "cold damaging the Ying," no sweating, alternating chills/fever, cough.
風寒表實證——治「寒傷營」、無汗、往來寒熱、咳嗽。

歌訣：麻黃湯中用桂枝，杏仁甘草四般施，發熱惡寒頭項痛，傷寒服此汗淋漓。治太陽病頭疼發熱、身疼腰痛、骨節疼痛、惡寒無汗而喘者，此方主之。

臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Aversion to cold
  • Fever
  • Headache
  • General body aches
  • Cough and dyspnea
  • No sweating
  • Thin-white coating
  • Superficial-tense pulse
  • 惡寒
  • 發熱
  • 頭痛
  • 全身痠痛
  • 咳嗽氣喘
  • 無汗
  • 苔薄白
  • 脈浮緊
加減
Modification
  • [San'ao Tang] — where the main issue is asthma with only mild aversion to cold, omit Guizhi, using only Mahuang and Xingren for asthma — a formula specially for relieving asthma
  • [Da Qing Long Tang] — serious aversion to cold with accompanying interior heat: add Gypsum (Shigao) to clear interior heat (if high fever, add cold-natured Shigao since Mahuang is warm)
  • Profuse phlegm — where cough with abundant white sputum is the main issue, replace Guizhi with Peucedanum Root (Qianhu)
  • Upper-body water retention — if accompanied by water retention on the upper body, add White Atractylodes (Baizhu)

Ma Huang Tang can add Banxia to address phlegm. Mahuang is toxic, so it should not be used unnecessarily (without sweating) — if a Gui Zhi Tang pattern person (with sweating) uses Ma Huang Tang, problems will arise.

  • 【三拗湯】：只有咳嗽但沒有冷熱交雜，可以移除桂枝，只需要麻黃跟杏仁處理咳嗽問題
  • 【大青龍湯】：高燒＋畏寒內熱，如果高燒，可加石膏（寒）（麻黃是溫性）
  • 多痰：咳嗽白痰多時把桂枝換成前胡
  • 上半身水腫：水份積在上半身加白朮

麻黃湯可加半夏處理痰的問題。麻黃有毒性所以非必要（沒有流汗）不要用，所以如果桂枝湯症有流汗的人使用麻黃湯會有問題。

Gui Zhi Tang
桂枝湯
Cinnamon Twig Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • [Guizhi + Baishao — herb-pair, harmonizes Ying and Wei] — Cinnamon Twigs (Guizhi) 9 g — tonifies the Lung, Wei-qi; White Peony Root (Baishao) 9 g — tonifies Ying-blood
  • Baked Licorice (Zhigancao) 6 g
  • [Shengjiang + Dazao — herb-pair] — Fresh Ginger (Shengjiang) 9 g — tonifies Wei, tonifies Lung qi; Jujube (Dazao) 3 pieces — tonifies blood
  • 【桂枝跟白芍對藥，調和營衛】— 桂枝 9 g——補肺、衛氣；白芍 9 g——補營血
  • 炙甘草 6 g
  • 【生薑跟大棗對藥】— 生薑 9 g——補衛、補肺氣；大棗 3 枚——補血
老師補充 ＆＆ 桂枝湯變方
小建中湯：白芍加倍＋麥芽糖。當歸四逆湯：加木通、細辛。黃耆桂枝五物湯：甘草換黃耆。
功效
Functions
Expel wind-cold from the exterior; harmonize Ying and Wei (Ying-blood is weak but Wei-qi is still strong). (Two herb-pairs — Guizhi/Baishao and Shengjiang/Dazao — both harmonize Ying and Wei. In the Shen Nong Ben Cao Jing, herbs are ranked high/middle/low class by toxicity — e.g. Ginseng is high-class, while Fuzi, despite its strong effect, is low-class because it is toxic.)
祛風散寒解表；調和營衛（營血弱但衛氣仍強）。兩個對藥（桂枝白芍＋生薑紅棗），同為調和營衛。神農本草經中分為上中下級的藥（以有沒有毒性來分界，例如人蔘為上級，附子因為有毒所以即便藥性強也是下級）。
主治
Indication
Wind-cold exterior deficiency syndrome, Ying/Wei disharmony — treats "wind damaging the Wei," with sweating. The most important criterion for Gui Zhi Tang is whether there is sweating — only use it if there IS sweating; if not, it cannot be used.
風寒表虛證，營衛不和——治「風傷衛」、有汗。桂枝湯最重要的判斷是有沒有流汗，有流汗才用桂枝湯，沒流汗不能用。

歌訣：桂枝湯治太陽風，芍藥甘草薑棗同。桂麻相合名各半，太陽如瘧此為功。治自汗惡風、頭疼體痛、發熱、脈浮緩，名曰中風。

臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Headache
  • Fever
  • Perspiration with aversion to wind
  • Thin, whitish coating
  • Superficial, moderate pulse
  • 頭痛
  • 發熱
  • 汗出惡風
  • 苔薄白
  • 脈浮緩
加減
Modification
  • Stiffness/pain of the neck and back → add Pueraria Root (Gegen)
  • Severe cough → add Magnolia Bark (Houpo), Apricot Seed (Xingren)
  • [Xiao Jian Zhong Tang] — if there is no Wei-level problem, just need to regulate Ying: for abdominal pain, double the Baishao and add honey to make Xiao Jian Zhong Tang
  • 後頸痛：加葛根
  • 咳嗽（嚴重）：加厚朴、杏仁
  • 【小建中湯】：如果沒有衛的問題，只要調營就好——腹痛：白芍加倍，加蜂蜜，成小建中湯
Xiao Qing Long Tang
小青龍湯
Minor Blue Dragon Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Ephedra (Mahuang) 9 g
  • [Guizhi + Baishao — herb-pair, harmonizes Ying and Wei] — Cinnamon Twigs (Guizhi) 6 g — unblocks yang, disperses cold; White Peony Root (Baishao) 9 g — nourishes Ying-blood, astringes yin
  • [The "three treasures" for draining water] — Asarum (Xixin) 3 g; Dried Ginger (Ganjiang) 3 g; Schisandra Fruit (Wuweizi) 3 g
  • Pinellia Tuber (Banxia) 9 g — drains phlegm; Licorice (Gancao) 6 g
  • 麻黃 9 g
  • 【桂枝＋白芍＝對藥，調和營衛】— 桂枝 6 g——通陽散寒；白芍 9 g——養營血、斂陰
  • 【排水三寶：細辛、乾薑、五味子】— 細辛 3 g；乾薑 3 g；五味子 3 g
  • 半夏 9 g——排痰；甘草 6 g
功效
Functions
Expel wind-cold from the exterior; warm the Lung to resolve retained fluid.
解表散寒；溫肺化飲。

歌訣：小青龍湯治水氣，咳嗽嘔噦渴利慰，薑桂麻黃芍藥甘，細辛半夏兼五味。治傷寒表不解，心下有水氣，乾嘔發熱而渴，或咳，或利，或噎，或小便不利少腹滿，或喘，此方主之。

主治
Indication
Exterior wind-cold and fluid retention — treats Taiyang pattern, moves water and induces sweating.
外感風寒兼水飲內停——治太陽證、行水發汗。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Aversion to cold
  • Fever without perspiration
  • Headache
  • Cough or asthma with profuse thin-white sputum
  • Inability to lie down
  • Or swollen extremities
  • Smooth, whitish tongue coating
  • Superficial pulse
  • 惡寒
  • 發熱無汗
  • 頭痛
  • 咳喘痰稀白量多
  • 不能平臥
  • 或肢體浮腫
  • 舌苔白滑
  • 脈浮
加減
Modification
  • Less severe exterior cold → omit Guizhi and replace raw Mahuang with honey-baked Mahuang
  • Complicated with thirst → remove Banxia, replace with Trichosanthes Root (Tianhuafen)
  • Heat signs such as red tongue and rapid pulse → add Gypsum (Shigao)
  • 外感風寒較輕：去桂枝，生麻黃改蜜炙麻黃
  • 口渴：去半夏，加天花粉
  • 熱象＋紅舌：加石膏
Jiu Wei Qiang Huo Tang
九味羌活湯
Nine-Herb Decoction with Notopterygium
組成
Ingredients
  • [For headache, various pains — Three Yang] — Notopterygium Root (Qianghuo) 5 g (Taiyang channel); Dahurian Angelica Root (Baizhi) 3 g (Yangming channel); Ledebouriella Root (Fangfeng) 5 g (Shaoyang channel)
  • [Three Yin] — Atractylodes Rhizome (Cangzhu) 5 g (Taiyin channel); Chuanxiong Rhizome (Chuanxiong) 3 g (Jueyin/Liver); Asarum (Xixin) 1 g (Shaoyin/Lung, per original notes)
  • Fresh Rehmannia Root (Shengdihuang) 3 g — a cold herb; Scutellaria Root (Huangqin) 3 g — a cold herb that purges heat; Licorice (Gancao) 3 g
  • 【針對頭痛、各種痛——三陽】— 羌活 5 g（太陽經）；白芷 3 g（陽明經）；防風 5 g（少陽經）
  • 【三陰】— 蒼朮 5 g（太陰經）；川芎 3 g（厥陰肝）；細辛 1 g（少陰肺，依原稿標註）
  • 生地 3 g——冷藥；黃芩 3 g——寒藥洩熱；甘草 3 g
功效
Functions
Promote sweating and eliminate dampness; clear interior heat. (Main symptoms: exterior wind-cold, headache/occipital neck pain.)
發汗祛濕；清裡熱。主要症狀：外感風寒、頭痛／脖子後痛。
主治
Indication
Invasion of wind, cold, and dampness accompanied by interior heat — a comprehensive exterior-releasing formula.
風寒濕邪侵襲兼裡熱——解表通劑。

歌訣：九味羌活用防風，細辛蒼芷與川芎，黃芩生地同甘草，三陽解表益薑蔥。陰虛氣弱人禁用，加減臨時在變通。

臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Aversion to cold
  • Fever
  • Absence of perspiration
  • Headache
  • Aching upper back and limbs
  • Bitter taste in the mouth with slight thirst
  • Whitish or yellowish tongue coating
  • Superficial pulse
  • 惡寒
  • 發熱
  • 無汗
  • 頭痛
  • 上背及四肢痠痛
  • 口苦微渴
  • 苔白或黃
  • 脈浮
加減
Modification
  • More pain in the extremities → the dosage of Qianghuo can be doubled
  • No bitter taste, only slight thirst → omit Shengdihuang and Huangqin

Caution: contraindicated in those with yin deficiency and weak qi; modify flexibly as needed.

  • 四肢痠痛較明顯：羌活劑量可加倍
  • 沒有口苦只有渴：去生地黃、黃芩

⚠ 使用禁忌：陰虛氣弱之人禁用；加減需臨時變通。

▍本組方劑比較總表 Group Comparison Table

方劑 Formula證型 Pattern核心病機 Core Mechanism主要功效 Key Function辨證重點症狀 Key Manifestation特別提醒 Notes
麻黃湯
Ma Huang Tang		太陽傷寒（表實）寒邪束表，衛陽被鬱，肺氣不宣發汗解表、宣肺平喘惡寒發熱、無汗而喘、脈浮緊血虛者禁用；必須有咳嗽才用
桂枝湯
Gui Zhi Tang		太陽中風（表虛）風邪傷衛，營衛不和解肌發表、調和營衛汗出惡風、脈浮緩服藥後啜熱粥以助藥力發汗
小青龍湯
Xiao Qing Long Tang		外寒內飲風寒束表，水飲內停解表散寒、溫肺化飲惡寒發熱無汗、咳喘痰稀白量多排水三寶：細辛、乾薑、五味子
九味羌活湯
Jiu Wei Qiang Huo Tang		外感風寒濕兼裡熱風寒濕邪外束，兼有蘊熱發汗祛濕、兼清裡熱惡寒發熱無汗、頭身痠痛、口苦微渴依三陽三陰經絡選藥；陰虛氣弱禁用

11 · Exterior Wind-Heat 辛涼解表劑

▍熱的四個層次 The Four Levels of Heat (衛氣營血)

English中文
Heat at the Wei (defensive) level: Sang Ju Yin, Yin Qiao San, Ma Xing Shi Gan Tang.
熱在衛：桑菊飲、銀翹散、麻杏甘石湯。
Heat at the Qi level: Bai Hu Tang, Zhu Ye Shi Gao Tang.
熱在氣：白虎湯、竹葉石膏湯。
Heat at the Ying (nutritive) level: Qing Ying Tang.
熱在營：清營湯。
Heat at the Blood level: bleeding, mental derangement; Xi Jiao Di Huang Tang.
熱在血：流血、神亂；犀角地黃湯。

（原稿註：五臟的熱都在氣分。）

▍外感風熱證 Exterior Wind-Heat Syndrome

English中文
Fever more than chill; cough; sore throat; red eyes; floating pulse; thin white or slightly yellow coating.
發熱重於惡寒；咳嗽；喉嚨痛；眼睛紅；脈浮；苔薄白或微黃。

▍辛涼解表常用藥 Herbs for Wind-Heat

English中文
Mentha (Bohe) — cooler, pungent; for sore throat.
薄荷——較涼、辛味；喉嚨痛。
Arctium Fruit (Niubangzi) — for sore throat.
牛蒡子——喉嚨痛。
Mulberry Leaf (Sangye) — acts on the Lung (and Liver).
桑葉——作用在肺（跟肝）。
Chrysanthemum Flower (Juhua) — acts on the Liver (eyes) (and Lung).
菊花——作用在肝（眼睛）（跟肺）。
Pueraria Root (Gegen) — acts on the Yangming Stomach, frontal headache.
葛根——作用在陽明胃，額頭痛。
Bupleurum Root (Chaihu) — acts on the Shaoyang Gallbladder, temporal headache.
柴胡——作用在少陽膽，頭側痛。

（原稿標註：Gegen、Chaihu 兩者都對頭痛有用，僅部位不同。）

▍風熱三階段方劑（老師補充彙整） Three Stages of Wind-Heat

English中文
Stage 1 (Wei) — Sang Ju Yin: cough only.
初期（衛）——桑菊飲：只有咳嗽。
Stage 2 (Wei) — Yin Qiao San: sore throat, cough, fever more than chills (alternating).
第二階段（衛）——銀翹散：喉嚨痛、咳嗽、發熱多於發冷（往來寒熱）。
Stage 3 (final Wei stage, next is the Qi level) — Ma Xing Shi Gan Tang: high fever, difficulty breathing, the last stage of the Wei level.
第三階段（衛的最後，再深就進入「氣」分）——麻杏石甘湯：高燒、難以呼吸，衛分的最後階段。
Bai Du San — for weak-bodied people with insufficient upright qi and wind-heat, prone to recurrent colds. (If the person is weak, Yu Ping Feng San can be used daily as prevention, to strengthen the immune system.)
敗毒散——因正氣不足有風熱的體虛的人用，容易反覆感冒。如果體虛可以用玉屏風散當作日常預防，強化免疫系統。
▍EXAM FOCUS — 本組出題重點
  • 桑菊飲 vs 銀翹散是風熱初期到中期的進展：桑菊飲只治咳嗽（最輕），銀翹散多了喉嚨痛與往來寒熱（較重）——常考「先後順序」與「哪一方只治咳嗽」。
  • 麻杏石甘湯的麻黃：石膏比例是常考重點：高燒用 1:5、無汗用 1:3——記得「石膏多寡跟出汗程度成正比」。
  • 麻杏石甘湯與麻黃湯只差桂枝換成石膏，一個治寒（麻黃湯）、一個治熱（麻杏石甘湯），是經典的加減關係。
  • 敗毒散的三個變方：人蔘敗毒散（原方，兒童用）／荊防敗毒散（去人蔘加荊芥防風，風寒重）／銀翹敗毒散（去人蔘加銀花連翹，風熱兼潰瘍）——常考三方差異。
  • 「全部的 exterior 症狀不論冷熱，累積後都會變肺熱（沒有肺冷的可能）」是老師特別強調的重點，常考「表證傳變的終點」。
Sang Ju Yin
桑菊飲【風熱初期單純咳嗽】
Mulberry Leaf and Chrysanthemum Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Mulberry Leaf (Sangye) 7.5 g
  • Chrysanthemum Flower (Juhua) 3 g
  • Mentha (Bohe) 2.5 g
  • Apricot Seed (Xingren) 6 g
  • Forsythia Fruit (Lianqiao) 5 g
  • Platycodon Root (Jiegeng) 6 g
  • Reed Root (Lugen) 6 g
  • Licorice (Gancao) 2.5 g
  • 桑葉 7.5 g
  • 菊花 3 g
  • 薄荷 2.5 g
  • 杏仁 6 g
  • 連翹 5 g
  • 桔梗 6 g
  • 蘆根 6 g
  • 甘草 2.5 g
功效
Functions
Dispel wind and clear heat at the early stage; promote Lung function to stop coughing. (For the very early stage of cough, when there is only a little coughing.)
疏風清熱；宣肺止咳。咳嗽初期，只有一點點咳嗽時。
主治
Indication
Slight wind-warm at the early stage.
初期輕微風溫。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Dry cough
  • Sore throat
  • Mild feverish sensation
  • Slight thirst
  • Red tip and margin of the tongue
  • Superficial, rapid pulse
  • 乾咳
  • 喉嚨痛
  • 輕微發熱感
  • 輕微口渴
  • 舌尖舌邊紅
  • 脈浮數
加減
Modification
  • Excessive heat → add Anemarrhena Rhizome (Zhimu), Gypsum (Shigao)
  • Cough with thick yellow sputum → add Trichosanthes Fruit (Gualou), Scutellaria Root (Huangqin)
  • Severe thirst → add Trichosanthes Root (Tianhuafen)
  • Severe sore throat → add Scrophularia (Xuanshen), Arctium Fruit (Niubangzi)
  • 高燒：加知母、石膏
  • 咳黏稠黃痰：加栝蔞、黃芩
  • 口渴（嚴重）：加天花粉
  • 喉痛（嚴重）：加玄蔘、牛蒡子
Yin Qiao San
銀翹散【風熱喉嚨痛】
Honeysuckle and Forsythia Powder
組成
Ingredients
  • [Clear heat-toxin] — Honeysuckle Flower (Jinyinhua) 9 g; Forsythia Fruit (Lianqiao) 9 g
  • Platycodon Root (Jiegeng) 6 g — travels to the throat; Mentha (Bohe) 6 g; Arctium Fruit (Niubangzi) 9 g
  • [Moisten the Lung] — Bamboo Leaf (Zhuye) 4 g; Reed Root (Lugen) 9 g
  • Schizonepeta Spike (Jingjie) 5 g — pungent, aids upward dispersal; Prepared Soybean (Dandouchi) 5 g; Licorice (Gancao) 5 g
  • 【去熱毒】— 金銀花 9 g；連翹 9 g
  • 桔梗 6 g（走喉嚨）；薄荷 6 g；牛蒡子 9 g
  • 【潤肺】— 竹葉 4 g；蘆根 9 g
  • 荊芥穗 5 g（辛味助升發）；淡豆豉 5 g；甘草 5 g
功效
Functions
Clear exterior heat and release toxins. (For classic wind-heat symptoms with the onset of sore throat. Used for very early-stage red, itchy skin — if it has developed over some time, use Xiao Feng San instead.)
清熱解表、解毒。用於經典的風熱症狀，開始有喉嚨痛。用於很初始的皮膚紅癢，如果已發展一段時間要用消風散。
⚠ 原稿 ＊＊ 重點
熱是全身性的，毒是局部的（例：喉嚨痛、青春痘）。
主治
Indication
Warm disease at the initial stage, or severe exterior wind-heat.
初期溫病，或嚴重外感風熱。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Fever
  • Slight aversion to cold
  • Headache
  • Cough
  • Thirst
  • Sore throat
  • Red tongue
  • Thin white-yellow coating
  • Floating, rapid pulse
  • 發熱
  • 輕微惡寒
  • 頭痛
  • 咳嗽
  • 口渴
  • 喉嚨痛
  • 舌紅
  • 苔薄白黃
  • 脈浮數
加減
Modification
  • High fever and sweating → replace Jingjie and Dandouchi with Gypsum (Shigao) and Anemarrhena Rhizome (Zhimu)
  • Severe sore throat (the following two herbs act at the blood level, both for more serious conditions, like the throat being cut by a knife) → add Scrophularia (Xuanshen), Puffball (Mabo)
  • Initial stage of measles (Shigao and Chantui address skin-layer eruption from blood-heat, e.g. redness/swelling) → replace Dandouchi with Arnebia Root (Zicao) and Cicada Slough (Chantui)
  • 高燒發汗：以石膏、知母取代荊芥、淡豆豉
  • 喉痛嚴重（用於血分，較嚴重狀況，像喉嚨被刀割）：加玄蔘、馬勃
  • 初期麻疹（用於皮膚層出血熱問題，例如紅腫）：以紫草、蟬蛻取代淡豆豉
Ma Xing Shi Gan Tang
麻杏石甘湯【高燒難以呼吸】
Ephedra, Apricot Kernel, Gypsum and Licorice Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Ephedra (Mahuang) 6 g — treats cold
  • Gypsum (Shigao) 20–30 g — treats heat
  • Apricot Seed (Xingren) 9 g
  • Licorice (Gancao) 6 g
  • 麻黃 6 g——麻黃治冷
  • 石膏 20–30 g——石膏治熱
  • 杏仁 9 g
  • 甘草 6 g
老師補充 ＆＆
跟麻黃湯只差桂枝跟石膏的替換。
功效
Functions
Promote the Lung's functions of dispersing and descending; clear heat in the Lung and relieve asthma. (High fever, difficulty breathing.)
宣降肺氣；清肺平喘。高燒、難以呼吸。
主治
Indication
Exterior syndrome developed into the Lung. All exterior symptoms, regardless of cold or heat, eventually become Lung heat when accumulated (there is no possibility of Lung cold).
表證發展入肺。全部的表證症狀不論冷熱，累積後都會變肺熱（沒有肺冷的可能）。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Fever
  • Severe cough or difficult breathing
  • Thirst
  • Red tongue with yellow coating
  • Superficial, rapid pulse
  • 發熱
  • 嚴重咳嗽或呼吸困難
  • 口渴
  • 舌紅苔黃
  • 脈浮數
加減
Modification
  • Mahuang:Shigao ratio — 1:5 for high fever with sweating; 1:3 if there is no sweating (if sweating continues, it means the heat hasn't reduced, so use more Shigao; if no sweating, increase Mahuang)
  • These two herbs are strong enough to "force-dehydrate" the Lung; cough with profuse sputum → add Lepidium Seed (Tinglizi), Mulberry Bark (Sangbaipi)
  • Chest pain can be relieved with Gualou; sticky yellow sputum with stifling sensation in the chest → add Gualou and Beimu
  • 麻黃石膏比例：高燒發汗麻黃石膏 1:5，無汗麻黃石膏 1:3（如果持續流汗，代表熱度一直沒減退，就要多用石膏；如果沒流汗，則麻黃要增加）
  • 這兩個藥效較強，可以強迫肺脫水；咳嗽痰多 → 加葶藶子、桑白皮
  • 如果胸痛，用栝蔞都能緩解；痰黏稠色黃兼胸悶 → 加栝蔞、貝母
Bai Du San
敗毒散【病毒】
Toxin-Vanquishing Powder
組成
Ingredients
  • [Wind-cold-dampness] — Notopterygium Root (Qianghuo) 30 g; Pubescent Angelica Root (Duhuo) 30 g
  • [Moves qi/blood, resolves phlegm and dampness] — Bupleurum Root (Chaihu) 30 g; Peucedanum Root (Qianhu) 30 g; Chuanxiong Rhizome (Chuanxiong) 30 g; Bitter Orange Peel (Zhiqiao) 30 g; Poria (Fuling) 30 g
  • [Tonifying] — Ginseng (Renshen) 30 g
  • [Guiding herbs] — Platycodon Root (Jiegeng) 30 g; Licorice (Gancao) 15 g
  • 【風寒濕】— 羌活 30 g；獨活 30 g
  • 【走氣血、化痰去濕】— 柴胡 30 g；前胡 30 g；川芎 30 g；枳殼 30 g；茯苓 30 g
  • 【補】— 人蔘 30 g
  • 【藥引】— 桔梗 30 g；甘草 15 g
功效
Functions
Replenish qi and release the exterior syndrome; dispel wind-cold and eliminate dampness.
益氣解表；祛風寒除濕。
📝 課堂筆記（原文僅中文）
人蔘敗毒散——原始的敗毒散，兒童用藥。荊防敗毒散——較多辛味助升發藥，明顯病毒感染，因為風寒。銀翹敗毒散——針對喉嚨痛，因為風熱。
主治
Indication
Deficiency of Zheng qi accompanied by invasion of wind, cold, and dampness. "Toxin" here means summer-damp-heat; treats summer-heat, dampness, heat, and epidemic disease.
正氣虛弱兼風寒濕侵襲。毒，即暑濕熱也，治暑、濕、熱、時行。

歌訣：人蔘敗毒茯苓草，枳桔柴前羌獨芎，薄荷少許薑三片，時行感冒有奇功。去蔘名為敗毒散，加入消風治亦同。針對體虛的人，很容易生病。

臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Aversion to cold
  • Fever
  • Headache
  • Body aches
  • No sweating
  • Nasal obstruction
  • Cough with whitish sputum
  • Chest distress
  • Pale tongue with a whitish coating
  • Superficial, weak pulse
  • 惡寒
  • 發熱
  • 頭痛
  • 身痛
  • 無汗
  • 鼻塞
  • 咳嗽痰白
  • 胸悶
  • 舌淡苔白
  • 脈浮弱
加減
Modification
  • [Jing Fang Bai Du San] — in the absence of vital-qi deficiency, omit Renshen or add Jingjie (Schizonepeta) and Ledebouriella Root (Fangfeng), creating this variant
  • [Yin Qiao Bai Du San] — for the initial stage of mouth sores/ulcers, replace Renshen with Honeysuckle Flower (Jinyinhua) and Forsythia Fruit (Lianqiao)
  • 【荊防敗毒散】：正氣不虛者，去人蔘或加荊芥、防風，成此變方
  • 【銀翹敗毒散】：初期症狀出現嘴破潰瘍者，以金銀花、連翹取代人蔘

▍本組方劑比較總表 Group Comparison Table

方劑 Formula證型 Pattern核心病機 Core Mechanism主要功效 Key Function辨證重點症狀 Key Manifestation特別提醒 Notes
桑菊飲
Sang Ju Yin		風溫初起（衛分，最輕）風熱襲肺，肺氣失宣疏風清熱、宣肺止咳乾咳、輕微發熱口渴、脈浮數只有咳嗽，是風熱三階段中最輕
銀翹散
Yin Qiao San		風熱表證（衛分，中期）風熱襲表，兼熱毒上攻咽喉辛涼透表、清熱解毒發熱重、喉嚨痛、咳嗽、脈浮數較桑菊飲重，兼喉嚨痛
麻杏石甘湯
Ma Xing Shi Gan Tang		肺熱壅盛（衛分末期／入氣分）邪熱壅肺，肺失宣降宣肺清熱、止咳平喘高熱喘咳、口渴、脈浮數麻黃石膏比例依汗出調整
敗毒散
Bai Du San		正虛外感風寒濕正氣不足，外邪易侵益氣解表、散風祛濕惡寒發熱、身痛無汗、脈浮弱三個變方：人蔘／荊防／銀翹敗毒散

12 · Purgation 瀉下劑

▍承氣湯系列比較 Indications of Different Cheng Qi Tang

English中文
Da Cheng Qi Tang — the strongest effect; the classic four major Yangming excess-heat symptoms; rarely seen in this complete form.
大承氣湯——效果最強，經典陽明實熱症四大症狀，很少見。
Xiao Cheng Qi Tang — removes Mangxiao; mainly addresses heat rather than focusing on constipation.
小承氣湯——拿掉了芒硝，主要在處理熱而不主力處理便秘。
Tiao Wei Cheng Qi Tang — removes Zhishi and Houpo, representing that there is no hard obstruction.
調胃承氣湯——移除枳實厚朴，代表沒有硬物卡住。
Zeng Ye Cheng Qi Tang — addresses constipation from yin damage, tonifies yin (Shengdi, Maidong, Xuanshen).
增液承氣湯——處理陰損的便秘，補陰（生地麥冬玄蔘）。
Huang Long Tang — addresses qi and blood deficiency, adds Renshen and Danggui.
黃龍湯——處理氣血不足，加入人蔘當歸。
Wen Pi Tang — for accumulated cold from Spleen yang deficiency.
溫脾湯——脾陽虛造成的寒症的累積。
Da Huang Fu Zi Tang — targets accumulated cold.
大黃附子湯——針對寒症的累積。
Ma Zi Ren Wan — mild; used for chronic constipation, helps intestinal peristalsis.
麻子仁丸——溫和，用於長期的便秘，幫助腸胃蠕動。
Ji Chuan Jian — constipation from Kidney deficiency in the elderly.
濟川煎——老年腎虛造成的便秘。
分類 ClassificationEnglish中文
Purgative formulas with a cold nature: Da Cheng Qi Tang, Xiao Cheng Qi Tang.
用涼藥：大承氣湯、小承氣湯。
Purgative formulas with a warm nature: Wen Pi Tang, Da Huang Fu Zi Tang.
用暖藥：溫脾湯、大黃附子湯。
Mild laxative formulas: Ma Zi Ren Wan.
用溫和：麻子仁丸。
Purgative formulas with a tonifying effect: Ji Chuan Jian.
用補：濟川煎。

▍瀉下與潤腸常用藥 Purging and Lubricating Herbs

攻下藥 PurgeEnglish中文
Rhubarb (Dahuang) — enters Spleen, Stomach, Large Intestine, Liver, Heart channels.
大黃——歸脾、胃、大腸、肝、心經。
Glauber's Salt (Mangxiao) — enters Stomach, Large Intestine channels.
芒硝——歸胃、大腸經。
潤下藥 LubricateEnglish中文
Hemp Seed (Huomaren) — enters Spleen, Large Intestine channels.
火麻仁——歸脾、大腸經。
Bush-Cherry Seed (Yuliren) — enters Large Intestine, Small Intestine channels.
郁李仁——歸大腸、小腸經。

▍陽明腑證「痞滿燥實」對應藥 The "Pi-Man-Zao-Shi" Pattern of the Yangming Organ

English中文
1. Constipation (Pi — qi mechanism blocked in the chest/abdomen, discomfort) → Rhubarb: attacks and flushes downward
1. 便秘（痞：胸腹間氣機阻塞不舒）→ 大黃：攻實滌下
2. Abdominal distension, dislike of pressing (Man) → Houpo: relieves fullness, moves qi
2. 腹脹拒按（滿）→ 厚朴：除滿行氣
3. Dry stool (Zao) → Mangxiao: moistens dryness, softens hardness
3. 大便乾燥（燥）→ 芒硝：潤燥軟堅
4. Fever with dark tongue coating (Shi — hard mass present, tender on pressing) → Zhishi: resolves stuffiness, breaks binding
4. 發熱兼舌苔深色（實：有硬物，按壓會痛）→ 枳實：消痞破結
▍EXAM FOCUS — 本組出題重點
  • 大、小、調胃三個承氣湯的藥味增減邏輯是必考重點：大承氣湯（四藥全）→小承氣湯（去芒硝，重在清熱不重通便）→調胃承氣湯（去枳實厚朴，代表無硬物阻塞）。
  • 寒下（大黃附子湯）vs 溫下（溫脾湯）vs 潤下（麻子仁丸）vs 補下（濟川煎）——四種瀉下法的藥性分類是核心考點。
  • 濟川煎專治老年腎虛便秘，特別強調老年人不可用大黃、芒硝、厚朴類峻下藥，是常見的臨床安全性考點。
  • 陳皮「行氣」與枳實「破氣」的差異：陳皮促進氣的流動，枳實則是破解已經結塊的氣滯（按壓會痛）——是常見的細節鑑別題。
Da Cheng Qi Tang
大承氣湯【陽明腑實熱】
Major Order the Qi Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Rhubarb (Dahuang) 12 g — attacks and flushes downward, clears heat, moves downward
  • Mirabilite (Mangxiao) 9 g — moistens dryness, softens hardness, softens masses (if not a true excess-heat pattern, this salty-cold substance can damage the body's foundation)
  • Immature Bitter Orange (Zhishi) 12 g — resolves stuffiness, breaks binding qi
  • Magnolia Bark (Houpo) 15 g — relieves fullness, moves qi downward, sends distension downward
  • 大黃 12 g——攻實滌下，去熱，往下走
  • 芒硝 9 g——潤燥軟堅，軟化結塊（如果不是實熱，鹹寒的東西會傷本）
  • 枳實 12 g——消痞破結
  • 厚朴 15 g——除滿行氣，往下走，脹滿時往下走
老師補充 ＆＆
陳皮走氣靠的是促進氣。枳實可以破結，也是走氣但是是破已經結塊的東西，按肚會痛。
功效
Functions
Yangming-organ excess pattern — drastic purgation for eliminating accumulated heat.
陽明腑實證——峻下攻積，清除熱結。
主治
Indication
Excess heat syndrome of the Yangming Fu organs — treats the Stomach organ and Triple Burner, great heat and great excess.
陽明腑實熱證——治胃府、三焦，大熱大實。

歌訣：承氣湯用芒硝，枳實大黃厚朴饒，救陰瀉熱功偏擅，急下陽明有數條。治陽明病大實大滿、大便不通、腹痛大熱，其脈沉實者，此方主之。

臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Constipation
  • Abdominal pain with tenderness
  • Thirst with desire to drink
  • Red tongue with dry, yellowish coating
  • Deep, forceful pulse
  • 便秘
  • 腹痛拒按
  • 口渴欲飲
  • 舌紅苔黃燥
  • 脈沉實
加減
Modification
  • [Xiao Cheng Qi Tang] — for mild constipation, omit Mangxiao (for Yangming-organ excess with chest/abdominal stuffiness, old yellow coating, slippery-rapid pulse, or early dysentery with abdominal pain and tenesmus)
  • [Tiao Wei Cheng Qi Tang] — for mild abdominal distension, omit Houpo and Zhishi
  • [Huang Long Tang] — for qi and blood deficiency, add Renshen and Danggui (clears heat and purges while boosting qi and nourishing blood; abdominal pain worse with pressure, scorched-yellow or scorched-black coating, deep, thready, rapid pulse)
  • [Zeng Ye Cheng Qi Tang] — for yin insufficiency, replace Houpo and Zhishi with Xuanshen, Shengdihuang, and Maidong (nourishes yin; dry mouth/throat, red dry tongue, dry yellow coating, thready rapid pulse)
  • 【小承氣湯】：便秘較輕，去芒硝（陽明腑實證，胸腹痞滿，苔老黃，脈滑而疾，或痢疾初期下利腹痛兼裡急後重）
  • 【調胃承氣湯】：腹脹較輕，去厚朴、枳實
  • 【黃龍湯】：氣血不足，加人蔘、當歸（清熱瀉下、益氣養血；腹痛拒按，舌苔焦黃或焦黑，脈沉細數）
  • 【增液承氣湯】：陰不足，以玄蔘、生地黃、麥冬取代厚朴、枳實（補陰；口燥咽乾，舌紅乾，苔黃燥，脈細數）
Wen Pi Tang
溫脾湯【虛：脾陽虛】
Warm the Spleen Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Rhubarb (Dahuang) 12 g
  • Mirabilite (Mangxiao) 9 g

  • Dried Ginger (Ganjiang) 6 g

功效
Functions
Warm and invigorate Spleen yang, eliminate cold-waste accumulation. (Targets deficiency-cold from Spleen yang deficiency, of a chronic nature.)
溫補脾陽、攻下冷積。針對脾陽虛造成的虛寒，並且是長期的。
主治
Indication
Constipation due to cold-waste accumulation — a "warm attack-purgation."
因寒積所致的便秘——「溫藥攻下」。

歌訣：溫脾蔘附與乾薑，甘草當歸硝大黃，寒熱並行治寒積，臍腹絞結痛非常。針對寒症的累積。

Ji Chuan Jian

因腎虛所致的便秘。老年腎虛，大便秘結，小便清長，腰酸足軟，背冷畏寒，脈沉弱者。

  • 嚴重體虛：移除枳實

13 · Harmonizing 和解劑

▍調和少陽 Harmonizing Shaoyang

English中文
Half-exterior, half-interior (Harmonizing usually involves multiple organs): alternating fever and chills, bitter taste, wiry pulse.
半表半裡（調和通常牽涉到多個臟腑）：往來寒熱、口苦、弦脈。
Taiyang: cold and heat present simultaneously. Yangming: high fever. Shaoyang: alternating chills and fever.
太陽：冷熱同時。陽明：高燒。少陽：往來寒熱。
📝 課堂筆記（原文僅中文）
調和肝脾：木剋土，肝氣滯會傷脾。調和脾胃：高燒、口渴、流汗、洪脈（胃氣下降，脾氣上升）。

▍調和少陽方劑 Formulas Harmonizing Shaoyang

English中文
Xiao Chai Hu Tang — the standard/most important Shaoyang formula.
小柴胡湯——少陽最重要藥方，standard。
Da Chai Hu Tang (cross-reference formula) — Yangming pattern + Shaoyang pattern.
大柴胡湯（跨方參照）——陽明症＋少陽症。
Hao Qin Qing Dan Tang — a special Shaoyang formula: Xiao Chai Hu Tang pattern + dampness, clears heat and removes dampness.
蒿芩清膽湯——special 的少陽藥方，小柴胡湯症＋濕氣，清熱去濕。

▍調和肝脾方劑 Formulas Harmonizing Liver/Spleen

English中文
Si Ni San — Liver qi stagnation (excess) leading to cold limbs.
四逆散——肝氣滯（實）導致四肢冷。
Xiao Yao San — all menstrual or women's issues; Liver qi stagnation = PMS; Liver invades Spleen → Spleen deficiency → blood deficiency.
逍遙散——所有生理期或女性問題，肝氣滯＝PMS，肝犯脾＞脾虛＞缺血。
Tong Xie Yao Fang — Liver invades Spleen → Spleen deficiency → diarrhea with abdominal pain.
痛瀉要方——肝犯脾＞脾虛＞腹瀉肚痛。

▍調和脾胃方劑 Formulas Harmonizing Spleen/Stomach

English中文
Ban Xia Xie Xin Tang — simultaneous vomiting + diarrhea, regulates Spleen/Stomach.
半夏瀉心湯——同時嘔吐＋腹瀉，調脾胃。

▍小柴胡湯用藥分析 Herb Combination Analysis in Xiao Chai Hu Tang

English中文
Dispersing/Clearing: Chaihu / Huangqin
發散／清熱：柴胡／黃芩
Warming/Cooling: Shengjiang, Banxia / Huangqin
溫／涼：生薑、半夏／黃芩
Tonifying/Reducing: Renshen, Dazao / Banxia, Huangqin
補／瀉：人蔘、大棗／半夏、黃芩
Ascending/Descending: Chaihu, Shengjiang / Banxia, Huangqin
升／降：柴胡、生薑／半夏、黃芩

▍七方速記總覽 Quick Summary of the Seven Formulas

English中文
Xiao Chai Hu Tang — the classic Shaoyang pattern.
小柴胡湯——經典少陽症。
Da Chai Hu Tang — Yangming + Shaoyang pattern.
大柴胡湯——陽明＋少陽症。
Hao Qin Qing Dan Tang — Shaoyang pattern + dampness.
蒿芩清膽湯——少陽症＋濕氣。
Si Ni San — cold limbs from excess + Liver qi stagnation.
四逆散——手腳冰冷（實）＋肝氣滯。
Xiao Yao San — Liver qi stagnation + blood & Spleen deficiency + cold limbs from deficiency.
逍遙散——肝氣滯＋血虛脾虛＋手腳冰冷（虛）。
Tong Xie Yao Fang — Liver invading Spleen, abdominal pain and diarrhea with emotional trigger, treats IBS.
痛瀉要方——肝犯脾，腹痛腹瀉＋情志，治療IBS。
Ban Xia Xie Xin Tang — Spleen + Stomach, simultaneous vomiting + diarrhea.
半夏瀉心湯——脾＋胃，同時嘔吐＋腹瀉。
▍EXAM FOCUS — 本組出題重點
  • 小柴胡湯的柴胡＋黃芩是全書「和解」的典範對藥：柴胡升散、黃芩清降，一升一降；四組陰陽對藥（升降、寒熱、補瀉、散清）是常考分析題。
  • 大柴胡湯＝小柴胡湯＋小承氣湯的概念（陽明＋少陽同時出現）——「只有陽明症=小承氣湯，只有少陽症=小柴胡湯」是老師特別強調的口訣。
  • 四逆散 vs 逍遙散：男用四逆（實證，肝氣滯致四肢冷）、女用逍遙散（補，肝血脾虛致四肢冷）——常考虛實與性別臨床傾向的鑑別。
  • 逍遙散的三個變方：丹梔逍遙散（加牡丹皮梔子，治虛熱口苦）、黑逍遙散（加熟地黃，治血虛較重）、加香附鬱金（治肝氣滯較重）——常考變方對應症狀。
  • 半夏瀉心湯的辛開苦降結構：乾薑（辛熱）配黃連黃芩（苦寒），是「寒熱並用、辛開苦降」治痞的代表方，並與小陷胸湯（見第08組，按之則痛）鑑別。
Xiao Chai Hu Tang
小柴胡湯【少陽症半表半裏】
Minor Bupleurum Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • [Addresses Shaoyang heat] — Bupleurum Root (Chaihu) 12 g: a warm herb, regulates Liver qi, sends qi upward; Scutellaria Root (Huangqin) 9 g: a cold herb, sends qi downward
  • [Moves qi] — Fresh Ginger (Shengjiang) 9 g: disperses qi outward, stops vomiting and dizziness; Pinellia Tuber (Banxia) 9 g: sends qi downward, removes phlegm
  • [Tonify] — Ginseng (Renshen) 6 g: tonifies qi; Jujube (Dazao) 4 pieces: tonifies blood
  • Baked Licorice (Zhigancao) 5 g
  • 【處理少陽熱】— 柴胡 12 g：溫藥，調整肝氣，讓氣向上；黃芩 9 g：寒藥，讓氣向下
  • 【走氣】— 生薑 9 g：讓氣往外散，止嘔止暈；半夏 9 g：讓氣往下，去痰
  • 【補】— 人蔘 6 g：補氣；大棗 4 枚：補血
  • 炙甘草 5 g
功效
Functions
Harmonize the Shaoyang meridians. (Alternating chills and fever, bitter mouth, wiry pulse.)
和解少陽。往來寒熱、口苦、脈弦。
主治
Indication
Febrile diseases in the Shaoyang meridian — half-exterior/half-interior, harmonizing. Also febrile disease during menstruation (women often catch a "cold" and get heat symptoms during their period).
少陽經發熱——半表半裡、和解。也用於生理期發熱（生理期常感冒，常常有熱象）。

歌訣：小柴胡湯和解供，半夏人蔘甘草從，更用黃芩加薑棗，少陽百病此為宗。治少陽經發熱、口苦耳聾、其脈弦者，又治太陽陽明二經發熱不退、寒熱往來。（麻黃湯、桂枝湯不處理生理期相關症狀）

臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Alternating chills and fever
  • Fullness in the chest
  • Hypochondriac discomfort
  • Bitter taste in mouth
  • Dry throat
  • Restless
  • Thin, whitish tongue coating
  • Wiry pulse
  • 往來寒熱
  • 胸悶
  • 脅肋不適
  • 口苦
  • 咽乾
  • 心煩
  • 舌苔薄白
  • 脈弦
加減
Modification
  • Chest fullness without vomiting → replace Banxia with Trichosanthes Fruit (Gualou)
  • Thirst → replace Banxia with Trichosanthes Root (Tianhuafen) (moistens the Lung, clears heat, resolves toxin, drains breast abscess)
  • Abdominal pain → replace Huangqin with White Peony Root (Baishao) (regulates blood/menses, smooths Liver qi, stops pain)
  • [Da Chai Hu Tang] — when Shaoyang and Yangming patterns appear together (constipation), combine with Xiao Cheng Qi Tang to create Da Chai Hu Tang
  • 胸滿但沒嘔：以栝蔞取代半夏
  • 口渴：以天花粉取代半夏（潤肺清熱、解毒、排乳房膿腫）
  • 腹痛：以白芍取代黃芩（血調經、順肝氣、止痛）
  • 【大柴胡湯】：陽明症＋少陽症同時出現（可處理便秘），合小承氣湯而成
老師補充 ＆＆
如果只有陽明症＝小承氣湯，如果只有少陽症＝小柴胡湯。
Hao Qin Qing Dan Tang
蒿芩清膽湯【少陽症＋濕熱】
Artemisia and Scutellaria Decoction to Clear Gall Bladder
組成
Ingredients
  • Clear heat — Sweet Wormwood (Qinghao) 6 g: clears empty heat, removes bone-steaming heat, resolves summer-heat, enters Liver/Gallbladder/Kidney; Scutellaria Root (Huangqin) 6 g: drains lower-Jiao damp-heat
  • [Resolve phlegm — herb-pair: Chenpi + Banxia = Er Chen Tang] — Pinellia Tuber (Banxia) 5 g: toxic, dries dampness and resolves phlegm, descends rebellion, stops vomiting, resolves stuffiness/masses; Citrus Peel (Chenpi) 5 g: regulates qi, strengthens the Spleen, dries dampness and resolves phlegm
  • Remove dampness — Bitter Orange (Zhiqiao) 5 g; Bamboo Shavings (Zhuru) 9 g: clears heat, resolves phlegm, relieves vexation, stops vomiting, enters Lung/Stomach; Poria (Fuling) 9 g
  • 清熱 — 青蒿 6 g：清虛熱，除骨蒸，解暑，走肝、膽、腎；黃芩 6 g：瀉下焦濕熱
  • 【去痰，對藥：陳皮＋半夏＝二陳湯】— 半夏 5 g：有毒、燥濕化痰，降逆止嘔，消痞散結；陳皮 5 g：理氣健脾，燥濕化痰
  • 去濕 — 枳殼 5 g；竹茹 9 g：清熱化痰，除煩止嘔，走肺、胃；茯苓 9 g
功效
Functions
Clear heat and dampness in the Gallbladder; harmonize the Stomach and resolve phlegm. (A special Shaoyang pattern = Shaoyang pattern + damp-heat; a modified variant of Wen Dan Tang, adding Qinghao + Huangqin.) Chills and fever like malaria, more chills less heat, chest/hypochondriac distension and oppression, bitter mouth with acid regurgitation, or vomiting yellow, sticky saliva, red tongue with greasy yellow coating, wiry-slippery pulse.
清膽利濕、和胃化痰。特殊少陽症狀＝少陽症＋濕熱，溫膽湯的變方，加青蒿＋黃芩。寒熱如瘧、寒輕熱重、胸脅脹悶、口苦泛酸、或嘔黃涎而粘、舌紅苔黃膩、脈弦滑。
📝 課堂筆記（原文僅中文）
青蒿以可以治瘧疾聞名，藥效比柴胡強。
主治
Indication
Damp-heat in the Shaoyang meridian.
少陽經之濕熱證。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Alternating chills and fever
  • Bitter taste in the mouth
  • Chest distress, acid regurgitation
  • Or vomiting, hiccupping
  • Red tongue with greasy, yellowish coating
  • Rapid pulse — wiry on the left side, smooth on the right side
  • 往來寒熱
  • 口苦
  • 胸悶泛酸
  • 或嘔吐呃逆
  • 舌紅苔黃膩
  • 脈數——左弦右滑
加減
Modification
  • Jaundice → replace Banxia/Chenpi with Oriental Wormwood (Yinchenhao: disinhibits dampness, resolves jaundice, clears damp-heat) and Capejasmine Fruit (Zhizi: clears heat, purges fire, disinhibits dampness, stops pain)
  • 黃疸：以茵陳蒿（利濕退黃、清利濕熱）、梔子（清熱瀉火利濕止痛）取代半夏／陳皮
Si Ni San
四逆散【少陰四肢冷】
Four Herbs Powder to Relieve Cold Limbs
組成
Ingredients
  • Bupleurum Root (Chaihu) 6 g — moves Liver qi
  • White Peony Root (Baishao) 9 g — tonifies Liver blood
  • Immature Bitter Orange (Zhishi) 6 g — breaks qi
  • Baked Licorice (Zhigancao) 6 g — paired with Baishao, can tonify

Gancao harmonizes the nature of other herbs, and can serve as a guiding herb in any formula. Baked Licorice, being sweet, can harmonize and tonify, and can also serve as a guiding herb.

  • 柴胡 6 g——走肝氣
  • 白芍 9 g——補肝血
  • 枳實 6 g——破氣
  • 炙甘草 6 g——跟白芍一起配，可補

甘草：調和其他藥的藥性，都可作為藥引。炙甘草：甜的藥可以調和、補，都可作為藥引。

功效
Functions
Soothe the Liver and regulate the Spleen; disperses stagnant heat, courses the Liver and regulates the Spleen. Heat reversal pattern — despite cold limbs, there is body heat or epigastric/abdominal pain, or diarrhea with tenesmus, or dysuria, wiry pulse. Normally cold limbs indicate yang deficiency, but Si Ni San's pattern is not this — it is cold limbs caused by dampness leading to poor appetite + fatigue + cold limbs.
疏肝理脾。透解鬱熱，疏肝理脾。熱厥證，雖手足厥冷，但身熱或脘腹疼痛，或泄利下重，或小便不利，脈弦。通常手腳冷是陽虛，但四逆散症不是如此，四逆散是因濕氣造成沒胃口＋疲勞＋手腳冷。
主治
Indication
Disharmony between the Liver and Spleen — treats yang-type heat reversal.
肝脾不和——治陽證熱厥。

歌訣：四逆散裏用柴胡，芍藥枳實甘草須，此是陽邪成厥逆，斂陰泄熱平劑扶。治少陰病四逆，其人或咳，或悸，或小便不利，或腹中痛，或泄利下重者主之。

臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Cold limbs
  • Hypochondriac distension and fullness
  • Epigastric and abdominal pain
  • Thin, whitish tongue coating
  • Wiry pulse
  • 四肢冷
  • 脅肋脹滿
  • 脘腹疼痛
  • 舌苔薄白
  • 脈弦
加減
Modification
  • Severe hypochondriac pain → add Cyperus Tuber (Xiangfu, has a pain-relieving effect) and Chuanxiong Rhizome (Chuanxiong) — this formula is Chaihu Shu Gan Tang (Chaihu Decoction to Soothe the Liver, derived from Si Ni San)
  • Jaundice → add Oriental Wormwood (Yinchenhao: disinhibits dampness, resolves jaundice, clears damp-heat) and Curcuma Root (Yujin: moves blood and qi, stops pain, relieves depression, clears the Heart, disinhibits dampness, resolves jaundice, cools blood)
  • 肋脅疼痛：加香附（有止痛效果）、川芎——柴胡疏肝散（源自四逆散）
  • 黃疸：加茵陳蒿（利濕退黃、清利濕熱）、鬱金（活血行氣止痛，解鬱清心，利陰退黃，涼血）
Xiao Yao San
逍遙散【肝氣滯犯脾】
Rambling Powder
組成
Ingredients
  • Bupleurum Root (Chaihu) 30 g
  • [Clear heat] — White Peony Root (Baishao) 30 g: tonifies blood; Chinese Angelica Root (Danggui) 30 g: tonifies blood; White Atractylodes (Baizhu) 30 g: tonifies the Spleen; Poria (Fuling) 30 g
  • Mentha (Bohe) 3 g; Fresh Ginger (Shengjiang) 3 g; Baked Licorice (Zhigancao) 15 g
  • 柴胡 30 g
  • 【清熱】— 白芍 30 g：補血；當歸 30 g：補血；白朮 30 g：補脾；茯苓 30 g
  • 薄荷 3 g；生薑 3 g；炙甘草 15 g
功效
Functions
Promote Liver qi circulation; nourish blood and invigorate the Spleen. (Liver invades Spleen, leading to Spleen deficiency, Spleen deficiency leading to blood deficiency — two organs involved: Liver + Spleen.)
疏肝解鬱、養血健脾。肝犯脾、導致脾虛、脾虛導致缺血。（兩個臟器：肝＋脾）
📝 課堂筆記（原文僅中文）
四逆散跟逍遙散分辨：男用四逆、女用逍遙散（補）。
主治
Indication
Liver stagnation with blood and Spleen deficiency — disperses depression, removes steaming heat. Treats PMS.
肝鬱兼血虛脾虛——散鬱、除蒸。治療PMS。

歌訣：逍遙散用當歸芍，柴苓朮草加薑薄，散鬱除蒸功最奇，調經八味丹梔著。

臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Hypochondriac pain
  • Headache, dizziness
  • Depression
  • Poor appetite
  • Irregular menstruation
  • Breast distension
  • Reddish tongue
  • Thready pulse
  • 脅肋疼痛
  • 頭痛暈眩
  • 情緒抑鬱
  • 食慾不振
  • 月經不調
  • 乳房脹痛
  • 舌偏紅
  • 脈細
加減
Modification
  • [Danzhi Xiaoyao San / "augmented" Xiaoyao San] — for empty heat with a bitter taste and dry mouth (and yin deficiency), add Moutan Bark (Mudanpi: moves blood, clears heat from the blood) and Capejasmine Fruit (Zhizi)
  • [Hei Xiaoyao San] — for more severe blood deficiency, add Prepared Rehmannia Root (Shudihuang: stronger blood-tonifying effect; note: fresh Rehmannia [Shengdihuang] instead nourishes yin, clears empty heat, cools blood)
  • Severe Liver qi stagnation — add Cyperus Tuber (Xiangfu) and Curcuma Root (Yujin) (for those prone to sudden rage, on the verge of losing control)
  • 【丹梔逍遙散（加味逍遙散）】：虛熱兼口苦口乾（兼陰虛）：加牡丹皮（活血、清血熱）、梔子
  • 【黑逍遙散】：血虛較重：加熟地黃（補血效果較強；生地黃則是滋陰，清虛熱涼血）
  • 肝氣滯（較嚴重）：加香附、鬱金（容易暴怒、快發瘋）
Tong Xie Yao Fang
痛瀉要方【肝滯而長期腹瀉＋痛】
Important Formula for Painful Diarrhea
組成
Ingredients
  • White Atractylodes (Baizhu) 18 g
  • White Peony Root (Baishao) 6 g
  • Tangerine Peel (Chenpi) 6 g
  • Ledebouriella Root (Fangfeng) 6 g
  • 白朮 18 g
  • 白芍 6 g
  • 陳皮 6 g
  • 防風 6 g
功效
Functions
Harmonize Liver and Spleen to relieve painful diarrhea — treats IBS. Tonifies the Spleen and drains the Liver, dispels dampness and stops diarrhea. (Treats IBS: chronic diarrhea and abdominal pain caused by Liver qi stagnation, i.e. diarrhea without an apparent cause.)
補脾瀉肝，祛濕止瀉——治療IBS。（治療IBS：因為肝氣滯導致的長期腹瀉腹痛，無原因的腹瀉）
主治
Indication
Treats painful diarrhea.
治痛瀉。

歌訣（原稿此處註記另一方歌訣「清脾飲」，與本方無直接關聯，保留供對照）：清脾飲用青朴柴，芩夏甘苓白朮偕，更加草果薑煎服，熱多陽瘧此方佳。

臨床表現
Manifestation
（原始資料此欄僅列一項加減：慢性腹瀉加升麻）
（原始資料此欄僅列一項加減：慢性腹瀉加升麻）
加減
Modification
  • Chronic diarrhea → add Cimicifuga (Shengma) — similar to Chaihu, raises qi upward
  • 慢性腹瀉：加升麻（類似柴胡，會讓氣上升）
Ban Xia Xie Xin Tang
半夏瀉心湯【同時嘔吐＋腹瀉】
Pinellia Decoction to Drain the Epigastrium
組成
Ingredients
  • Pinellia Tuber (Banxia) 9 g
  • Scutellaria Root (Huangqin) 6 g — drains lower-Jiao damp-heat
  • Dried Ginger (Ganjiang) 6 g
  • Coptis (Huanglian) 3 g — clears middle-Jiao damp-fire binding
  • Ginseng (Renshen) 6 g
  • Jujube (Dazao) 4 pieces
  • Licorice (Gancao) 6 g
  • 半夏 9 g
  • 黃芩 6 g——瀉下焦濕熱
  • 乾薑 6 g
  • 黃連 3 g——清中焦濕火鬱結
  • 人蔘 6 g
  • 大棗 4 枚
  • 甘草 6 g
功效
Functions
Harmonize the Spleen and Stomach. (Stomach qi disharmony, stuffiness below the Heart, but fullness without pain — presents with simultaneous vomiting + diarrhea.)
和胃降逆、開結除痞。胃氣不和，心下痞滿，但滿不痛；同時嘔吐＋腹瀉者。
主治
Indication
Disharmony of Spleen and Stomach — treats "empty stuffiness" from erroneous purgation.
脾胃不和——治誤下虛痞。

歌訣：半夏瀉心黃連芩，乾薑甘草與人蔘，大棗和之治虛痞，法在降陽而和陰。

📝 課堂筆記（原文僅中文）
見第08組・化痰劑「小陷胸湯」卡片：半夏瀉心湯不會有疼痛感；小陷胸湯按心下會有疼痛感。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Stomach fullness without pain
  • Vomiting, borborygmus, and diarrhea
  • Slight yellowish, greasy tongue coating
  • Wiry, rapid pulse
  • 胃部痞滿而不痛
  • 嘔吐、腸鳴、腹瀉
  • 舌苔微黃膩
  • 脈弦數
加減
Modification
  • Severe fullness/distension in the epigastric region → replace Renshen with Immature Bitter Orange (Zhishi)
  • If the constitution is not weak → remove Renshen and Ganjiang
  • For vomiting → change dried ginger to fresh ginger (Shengjiang stops vomiting)
  • Weak Stomach qi → double the dosage of Gancao
  • 腹滿（嚴重）：以枳實取代人蔘
  • 體質不虛：去人蔘、乾薑
  • 嘔吐：乾薑改生薑（生薑止嘔）
  • 胃氣弱：甘草加倍

14 · Clearing Heat 清熱劑

▍衛氣營血四個層次 The Four Levels (Wei-Qi-Ying-Xue)

English中文
Wei: exterior, cough, sore throat.
衛：表證，咳嗽，喉嚨痛。
Qi: interior — Lung, Spleen, Stomach. High fever, thirst, sweating, big pulse.
氣：內在——肺脾胃。高燒、口渴、流汗、洪脈。
Ying: the blood-vessel level. Early stage of the Blood level. Fever severe at night, heat disturbing the Shen (insomnia), blood stasis / indistinct skin rashes.
營：血脈層次。血分的早期階段。夜熱較重，熱擾神（失眠），瘀血／隱約皮疹。
Xue: deep heat at the deepest level. Bleeding, various symptoms worsen.
血：最深層的深熱。流血、各種症狀加重。

＆＆老師補充：衛跟氣統稱為「氣分」層級（Qi level），營跟血統稱為「血分」層級（Blood level）。

▍氣分熱二方比較 Bai Hu Tang vs. Zhu Ye Shi Gao Tang

English中文
Bai Hu Tang — yin not yet damaged, early-stage Qi-level heat.
白虎湯——還沒傷到陰，初期氣分熱。
Zhu Ye Shi Gao Tang — both qi and yin deficient, late-stage Qi-level heat.
竹葉石膏湯——氣＆陰兩虛，晚期氣分熱。

▍血分熱二方比較 Qing Ying Tang vs. Xi Jiao Di Huang Tang

English中文
Distinguished by presence/absence of bleeding.
區別方式：有無流血。
Qing Ying Tang — clears heat at the early stage of the Blood level.
清營湯——清早期血分熱。
Xi Jiao Di Huang Tang — clears heat at the deep Blood-level stage.
犀角地黃湯——清血分深層熱。

▍臟腑熱總覽 Organ Heat Overview

English中文
Heart and Small Intestine fire — insomnia, red tongue tip, sore tongue. Heat transmits to the Small Intestine — dark, yellow urine, acute symptoms.
心與小腸火——失眠、舌尖紅、舌頭痠痛。熱會傳到小腸：症狀尿變黑變黃，急性症狀。
Liver and Gallbladder fire — severe headache, high-pitched tinnitus, yellow sclera. (Liver fire has two directions: Liver fire rises upward, damp or damp-heat flows downward, while heat itself moves everywhere.)
肝膽火——嚴重頭痛、高頻耳鳴、眼白黃。（肝火分兩個方向：肝火往上，濕或濕熱往下，熱則是到處跑。）
Stomach fire — acid reflux, swollen and painful gums.
胃火——胃酸逆流、牙齦腫痛。
Lung and Large Intestine heat — high fever, cough, constipation, chronic cough.
肺與大腸熱——高燒、咳嗽、便祕、長期咳嗽。
The Kidney has no true "heat" symptoms; if it's heat from Kidney yin deficiency, it becomes a chronic empty-fire pattern.
腎沒有Heat的症狀，如果是腎陰虛的熱，會變成長期虛火症狀。

▍五臟清熱方劑對照 Formulas for Clearing Heat by Organ

心 HeartEnglish中文
Dao Chi San — Mutong enters the Heart directly (excess Heart fire).
導赤散——木通直接入心（實心火）。
肝 LiverEnglish中文
Long Dan Xie Gan Tang — Liver fire and heat (excess Liver fire).
龍膽瀉肝湯——肝火＆熱（實肝火）。
Zuo Jin Wan — Liver fire damaging the Stomach (if it's Liver heat rather than fire, it damages the Spleen instead).
左金丸——肝火犯胃（如果是肝熱則犯脾）。
胃 StomachEnglish中文
Qing Wei San — uses Shengma to let heat vent upward (excess Stomach fire).
清胃散——用升麻讓熱往上揮發（實胃火）。
Yu Nu Jian — uses Niuxi to send energy downward (Stomach yin deficiency).
玉女煎——用牛膝讓能量往下走（胃陰虛）。
肺 LungEnglish中文
Xie Bai San — for chronic, long-accumulated Lung heat.
瀉白散——長期慢性肺熱累積。
Ma Xing Shi Gan Tang (see Group 11) — used for acute Lung heat, which is fast and severe, so it easily causes high fever.
麻杏石甘湯（見第11組）——用於急性的肺熱，會快速又劇烈因此容易高燒。

總結：導赤散——治心火；龍膽瀉肝湯——治肝火、肝熱；左金丸——治肝火犯胃；清胃散——治胃火，牙齦腫痛（實火，急性症狀）；玉女煎——治虛熱，牙齦腫痛（虛火，長期症狀）；瀉白散——治肺火（轉為內在）；芍藥湯——治大腸濕熱下痢；白頭翁湯——治腹瀉血便。

▍熱毒／暑熱／虛熱總覽 Heat-Toxin / Summer-Heat / Empty-Heat Overview

清熱毒English中文
Huang Lian Jie Du Tang — the most common, general heat-toxin formula.
黃連解毒湯——最常見的通用解熱毒藥方。
Qing Wen Bai Du Yin — can be used to manage COVID-19.
清瘟敗毒飲——可控制新冠。
Pu Ji Xiao Du Yin — can suppress mumps.
普濟消毒飲——可抑制腮腺炎。
解暑熱English中文
Xin Jia Xiang Ru Yin — adds Honeysuckle and Forsythia to the original formula, which treats exterior cold + dampness; the "newly augmented" version treats exterior heat.
新加香薷飲——多了金銀花連翹，原方治外感寒＋濕，新加治外感熱。
清虛熱English中文
Qing Hao Bie Jia Tang — because heat has caused Liver-Kidney yin deficiency.
青蒿鱉甲湯——因為熱造成肝腎陰虛。

火往上，熱沒有固定方向：心火在舌，肝火在眼。

▍EXAM FOCUS — 本組出題重點（17方，分六類）
  • 白虎湯的「四大」症狀（高燒、發汗、口渴、洪脈）與陽明腑證的「痞滿燥實」（見第12組）是陽明經證與陽明腑證的兩大代表，是全書最常考的分類架構之一。
  • 四個層次（衛氣營血）與對應方劑是必考主軸：桑菊飲／銀翹散／麻杏石甘湯（衛）→白虎湯／竹葉石膏湯（氣）→清營湯（營）→犀角地黃湯（血）。
  • 臟腑熱八方依臟腑＋虛實兩個維度整理：心（導赤散）、肝（龍膽瀉肝湯、左金丸）、胃（清胃散＝實，玉女煎＝虛）、肺（瀉白散）、腸（芍藥湯、白頭翁湯）。
  • 清胃散 vs 玉女煎是經典鑑別：清胃散治實火牙痛（急性，臉腫一邊）；玉女煎治虛火牙痛（慢性，牙齒鬆動脫落）。
  • 芍藥湯 vs 白頭翁湯：兩者都治痢疾，但芍藥湯治濕熱痢（赤白相間，兼調氣血）；白頭翁湯治熱毒血痢（赤多白少，純涼血解毒）。
  • 黃連解毒湯是唯一同時清三焦熱（黃連上焦、黃芩中焦、黃柏下焦、梔子通三焦）的基礎方，其變方涼膈散（去黃柏加大黃芒硝）常考。
  • 青蒿鱉甲湯 vs 竹葉石膏湯：青蒿鱉甲湯是血分虛熱末期（肝腎陰虛，夜熱早涼）；竹葉石膏湯是氣分虛熱末期（肺脾氣陰兩虛）——常考「哪個更深層」。
Bai Hu Tang
白虎湯【初期氣分熱肺胃實熱】
White Tiger Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • [Clears Lung/Stomach Qi-level excess heat, herb-pair: Shigao + Zhimu] — Gypsum (Shigao) 30 g; Anemarrhena Rhizome (Zhimu) 9 g
  • Licorice (Gancao) 3 g; Rice (Jingmi) 9 g — benefits the Stomach and generates fluids, so the very cold formula doesn't damage the Stomach
  • 【清肺胃氣分實熱，對藥＝石膏＋知母】— 石膏 30 g；知母 9 g
  • 甘草 3 g；粳米 9 g——益胃生津，使大寒之劑不會傷胃
功效
Functions
Clear away heat and promote the production of body fluids. (Heat at the Qi level — has not yet damaged yin; early-stage Qi-level heat.)
清熱生津。氣分熱——還沒傷到陰；初期氣分熱。
主治
Indication
Excessive heat in the Yangming level — treats Lung and Stomach excess heat.
陽明經熱盛——治肺胃實熱。

歌訣：白虎湯用石膏煨，知母甘草粳米陪，亦有加入人蔘者，躁煩熱渴舌生胎。治發汗後大熱不解，多汗出，不惡寒，大渴能飲水者，此方主之。

臨床表現
Manifestation
  • [The Four Bigs]: fever; sweating; thirst; strong pulse
  • 四大症狀：發熱；出汗；口渴；脈洪大
加減
Modification
  • [Bai Hu Jia Renshen Tang] — treats exuberant Qi-level heat with insufficient fluids/qi: for a large but weak pulse and thirst not quenched by drinking → add Ginseng
  • [Bai Hu Jia Cangzhu Tang] — treats arthritis/rheumatoid heat: for swollen, painful joints → add Atractylodes Rhizome (Cangzhu): dries dampness, strengthens the Spleen
  • Excess heat causing wind with limb spasms → add Uncaria Stem (Gouteng: clears heat, calms the Liver), Earthworm (Dilong: clears high fever, extinguishes wind, calms fright), and Antelope Horn (Lingyangjiao: clears the Liver, brightens the eyes, clears heat and resolves toxin)
  • 【白虎加人蔘湯】：治氣分熱盛而津氣不足：脈大而虛、渴不解者：加人蔘
  • 【白虎加蒼朮湯】：治風濕性關節炎、風濕熱：關節腫痛：加蒼朮（燥濕健脾）
  • 風熱（實熱造成風症，兼四肢痙攣）：加鉤藤（清熱平肝）、地龍（清高熱、息風、定驚）、羚羊角（清肝明目，清熱解毒）
Zhu Ye Shi Gao Tang
竹葉石膏湯【晚期氣分熱氣＆陰兩虛】
Lophatherus and Gypsum Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Clear heat — Gypsum (Shigao) 30 g; Bamboo Leaf (Zhuye) 6 g (replaces Zhimu with Zhuye — similar to Ma Huang Tang omitting Guizhi)
  • Nourish yin — Licorice (Gancao) 3 g — tonifies qi; Rice (Jingmi) 9 g — nourishes yin
  • Ginseng 6 g; Ophiopogon (Maidong) 6 g; Pinellia (Banxia) 6 g — moves qi
  • 清熱 — 石膏 30 g；竹葉 6 g（把知母換成竹葉，很像麻黃湯拿掉桂枝）
  • 養陰 — 甘草 3 g——補氣；粳米 9 g——養陰
  • 人蔘 6 g；麥冬 6 g；半夏 6 g——通氣
功效
Functions
Late-stage Qi-level heat with both qi and yin deficiency.
晚期氣分熱，氣＆陰兩虛。
主治
Indication
Late stage of Yangming heat syndrome with qi and yin deficiency — treats Lung and Stomach empty heat.
陽明熱證晚期，氣陰兩虛——治肺胃虛熱。

歌訣：竹葉石膏湯人蔘，麥冬半夏與同林，甘草生薑兼粳米，暑煩熱渴脈虛尋。陽明熱的最後階段，氣跟陰都虛——氣的最後階段，長期生病一直沒好，導致熱傷到氣跟陰，因此覺得疲勞。

📝 課堂筆記（原文僅中文）
生脈飲；麥門冬湯（見第05組）；清燥救肺湯（見第05組）。

  • Red tongue with a dry coating
  • Nausea
  • Weak, rapid pulse
  • 疲勞（因氣陰都虛，所以很累）
  • 舌紅苔乾
  • 噁心
  • 脈虛數

Qing Ying Tang
清營湯【初期營分熱】
Clear Heat from the Nutritive Level Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Rhinoceros Horn (Xijiao) 3 g (substitute: buffalo horn — Xijiao is strongly effective for reducing fever, clears Blood-level heat)
  • Nourish yin — Fresh Rehmannia Root (Shengdihuang) 15 g; Scrophularia (Xuanshen) 9 g; Ophiopogon Root (Maidong) 9 g
  • Clear heat, resolve toxin ⚠ — Honeysuckle Flower (Jinyinhua) 9 g; Forsythia Fruit (Lianqiao) 6 g (this herb-pair helps draw Blood-level symptoms back to the shallower Wei level)
  • Clear Heart fire — Red Sage Root (Danshen) 6 g: cools blood, calms the spirit, for heat pathogen entering the Heart-Ying; Coptis (Huanglian) 5 g: clears middle-Jiao damp-fire; Bamboo Leaf (Zhuye) 3 g: clears the Heart and purges fire
  • 犀角 3 g（犀角換成牛角，犀角對退熱有強效，清血分熱）
  • 滋陰 — 生地黃 15 g；玄蔘 9 g；麥冬 9 g
  • 清熱解毒 ＊＊＊ — 金銀花 9 g；連翹 6 g（對藥，讓血分症狀退回衛分）
  • 清心火 — 丹蔘 6 g：涼血安神，熱邪入心營；黃連 5 g：清中焦濕火；竹葉 3 g：清心降火
功效
Functions
Clear away heat at the Ying level and nourish yin. (Early-stage Blood-level heat.)
清營解毒、透熱養陰。初期血分熱。
主治
Indication
Heat syndrome at the Ying level — the early stage of the Blood level (Ying). Because heat has entered the Ying level and damaged yin, causing high fever at night but a cold feeling during the day.
營分熱證——血分的早期階段（營）。因為熱已經進入到營分，傷到陰，導致晚上高燒但白天覺得冷。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Fever at night
  • Intermittent delirium
  • Restless
  • Thirsty
  • Indistinct skin rashes
  • Crimson tongue
  • Thready, rapid pulse
  • 夜間發熱
  • 斷斷續續的譫妄
  • 煩躁
  • 口渴
  • 隱約皮疹
  • 舌絳
  • 脈細數
加減
Modification

Substitution example: Elsholtzia (Xiangru) can replace Mahuang; Buffalo Horn can replace Rhinoceros Horn.

藥品替代範例：香薷替換掉麻黃，犀角換成牛角。

Dao Chi San
導赤散【心火】
Guide Out the Red Powder
組成
Ingredients
  • Chief — Fresh Rehmannia Root (Shengdihuang) 10 g: cools blood, nourishes yin
  • Chief — Clematis Stem (Mutong) 10 g: directly drains Heart fire, bitter-cold, guides heat out through urination
  • Raw Licorice (Shenggancao) 10 g; Bamboo Leaf (Zhuye) 10 g: enters the Heart, can clear Heart fire
  • 君 — 生地黃 10 g：涼血滋陰
  • 君 — 木通 10 g：直接降心火，寒苦，將熱導至尿
  • 生甘草 10 g；竹葉 10 g：入心，可清心火
功效
Functions
Clear Heart fire, promote urination, and nourish yin. (Treats acute symptoms by guiding Heart fire away, nourishing yin to lower fire. If the heat sign is not very severe, the dose of Shengdihuang can be increased to nourish yin and lower fire. Mutong and Fangji can damage the Kidney and should not be taken in large amounts for long periods. Two organs: Heart + Small Intestine.)
清心利水養陰。治療緊急症狀，將心火導走、滋陰以降火。如果熱象沒有非常嚴重，可以加重生地黃滋陰以降火。木通、防己會傷腎，不可長期大量服用。（兩個臟器：心＋小腸）
主治
Indication
Intense heat syndrome in the Heart Meridian–Small Intestine.
心經、小腸實熱證。

歌訣：導赤生地與木通，草梢竹葉四般攻，口糜淋痛小腸火，引熱同歸小便中。心會將熱傳給小腸經，導致尿深色＋排尿疼痛。

【比較】八正散（見第07組）；小薊飲子（見第04組）。

臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Irritability
  • Feverish sensation in the chest
  • Thirst
  • Flushed face
  • Preference for cold drinks
  • Oral ulceration
  • Or dark urine with difficult, painful urination
  • Red tongue with a thin, yellowish coating
  • Rapid pulse
  • Insomnia
  • 心煩
  • 胸中發熱感
  • 口渴
  • 面赤
  • 喜冷飲
  • 口舌生瘡
  • 或尿色深、排尿澀痛
  • 舌紅苔薄黃
  • 脈數
  • 失眠
加減
Modification
  • Severe ulceration of the mouth and tongue → add Coptis (Huanglian, the bitterest herb)
  • Severe urinary tract infection → add Small Thistle (Xiaoji), Capejasmine Fruit (Zhizi), Plantain Seed (Cheqianzi)
  • 嘴破舌破（嚴重）：加黃連（最苦的藥）
  • 尿道炎（嚴重）：加小薊、梔子、車前子
Long Dan Xie Gan Tang
龍膽瀉肝湯【肝火＆熱】
Gentiana Decoction to Drain the Liver
組成
Ingredients
  • Remove Liver fire/heat — Chinese Gentian (Longdancao) 6 g; Scutellaria Root (Huangqin) 9 g: removes upper-Jiao heat; Capejasmine Fruit (Zhizi) 9 g: removes Triple-Burner heat
  • Promote urination — Alismatis Rhizome (Zexie) 12 g; Clematis Stem (Mutong) 9 g; Plantain Seed (Cheqianzi) 9 g
  • Nourish yin and tonify blood — Chinese Angelica Root (Danggui) 3 g: tonifies blood; Fresh Rehmannia Root (Shengdihuang) 6 g: nourishes yin
  • Guide and harmonize — Bupleurum Root (Chaihu) 6 g: regulates the Liver, guides the herbs' effect into the Liver; Licorice (Gancao) 6 g: harmonizes
  • 去肝火熱 — 龍膽草 6 g；黃芩 9 g（去上焦熱）；梔子 9 g（去三焦熱）
  • 利尿 — 澤瀉 12 g；木通 9 g；車前子 9 g
  • 滋陰補血 — 當歸 3 g（補血）；生地黃 6 g（滋陰）
  • 引導調和 — 柴胡 6 g（調肝，引導藥性入肝）；甘草 6 g（調和）
功效
Functions
Clear excessive heat and dampness in the Liver and Gallbladder. (Liver damp-fire; damp-heat flows downward to the reproductive system.)
清瀉肝膽實火、清利肝經濕熱。肝濕火，濕熱下注至生殖系統。
主治
Indication
Flaring up of Liver fire (fire rushes to the head), damp-heat in the lower Jiao (damp-heat flows to the lower limbs/genitals).
肝火上炎（火往頭部跑），下焦濕熱（濕熱往下肢跑）。

歌訣：龍膽瀉肝梔芩柴，生地車前澤瀉偕，木通甘草當歸合，肝經濕熱力能排。常發生頭部各種痛（火向上），生殖系統的濕熱症狀（damp heat往下）。

臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Headache
  • Hypochondriac pain
  • Bitter taste in the mouth
  • Deafness
  • Ear pain (or high-pitched tinnitus)
  • Turbid urine
  • Leukorrhagia
  • Red tongue with a thick, yellowish coating
  • Wiry, rapid pulse

Because of the distribution of the tinnitus and the Gallbladder channel, the back of the neck and shoulder often become swollen; GB20 is often a tender point, with pain radiating from the neck up to the ear along with severe headache/temporal headache.

  • 頭痛
  • 脅肋痛
  • 口苦
  • 耳聾
  • 耳痛（或高頻耳鳴）
  • 尿混濁
  • 白帶增多
  • 舌紅苔厚黃
  • 脈弦數

因為耳鳴跟膽經的分佈，常常後頸跟上肩腫脹，GB20會是一個痛點，常常從脖子往上痛到耳朵跟劇烈頭痛／頭側痛。

Zuo Jin Wan
左金丸【肝火犯胃胃酸上逆】
Left Metal Pill
組成
Ingredients
  • Coptis (Huanglian) 18 g — a bitter-cold herb, enters the middle-Jiao Heart/Liver/Stomach/Large Intestine
  • Evodia Fruit (Wuzhuyu) 3 g — a warm herb, enters the Liver

Ratio: Huanglian:Wuzhuyu = 6:1, keeping Huanglian's bitter-cold nature from being too strong; often used together with other formulas.

  • 黃連 18 g——苦寒藥，走中焦心肝胃大腸
  • 吳茱萸 3 g——溫藥，入肝

黃連：吳茱萸＝6：1，讓黃連的寒苦不至於過強；常常跟其他藥一起用。

功效
Functions
Clear Liver fire and stop vomiting. (Used for a special situation: Liver fire damaging the Stomach, causing acid reflux. Two organs: Liver + Stomach.)
清肝瀉火、降逆止嘔。用於特殊的狀況，肝火犯胃造成胃酸上逆。（兩個臟器：肝＋胃）
主治
Indication
Liver fire.
肝火。

歌訣：左金茱連六一丸，肝經火鬱吐吞酸，再加芍藥名戊己，熱瀉熱痢服之安。連附六一治胃痛，寒因熱用理一般。

臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Liver fire damaging the Stomach
  • Acid reflux
  • Bitter taste and vomiting
  • Red tongue with yellow coating
  • Wiry, rapid pulse
  • 肝火犯胃
  • 逆流吞酸
  • 口苦嘔吐
  • 舌紅苔黃
  • 脈弦數
加減
Modification
  • Fixed 6:1 ratio of Huanglian to Wuzhuyu keeps Huanglian's bitter-cold nature from being too strong; often used together with other formulas.
  • 【黃連6：1吳茱萸】固定比例讓黃連的寒苦不至於過強，常常跟其他藥一起用
Qing Wei San
清胃散【胃實火牙齦腫痛】
Clear the Stomach Powder
組成
Ingredients
  • Coptis (Huanglian) 3–5 g
  • Fresh Rehmannia Root (Shengdihuang) 12 g — nourishes yin, cools blood
  • Moutan Bark (Mudanpi) 9 g — cools blood
  • Chinese Angelica Root (Danggui) 6 g — aids circulation
  • Cimicifuga (Shengma) 6 g — moves upward, follows Yangming fire upward, "opens up" the vertex to let heat escape
  • 黃連 3–5 g
  • 生地黃 12 g——滋陰涼血
  • 牡丹皮 9 g——涼血
  • 當歸 6 g——幫助循環
  • 升麻 6 g——往上走，跟著陽明火往上走，打開頭頂讓熱出去
功效
Functions
Clear heat in the Stomach; cool the blood. (Acute gum pain [excess fire]: acute, very painful, one side of the face swells. If gum pain is from empty fire, there will be diarrhea symptoms instead. Fire rises upward, whereas "heat" is everywhere, with no up/down direction. Shengma has no tonifying action; Huangqi does.)
清胃涼血。急性的牙齦疼痛（實火）：急性、很痛、臉會腫一邊。如果牙齦疼痛是虛火，會有腹瀉症狀。火往上，熱則是到處都是沒有上下。升麻沒有補的作用，黃耆有補的作用。
主治
Indication
Toothache due to fire in the Stomach.
因胃火所致的牙痛。

歌訣：清胃散用升麻連，當歸生地牡丹全，或益石膏平胃熱，口瘡吐衄及牙宣。

臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Headache arising from toothache
  • Feverish cheeks
  • Aversion to heat with a preference for cold
  • Gingival bleeding or ulceration of the gums
  • Or swelling and soreness of the tongue, lips, and cheeks
  • Hot, foul breath
  • Dryness of the mouth and tongue
  • Red tongue with a yellowish coating
  • Smooth, rapid pulse
  • 牙痛連及頭痛
  • 面頰發熱
  • 惡熱喜冷
  • 牙齦出血或潰瘍
  • 或舌頭、嘴唇、臉頰腫痛
  • 口氣熱臭
  • 口舌乾燥
  • 舌紅苔黃
  • 脈滑數
加減
Modification
  • Bleeding gums → add Cyathula Root (Niuxi) — sends qi downward
  • Severely swollen gums → add Honeysuckle Flower (Jinyinhua)
  • Constipation → add Rhubarb (Dahuang)
  • 牙齦流血：加牛膝（會讓氣往下走）
  • 牙齦腫（嚴重）：加金銀花
  • 便祕：加大黃
Yu Nu Jian
玉女煎【陰虛胃火牙齦疼痛】
Jade Woman Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Gypsum (Shigao) 15 g — clears heat
  • [All yin-nourishing herbs] — Prepared Rehmannia Root (Shudihuang) 9 g; Ophiopogon Root (Maidong) 6 g; Anemarrhena Rhizome (Zhimu) 6 g
  • Achyranthes (Niuxi) 6 g
  • 石膏 15 g——清熱
  • 【全為滋陰的藥】— 熟地黃 9 g；麥冬 6 g；知母 6 g
  • 牛膝 6 g
功效
Functions
Clear Stomach heat and replenish yin. (Mainly for chronic toothache [empty heat] due to Stomach heat. Because yin deficiency causes yang qi to rise, one must nourish yin so that yang qi descends. To judge whether it is yin deficiency: is there bleeding, are teeth loose or falling out. Compared to Qing Hao Bie Jia Tang, Yu Nu Jian has no fever.)
清胃熱、滋腎陰。主要是為了慢性的牙齦疼痛（虛熱），因為胃熱；因陰虛造成陽氣上升，所以要滋陰讓陽氣往下降。判斷是否陰虛：是否流血、牙鬆動或落齒。跟青蒿鱉甲湯相比，玉女煎沒有fever。
主治
Indication
Stomach heat and yin deficiency.
胃熱兼陰虛。

【比較】玉液湯（見第05組）；玉女煎。

臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Toothache and loosening of teeth
  • Dysphoria with thirst
  • Or diabetes
  • Polyorexia (or loss of appetite)
  • Dry, red tongue with a dry, yellowish coating
  • Thready, rapid pulse

Diabetes (Xiao Ke) is due to Stomach yin deficiency, so Yu Nu Jian can also treat diabetes. Drinking excessively: upper Jiao, Lung yin deficiency, "upper wasting." Eating excessively: middle Jiao, Spleen-Stomach yin deficiency, "middle wasting." Urinating excessively: lower Jiao, Kidney deficiency, "lower wasting."

  • 牙痛及牙齒鬆動
  • 心煩口渴
  • 或消渴症
  • 食慾異常（或厭食）
  • 舌紅而乾，苔黃燥
  • 脈細數

糖尿病（消渴症）是因胃陰虛，所以玉女煎也可以治糖尿病。喝多：上焦，肺陰虛，上消。吃多：中焦，脾胃陰虛，中消。尿多：下焦，腎虛，下消。

加減
Modification
（原始資料未列加減內容）
（原始資料未列加減內容）
Xie Bai San
瀉白散【長期咳嗽肺熱累積】
Drain the White Powder
組成
Ingredients
  • Mulberry Bark (Sangbaipi) 30 g — clears empty heat
  • Wolfberry Bark (Digupi) 30 g
  • Licorice (Gancao) 3 g
  • Rice (Jingmi) 3 g
  • 桑白皮 30 g——清虛熱
  • 地骨皮 30 g
  • 甘草 3 g
  • 粳米 3 g
功效
Functions
Drain heat from the Lung to stop cough. (Used for treating chronic, interior symptoms — clears empty heat but has no tonifying action. This treats long-accumulated interior Lung heat, which over time causes yin deficiency and afternoon fever; very commonly used after Ma Xing Shi Gan Tang [the final Qi-level stage]. Children easily progress quickly from exterior to interior, and this is also mainly used for deficiency patterns. Can be used for COPD [Chronic Obstructive Pulmonary Disease]. Exterior progression: Sang Ju Yin → Yin Qiao San → Ma Xing Shi Gan Tang → [Xie Bai San, interior]. Zhu Ye Shi Gao Tang is qi-and-yin deficiency, but still classified as exterior/Qi-level.)
瀉肺熱、止咳平喘。用於治療長期內在症狀，清虛熱但沒有補的作用；這是治長期累積的內在肺熱，長期導致陰虛而下午發燒，很常接在麻杏石甘湯（氣分最後階段）後使用。小孩很容易症況快速從表走到裡，也是治虛的用處居多。可用於COPD（慢性阻塞性肺臟疾病）。外感進程：桑菊飲→銀翹散→麻杏石甘湯→瀉白散（內在）。竹葉石膏湯是氣陰都虛，但一樣在外感範疇。
主治
Indication
Prolonged cough due to heat accumulated in the Lung with yin damaged (referring to Kidney yin, not Lung yin — mainly for children).
因熱蘊肺而傷陰所致的久咳（此處指腎陰，非肺陰——主要用於兒童）。

歌訣：瀉白桑皮地骨皮，甘草粳米四般宜，蔘茯知芩皆可入，肺炎喘嗽此方施。例如新冠後期久咳不癒。

臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Cough
  • Fever especially in the afternoon (because heat has long accumulated in the Lung, causing yin deficiency)
  • Red tongue with little coating
  • Weak, rapid pulse
  • 咳嗽
  • 發熱，尤以下午為甚（因為熱長期累積在肺，導致陰虛）
  • 舌紅少苔
  • 脈虛數
加減
Modification
  • More severe heat → add Scutellaria (Huangqin), Zhimu/Shigao+Zhimu
  • Dry cough → add Fritillary Bulb (Beimu), Trichosanthes Fruit (Gualou)
  • Empty heat progressing from Yangming to Shaoyang (e.g. pulmonary TB, cough + flushed face in the afternoon) with more severe Lung yin deficiency → add Sweet Wormwood (Qinghao, specifically for Shaoyang), Turtle Shell (Biejia)
  • Thirst → add Trichosanthes Root (Tianhuafen), Zhimu
  • 高熱：加黃芩、知母／石膏＋知母
  • 乾咳：加貝母、栝樓
  • 從陽明轉少陽的虛熱（例如肺結核，咳嗽＋下午臉潮紅）：肺陰虛較重者：加青蒿（專門少陽）、鱉甲
  • 口渴：加天花粉、知母
Shao Yao Tang
芍藥湯【腸濕熱毒】
Peony Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • White Peony Root (Baishao) 15–30 g
  • Coptis (Huanglian) 3–9 g
  • Scutellaria (Huangqin) 6–12 g
  • Rhubarb (Dahuang) 3–6 g
  • Chinese Angelica Root (Danggui) 3–9 g
  • Areca Seed (Binglang) 3–6 g
  • Aucklandia Root (Muxiang) 3–6 g
  • Cinnamon Bark (Rougui) 2–5 g
  • Baked Licorice (Gancao) 2–5 g
  • 芍藥 15–30 g
  • 黃連 3–9 g
  • 黃芩 6–12 g
  • 大黃 3–6 g
  • 當歸 3–9 g
  • 檳榔 3–6 g
  • 木香 3–6 g
  • 肉桂 2–5 g
  • 甘草（炙）2–5 g
功效
Functions
Clear heat and regulate blood; guide out stagnation and stop pain. For dysenteric discharge of thick, purulent blood, very severe abdominal pain, tenesmus, burning sensation at the anus, short/dark urine.
清熱理血、導滯止痛。下痢膿血粘稠、腹痛甚劇、裡急後重、肛門灼熱、小便短赤。
主治
Indication
Early-stage dysentery of an excess pattern without an exterior pattern — besides clearing heat, it also harmonizes qi and blood, and unblocks the bowels to guide out stagnation.
痢疾初期屬實證而無表證者，除有清熱作用外，尚能調和氣血、通便導滯。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Damp-heat dysentery with tenesmus
  • Stool mixed with red and white (blood and mucus)
  • Greasy, yellow tongue coating
  • Red tongue, rapid pulse
  • 濕熱痢裡急後重
  • 大便赤白相間
  • 苔黃膩
  • 舌紅脈數

Bai Tou Weng Tang
白頭翁湯【腸熱毒血】
Pulsatilla Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Chief — Pulsatilla Root (Baitouweng) 2 liang: clears heat at the Blood level, cools blood and resolves toxin, the key herb for treating heat-toxin dysentery with fresh blood
  • Coptis (Huanglian) 3 liang: clears heat, resolves toxin, dries dampness, treats dysentery
  • Phellodendron Bark (Huangbai) 3 liang: clears heat, resolves toxin, dries dampness, treats dysentery
  • Ash Bark (Qinpi) 3 liang: clears heat, dries dampness, and also has an astringent effect
  • 君 — 白頭翁 2 兩：清血分之熱、涼血解毒，為治熱毒赤痢之要藥
  • 黃連 3 兩：清熱解毒、燥濕治痢
  • 黃蘗（黃柏）3 兩：清熱解毒、燥濕治痢
  • 秦皮 3 兩：清熱燥濕兼有收澀之效
功效
Functions
Clear heat, resolve toxin; cool blood and stop dysentery.
清熱解毒、涼血止痢。
主治
Indication
Treats heat-type dysentery with tenesmus, and diarrhea with a desire to drink water. Heat-toxin blood-dysentery pattern: abdominal pain, tenesmus, burning sensation at the anus, discharge of pus and blood, more red than white, thirst with desire to drink water.
治熱利下重，及下利慾飲水者主之。熱毒血痢證：腹痛，裏急後重、肛門灼熱，瀉下膿血，赤多白少，渴欲飲水。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Heat dysentery
  • Abdominal pain
  • Stool mixed with pus and blood
  • Yellow tongue coating
  • Wiry, rapid pulse
  • 熱痢
  • 腹痛
  • 便挾膿血
  • 苔黃
  • 脈弦數
加減

（原始資料未列加減內容）
Huang Lian Jie Du Tang
黃連解毒湯【基礎解熱】
Coptis Decoction to Relieve Toxicity
組成
Ingredients
  • Coptis (Huanglian) 9 g — upper-Jiao heat
  • Scutellaria Root (Huangqin) 6 g — middle-Jiao heat
  • Phellodendron Bark (Huangbai) 6 g — lower-Jiao heat
  • Capejasmine Fruit (Zhizi) 9 g — clears heat throughout the Triple Burner channel
  • 黃連 9 g——上焦熱
  • 黃芩 6 g——中焦熱
  • 黃柏 6 g——下焦熱
  • 梔子 9 g——清三焦經的熱
功效
Functions
Purge fire and release toxins. (Only Huang Lian Jie Du Tang covers all three Burners, removing heat from all three at once.)
瀉火解毒。只有黃連解毒湯有包含三個焦，去三焦熱。
主治
Indication
Excessive toxic-heat syndromes of the Triple Burner ("toxin" here means fire-heat) — treats Triple-Burner excess heat.
三焦實熱毒盛之證（毒，即火熱也）——治三焦實熱。

歌訣：黃連解毒湯四味，黃柏黃芩梔子備，躁狂大熱嘔不眠，吐衄斑黃均可使。若云三黃石膏湯，再加麻黃及淡豉，此為傷寒溫毒盛，三焦表裏相兼治。梔子金花加大黃，潤腸瀉熱真堪倚。最基礎的解熱藥，沒有特殊類別的熱，可以再加其他藥以針對症狀。解熱：黃芩、黃連、黃柏。滋陰：玄蔘、麥冬、生地。治三焦經上的熱。

臨床表現
Manifestation
  • High fever
  • Restlessness (upper Jiao)
  • Thirst (upper Jiao)
  • Delirium (upper Jiao)
  • Insomnia (upper Jiao)
  • Or dysentery (lower) with fever
  • Or sores and carbuncles (heat-toxin)
  • Red tongue with a yellowish coating
  • Rapid, forceful pulse
  • 高燒
  • 煩躁不安（上焦）
  • 口渴（上焦）
  • 譫妄（上焦）
  • 失眠（上焦）
  • 或痢疾（下焦）兼發熱
  • 或瘡瘍（熱毒）
  • 舌紅苔黃
  • 脈數有力
加減
Modification
  • Jaundice → add Oriental Wormwood (Yinchenhao), Rhubarb (Dahuang)
  • Severe macula → add Moutan Bark (Mudanpi), Fresh Rehmannia Root (Shengdihuang)
  • Red, peeled tongue → add Scrophularia (Xuanshen), Fresh Rehmannia Root (Shengdihuang), Ophiopogon Root (Maidong)
  • [Liang Ge San] — accumulated heat in the upper and middle Burners: for constipation, replace Huangbai with Rhubarb (Dahuang) and Mirabilite (Mangxiao), forming Liang Ge San (Cool the Diaphragm Powder) — cools the diaphragm and eliminates heat, purges fire and unblocks the bowels (heat accumulated in the upper Jiao diaphragm, so it needs to be sent downward)
  • 黃疸：加茵陳蒿、大黃
  • 斑疹（嚴重）：加牡丹皮、生地黃
  • 紅剝皮舌：加玄蔘、生地黃、麥冬
  • 【涼膈散】：上、中二焦積熱：便秘者，以大黃、芒硝取代黃柏，成涼膈散——涼膈除熱，瀉火通便（熱積在上焦橫膈膜，所以要讓往下走）
Qing Wen Bai Du Yin
清瘟敗毒飲【新冠溫病】
Decoction to Relieve Toxin for Pandemic Disease
組成
Ingredients
  • [Huang Lian Jie Du Tang — heat & toxin] — Coptis (Huanglian) 9 g: clears heat throughout the body; Scutellaria (Huangqin) 9 g; Capejasmine Fruit (Zhizi) 9 g
  • [Bai Hu Tang — for fever] — Gypsum (Shigao) 30 g: heat moving to the Qi level; Anemarrhena Rhizome (Zhimu) 9 g
  • [Xi Jiao Di Huang Tang — rapidly moves heat toward the Blood level] — Rhinoceros Horn (Xijiao) 6 g: because heat can rapidly reach the Blood level; Fresh Rehmannia Root (Shengdihuang) 9 g; Red Peony (Chishao) 9 g; Moutan Bark (Mudanpi) 9 g
  • [For sore throat] — Scrophularia (Xuanshen) 9 g: relaxes the throat; Platycodon Root (Jiegeng)
  • Licorice (Gancao)
  • 【黃連解毒湯（熱與毒）】— 黃連 9 g：全身清熱；黃芩 9 g；梔子 9 g
  • 【白虎湯（發燒）】— 石膏 30 g：熱跑到氣分；知母 9 g
  • 【犀角地黃湯（快速使熱轉去血分）】— 犀角 6 g：因為熱會快速跑到血分；生地黃 9 g；赤芍 9 g；牡丹皮 9 g
  • 【喉嚨痛】— 玄蔘 9 g：可鬆頸；桔梗
  • 甘草
功效
Functions
General heat & toxin.
通治熱與毒。
主治
Indication
Specifically handles COVID-19-type symptoms — the most basic combination, which can be modified according to symptoms, e.g. only add Shigao if there is fever. When symptoms shift toward Shaoyang, Xiao Chai Hu Tang can be added. When difficulty breathing appears, Ma Xing Shi Gan Tang can be added.
專門處理新冠的症狀，最基礎的組合，可依照症狀加減，例如：有發燒才加石膏。當症狀轉向少陽，可加小柴胡湯。當症狀出現呼吸困難，可加麻杏石甘湯。
臨床表現
Manifestation
（原始資料此欄留空；症狀對應主治：熱毒瀰漫氣血兩燔）
（原始資料此欄留空；症狀對應主治：熱毒瀰漫氣血兩燔）
加減
Modification
（見上方主治欄加減說明）
（見上方主治欄加減說明）

Xin Jia Xiang Ru Yin
新加香薷飲【暑熱】
Newly Augmented Mosla Drink
組成
Ingredients
  • [Xiang Ru Yin base] — Elsholtzia (Xiangru) 15 g: clears summer-heat; Hyacinth Bean (Biandou) 12 g: strengthens the Spleen, resolves dampness, stops diarrhea/discharge; Magnolia Bark (Houpo) 12 g: sends qi downward
  • Honeysuckle Flower (Jinyinhua) 9 g — resolves exterior heat, resolves heat-toxin, treats the throat
  • Forsythia Fruit (Lianqiao) 9 g — resolves exterior heat, resolves heat-toxin, treats the throat
  • 【香薷飲】— 香薷 15 g（清暑熱）；扁豆 12 g（健脾化濕，止瀉、止帶）；厚朴 12 g（讓氣往下）
  • 金銀花 9 g——解外感熱，解熱毒及治喉嚨
  • 連翹 9 g——解外感熱，解熱毒及治喉嚨
功效
Functions
Eliminate summer-heat and release the exterior; resolve dampness and harmonize the Spleen and Stomach.
祛暑解表；化濕和中。
主治
Indication
Summer-heat syndrome — summer-heat begins with diarrhea, then progresses to alternating chills and fever. (Typical exterior symptoms usually start with cough [Sang Ju Yin → Yin Qiao San]. Xiangru can substitute for Mahuang: 1. when Mahuang cannot be used; 2. for treating summer-heat exterior symptoms, it is more suitable than Mahuang.)
暑熱證——暑熱從拉肚子開始，再往來寒熱。（一般外感的症狀從咳嗽開始，桑菊飲→銀翹散。香薷可替換麻黃：1. 當麻黃不能用；2. 治療暑熱這種外感症狀，會比麻黃更適合。）

Qing Hao Bie Jia Tang
青蒿鱉甲湯【熱造成陰虛火】
Artemisia Annua and Turtle Shell Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Sweet Wormwood (Qinghao) 15 g — clears Shaoyang empty heat
  • Turtle Shell (Biejia) 15 g — nourishes yin
  • Anemarrhena Rhizome (Zhimu) 9 g — nourishes yin
  • Fresh Rehmannia Root (Shengdihuang) 12 g — nourishes yin
  • Moutan Bark (Mudanpi) 9 g — clears heat, cools blood
  • 青蒿 15 g——清少陽虛熱
  • 鱉甲 15 g——滋陰
  • 知母 9 g——滋陰
  • 生地黃 12 g——滋陰
  • 牡丹皮 9 g——清熱涼血
功效
Functions
Nourish yin and clear heat.
滋陰清熱、透邪外出。
主治
Indication
Heat syndrome at the late stage causing Liver and Kidney yin deficiency. Can occur at any age, though it's more common in the elderly. Empty fire is a chronic condition, whereas excess fire is acute.
因熱造成的肝腎陰虛晚期熱證。可發生在任何年紀，年紀大的人更容易發生；虛火是長期症狀，實火是急症。
📝 課堂筆記（原文僅中文）
青蒿鱉甲湯：熱傷氣陰，陰虛（肝腎）末期，有明顯的陰虛症狀，如夜熱但白天不熱或冷。竹葉石膏湯（見上方卡片）：熱傷氣陰，氣分（肺脾）末期。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Feeling hot at night but cold (or normal) during the day (because of yin deficiency)
  • No sweating
  • Red tongue with little coating
  • Weak, rapid pulse

Fever symptoms do appear here (unlike Yu Nu Jian, which has no fever).

  • 夜熱早涼（因陰虛）
  • 無汗
  • 舌紅少苔
  • 脈虛數

會有發熱的症狀出現（玉女煎沒有發熱）。
