15 · Warming Interior 溫裡劑

▍溫裡常用藥 Herbs for Warming the Interior

English中文
Prepared Aconite Root (Fuzi) — toxic, a great heat herb.
附子——有毒，大熱藥。
Dried Ginger (Ganjiang) — warms the Spleen, can reduce the dose of Fuzi to lessen side effects. Fresh ginger stops vomiting, is spicier and more exterior-focused. Dried ginger is for warming.
乾薑——溫脾，可以減少附子的量以減少副作用。生薑是止嘔，較辣比較針對外感。乾薑是為了溫暖。
Cinnamon Bark (Rougui) — can warm the Kidney. Guizhi is more dispersing, more fragrant/spicy, treats the limbs. Shaoyin patterns use Rougui.
肉桂——可以溫腎。桂枝比較發散，比較香辣，治療四肢。少陰症狀用肉桂。
Evodia Fruit (Wuzhuyu) — treats headache and warming, specifically treats Jueyin.
吳茱萸——頭痛跟溫，專門治厥陰。
Asarum Herb (Xixin) — a great heat herb, makes things hot; can treat toothache, very pungent, has a numbing effect; can be added to moxibustion.
細辛——大熱藥，讓東西變熱；可治療牙痛，很辛辣，有麻痺作用；溫灸可以加細辛。

▍四肢冷三方鑑別 Three Formulas for Cold Limbs

English中文
Si Ni San (see Group 13) — treats chronic Liver qi stagnation (excess) invading the Spleen, causing cold limbs, usually with a tendency toward depression.
四逆散（見第13組）——治長期的肝氣滯（實）犯脾導致四肢寒冷，通常有憂鬱傾向。
Si Ni Tang — treats acute internal-organ cold limbs due to yang collapse.
四逆湯——治療急性的臟腑問題，因為陽脫導致的四肢寒冷。
Li Zhong Tang — cold limbs due to Taiyin Spleen deficiency.
理中湯——太陰脾虛造成的四肢寒冷。
Dang Gui Si Ni Tang (see Group 16) — cold limbs from yang deficiency and blood deficiency, with cold lodged in the channels — needs to warm the channels and unblock the vessels.
當歸四逆湯（見第16組）——陽虛血虛的寒氣卡在經絡的四肢寒冷，需要溫經通脈。
Wen Pi Tang (see Group 12) — cold accumulation from Spleen yang deficiency causing constipation + deficient-type cold limbs.
溫脾湯（見第12組）——脾陽虛造成的寒氣累積造成便秘＋體虛四肢寒冷。

▍小建中湯 vs 桂枝湯 Xiao Jian Zhong Tang and Gui Zhi Tang

English中文
Xiao Jian Zhong Tang — a modified variant of Gui Zhi Tang, doubling the Baishao, because it is more warming internally, given to weak-bodied people.
小建中湯——桂枝湯變方，白芍加倍，因為較為溫暖內部，給體虛的人用。
Gui Zhi Tang (see Group 10) — exterior wind-cold, Taiyang wind.
桂枝湯（見第10組）——外感風寒，太陽風。

▍理中丸 vs 吳茱萸湯（治腹痛） Li Zhong Wan and Wu Zhu Yu Tang

English中文
Li Zhong Wan — treats the Taiyin pattern, more related to the Spleen, deficiency-cold, tonifies qi and strengthens the Spleen.
理中丸——治太陰症，跟脾較相關，虛寒，補氣健脾。
Wu Zhu Yu Tang — Jueyin vertex headache + vomiting; Shaoyin Spleen and Jueyin Liver deficiency-cold invading the Stomach (Stomach deficiency-cold pattern). Warms the middle and tonifies deficiency, disperses yin and supports yang.
吳茱萸湯——厥陰頭頂痛＋嘔吐，少陰脾與厥陰肝虛寒犯胃（胃虛寒證）。溫中補虛，消陰扶陽。

▍太陰／厥陰／少陰系列方劑 Taiyin / Jueyin / Shaoyin Formula Series

太陰 TaiyinEnglish中文
Li Zhong Wan — Taiyin Spleen deficiency, diarrhea.
理中丸——太陰脾虛，腹瀉。
Xiao Jian Zhong Tang — deficiency-type abdominal pain and diarrhea.
小建中湯——虛型腹瀉腹痛。
Da Jian Zhong Tang — severe abdominal pain and diarrhea.
大建中湯——嚴重的腹瀉腹痛。
厥陰 JueyinEnglish中文
Wu Zhu Yu Tang — vertex headache + vomiting.
吳茱萸湯——頭頂痛＋嘔吐。
少陰 ShaoyinEnglish中文
Si Ni Tang — yang collapse.
四逆湯——陽脫。
Hui Yang Jiu Ji Tang (cross-reference, a modified variant of Si Ni Tang) — Shaoyin pattern.
回陽救急湯（Si Ni Tang變方，跨方參照）——少陰症。
▍EXAM FOCUS — 本組出題重點
  • 理中丸是四君子湯的變方：把茯苓（利水，讓氣往下）換成乾薑（溫暖），因為理中湯證已經在腹瀉，若再用茯苓會加重腹瀉——這個「換藥邏輯」是常考重點。
  • 小建中湯／大建中湯／理中丸的嚴重程度階梯：理中丸（一般脾虛腹瀉）→小建中湯（虛勞腹痛，桂枝湯白芍加倍加飴糖）→大建中湯（嚴重虛寒腹痛，用蜀椒，痛到手不可近）。
  • 吳茱萸湯可治陽明、少陰、厥陰三經之病（乾嘔吐涎沫頭痛屬厥陰；吐利手足厥冷屬少陰；食谷欲嘔屬陽明）——是三經共治的特殊代表方，常考。
  • 四逆湯的煎煮法特殊：附子須先煮一小時，再加入其他藥同煎——考試偶爾考服藥/煎藥細節。
  • 四逆湯的變方系列：人蔘四逆湯（脈極弱加人蔘）／通脈四逆湯（虛熱加倍乾薑）／真武湯（加白朮茯苓治水腫）／回陽救急湯（加人蔘肉桂麝香治昏迷）——常考哪個症狀對應哪個變方。
Li Zhong Wan
理中丸【太陰脾虛】
Regulate the Middle Pill
組成
Ingredients
  • Dried Ginger (Ganjiang) 5 g (Si Junzi Tang with Fuling replaced by Ganjiang, giving it a warming effect)
  • Ginseng (Renshen) 6 g
  • White Atractylodes (Baizhu) 9 g
  • Baked Licorice (Zhigancao) 6 g
  • 乾薑 5 g（四君子湯的茯苓換成乾薑，變成溫的效果）
  • 人蔘 6 g
  • 白朮 9 g
  • 炙甘草 6 g
老師補充 ＆＆
理中湯因為已經氣往下腹瀉，如果再用茯苓（氣往下），就沒有止瀉作用，因此要用乾薑暖胃。如果要額外止痛，可加附子。
功效
Functions
Warm the middle Jiao and dispel cold; tonify qi and strengthen the Spleen. (Li Zhong Tang treats the Taiyin pattern, more related to the Spleen; abdominal pain is relieved by warming the middle Jiao. Li Zhong Wan can also treat excessive menstrual flow — dark blood indicates Spleen yang deficiency, pale blood indicates Spleen qi deficiency. Gui Pi Tang mainly regulates qi rather than warming the middle; Li Zhong Wan warms the middle.)
溫中散寒；補氣健脾。理中湯是治太陰症，跟脾較相關。腹痛，溫中焦會舒緩。理中丸也可以治療月經量過多（脾陽虛血深，脾氣虛血淡）。歸脾湯主要是理氣，不是溫中，理中丸溫中。
主治
Indication
Deficiency-cold syndrome of the Spleen and Stomach (Taiyin pattern) — treats cholera-type vomiting and diarrhea, with more cold and no desire to drink.
脾胃虛寒證（太陰症）——治霍亂病嘔吐泄利，寒多不飲水者。

歌訣：理中湯主理中鄉，甘草人蔘朮黑薑，嘔利腹痛陰寒盛，或加附子總扶陽。①肚子痛、腹瀉；②胸緊（陽虛）；③月經過量（脾陽虛的顏色比較深，脾氣虛的顏色比較淡）。

【比較】四君子湯（補的效果）：人蔘、白朮、茯苓（換成乾薑就是理中丸）、甘草。

臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Epigastric and abdominal pain
  • Preference for warmth and pressure
  • Cold limbs
  • Poor appetite
  • Vomiting
  • Diarrhea
  • Bleeding
  • Chest tightness
  • Pale tongue with whitish coating
  • Deep, weak pulse
  • 脘腹疼痛
  • 喜溫喜按
  • 四肢冷
  • 食慾不振
  • 嘔吐
  • 腹瀉
  • 出血
  • 胸悶
  • 舌淡苔白
  • 脈沉弱
加減
Modification
  • Severe vomiting → replace Baizhu with Fresh Ginger (Shengjiang) and Banxia
  • [Fuzi Li Zhong Wan] — severe abdominal pain and distension → add Prepared Aconite Root (Fuzi)
  • Severe diarrhea → add Dioscorea (Shanyao), Hyacinth Bean (Biandou) to drain dampness
  • Loss of blood due to yang deficiency → add Donkey Hide Gelatin (Ejiao), Mugwort Leaf (Aiye, for moxibustion)
  • Combined with exterior syndrome → add Guizhi
  • 嘔吐（嚴重）：以生薑、半夏取代白朮
  • 【附子理中湯】：腹痛嚴重：加附子
  • 去濕（腹瀉嚴重）：加山藥、扁豆
  • 因陽虛而失血：加阿膠、溫灸艾葉
  • 外感風寒：加桂枝
Xiao Jian Zhong Tang
小建中湯【太陰】
Minor Construct the Middle Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • [A modified variant of Gui Zhi Tang] — Cinnamon Twigs (Guizhi) 9 g; White Peony Root (Baishao) 18 g — doubled from Gui Zhi Tang, nourishes blood
  • Baked Licorice (Zhigancao) 6 g; Fresh Ginger (Shengjiang) 10 g; Jujube (Dazao) 12 pieces; Maltose (Yitang) 30 g
  • 【桂枝湯（桂枝＋白芍）的變方】— 桂枝 9 g；白芍 18 g（從桂枝湯加重白芍2倍，養血）
  • 炙甘草 6 g；生薑 10 g；大棗 12 枚；飴糖 30 g
功效
Functions
For those with chronic deficiency of the five Zang-organs and qi/blood. Warm and tonify the middle Jiao to relieve pain.
五臟跟氣血都長期缺乏的人。溫中補虛，緩急止痛。
主治
Indication
Interior contraction syndrome due to over-consumption (Xu Lao, "vacuity taxation") — warms the middle, disperses cold.
虛勞裡急（虛勞）——溫中、散寒。

歌訣：小建中湯芍藥多，桂薑甘草大棗和，更加飴糖補中氣，虛勞腹冷服之瘥。增入黃耆名亦爾，表虛身痛效無過。又有建中十四味，陰斑勞損起沉痾。十全大補加附子，麥夏蓯蓉仔細哦。治傷寒陽脈澀，陰脈弦，法當腹中急痛者，以此方主之。又傷寒二三日，心中悸而煩者，此方主之。

臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Interior contraction syndrome from over-consumption
  • Abdominal pain relieved by heat and pressure (a yang-deficiency symptom)
  • Consumption symptoms
  • Pale tongue with a whitish coating
  • Thready-wiry, slow pulse
  • 虛勞裡急
  • 腹痛喜溫喜按（陽虛的症狀）
  • 虛勞症狀
  • 舌淡苔白
  • 脈細弦而遲
加減
Modification
  • [Danggui Jianzhong Tang] — severe blood deficiency → add Chinese Angelica Root (Danggui): tonifies blood, enhances blood circulation, relieves smooth-muscle spasm; warms and tonifies qi and blood, relieves urgency and pain (postpartum abdominal pain, anemia, chronic gastroenteritis, with continuous abdominal pain, shortness of qi, or lower abdominal cramping pain radiating to the low back, thready weak pulse)
  • [Huangqi Jianzhong Tang] — severe qi deficiency → add Astragalus Root (Huangqi): warms and tonifies middle qi and deficiency, relieves urgency and pain, harmonizes yin/yang and Ying/Wei (vacuity taxation with interior urgency, various deficiencies/insufficiencies, cold pain in the epigastrium relieved by warmth and pressure — sallow complexion, thin body, reduced food intake, shortness of qi with disinclination to speak, pale tongue with thin white coating)
  • 【當歸建中湯】：血虛較重：加當歸——補血，增強血循環，緩解平滑肌痙攣；溫補氣血，緩急止痛（產後腹痛、貧血、慢性胃腸炎，見腹中疼痛不止，少氣，或小腹拘急攣痛引腰背，脈細弱者）
  • 【黃耆建中湯】：氣虛較重：加黃耆——溫補中氣補虛，緩急止痛，和陰陽、調營衛。虛勞裡急，諸損不足，脘腹冷痛，喜溫喜按（面黃體瘦，飲食減少，少氣懶言，舌淡苔薄白）
Da Jian Zhong Tang
大建中湯【太陰因寒而嚴重腹痛】
Major Construct the Middle Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Sichuan Pepper (Chuanjiao) 3 g — very hot/spicy
  • Ginseng (Renshen) 9 g
  • Dried Ginger (Ganjiang) 6 g
  • Maltose (Yitang) 18 g
  • 蜀椒 3 g——大辣
  • 人蔘 9 g
  • 乾薑 6 g
  • 飴糖 18 g
功效
Functions
Relieve pain by warming the middle Jiao; dispel cold and tonify deficiency, descend rebellion and stop pain.
溫中止痛；祛寒補虛，降逆止痛。
主治
Indication
Severe abdominal cold-pain — deficiency-cold abdominal pain that is relieved by warmth; middle yang weakened, interior cold-yin exuberant, severe epigastric/abdominal pain that cannot be touched by the hand; abdominal fullness with vomiting, poor appetite, or gurgling sounds in the abdomen, pale tongue with a white, slippery coating.
因寒而起的嚴重腹痛——虛寒性腹痛，得溫則痛減者；中陽衰弱，陰寒內盛，脘腹劇痛，手不可近；腹滿嘔吐，納呆，或腹中漉漉有聲，舌淡，苔白滑者。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Weak constitution, interior cold
  • Abdominal pain with weak abdominal muscles
  • Deep, slow, wiry, weak pulse
  • 體虛裡寒
  • 腹痛腹肌無力
  • 脈沉、遲、弦、弱
加減
Modification
（原始資料未列加減內容）
（原始資料未列加減內容）
Wu Zhu Yu Tang
吳茱萸湯【厥陰】
Evodia Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Evodia Fruit (Wuzhuyu) 9 g — can treat vomiting
  • Ginseng (Renshen) 9 g
  • Fresh Ginger (Shengjiang) 18 g — pungent-dispersing, warms the Stomach, descends rebellion, stops vomiting, strengthens the chief herb's effect of descending rebellion and dispersing cold
  • Jujube (Dazao) 4 pieces
  • 吳茱萸 9 g——可治嘔吐
  • 人蔘 9 g
  • 生薑 18 g——辛散溫胃，降逆止嘔，加強主藥降逆止嘔散寒
  • 大棗 4 枚
功效
Functions
Warm the Liver and Stomach, and send qi downward. (Jueyin pattern: treats vertex headache + vomiting. Liver qi stagnation invades the Spleen, causing more localized edema; Liver fire invading the Stomach easily causes vomiting.) Warms the middle and tonifies deficiency, disperses yin and supports yang. A middle-warming, rebellion-descending formula that can treat diseases of the Yangming, Shaoyin, and Jueyin channels.
溫肝暖胃、降逆止嘔。厥陰症：治療頭頂痛＋嘔吐。肝氣滯會犯脾，比較造成局部的水腫；肝火犯胃，容易造成嘔吐。溫中補虛，消陰扶陽。溫中降逆之方，可治陽明、少陰、厥陰三經之病。
主治
Indication
Vomiting and headache due to Liver cold affecting the Stomach (Jueyin pattern) — treats vomiting/diarrhea and cold reversal.
因肝寒犯胃所致的嘔吐及頭痛（厥陰症）——治吐利、寒厥。

歌訣：吳茱萸湯人蔘棗，重用生薑溫胃好，陽明寒嘔少陰利，厥陰頭痛皆能保。治厥陰病乾嘔吐涎沫、頭痛者主之；又少陰病吐利，手足厥冷，煩躁欲死者主之；又食谷欲嘔者，屬陽明也，吳茱萸湯主之，得湯反劇者，屬上焦也。

臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Headache on the top of the head
  • Cold and pain in the Stomach
  • Acid regurgitation
  • Vomiting
  • Pale tongue with a thin, whitish coating
  • Deep, thready pulse, or wiry, slow pulse
  • 頭頂痛
  • 胃部冷痛
  • 吞酸
  • 嘔吐
  • 舌淡苔薄白
  • 脈沉細或弦遲
加減
Modification
  • Severe headache → add Chuanxiong, Danggui
  • Severe vomiting → add Banxia, Sharen, Chenpi
  • 頭痛嚴重：加川芎、當歸
  • 嘔吐嚴重：加半夏、砂仁、陳皮
Si Ni Tang
四逆湯【少陰】
Frigid Extremities Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Aconite Root (Fuzi) 9 g
  • Dried Ginger (Ganjiang) 9 g
  • Baked Licorice (Zhigancao) 12 g
  • 附子 9 g
  • 乾薑 9 g
  • 炙甘草 12 g
老師補充 ＆＆ 服法
附子須先單獨煮一小時，再加入其他兩味藥一起煎煮。
功效
Functions
Rescue the patient from yang collapse. For Heart & Kidney yang deficiency. (Foot edema is due to Kidney yang deficiency; facial edema is due to Lung qi deficiency; abdominal edema is in the middle Jiao, a Liver and Spleen problem.)
回陽救逆。心腎陽虛。腳水腫：是因為腎陽虛。臉水腫：是因為肺氣虛。肚子水腫：在中焦，是肝跟脾的問題。
主治
Indication
Yang exhaustion (Shaoyin pattern) — treats yin-pattern reversal.
陽脫（少陰症）——治陰證厥逆。

歌訣：四逆湯中薑附草，三陰厥逆太陽沈，或益薑蔥蔘芍桔，通陽復脈力能任。治下利清穀，三陰厥逆，惡寒，脈沉而微者，此方主之，此乃溫經救陽之峻劑也。加了茯苓變真武湯。

臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Cold limbs
  • Lying huddled up
  • Vomiting
  • Absence of thirst
  • Abdominal pain
  • Diarrhea
  • Fatigue and sleepiness
  • Whitish, slippery tongue coating
  • Feeble, thready pulse
  • 四肢冷
  • 蜷臥
  • 嘔吐
  • 不渴
  • 腹痛
  • 腹瀉
  • 倦怠嗜睡
  • 舌苔白滑
  • 脈微細
加減
Modification
  • [Renshen Si Ni Tang] — when the pulse becomes really weak → add Ginseng
  • [Tongmai Si Ni Tang] — in case of yin rejecting yang with pseudo-heat → double the dose of dried ginger (when there is already empty heat)
  • [Zhen Wu Tang] — in case of edema → add White Atractylodes (Baizhu), Poria (Fuling) (Heart-yang and Kidney-yang deficiency, will have lower-limb edema, Kidney failure)
  • [Hui Yang Jiu Ji Tang] — for coma → add Ginseng, Rougui, Shexiang (restores yang and rescues from emergency collapse, boosts qi and revives the pulse)
  • 【人蔘四逆湯】：脈變得極弱時：加人蔘
  • 【通脈四逆湯】：陰盛格陽，有假熱象：乾薑加倍（如果已經有虛熱）
  • 【真武湯】：水腫：加白朮、茯苓（心陽腎陽虛，會有下肢水腫，腎衰竭）
  • 【回陽救急湯】：昏迷：加人蔘、肉桂、麝香（回陽救急、益氣生脈）

16 · Interior-Exterior 表裡雙解劑

▍表裡雙解方劑速記 Formulas for Both Interior-Exterior

English中文
Fang Feng Tong Sheng San — a mix of Taiyang + Yangming patterns: alternating chills/fever, headache, constipation.
防風通聖散——太陽＋陽明症的混合：往來寒熱、頭痛、便秘。
Da Chai Hu Tang — a mix of Shaoyang + Yangming patterns: a combination of Xiao Chai Hu Tang (Shaoyang) + Xiao Cheng Qi Tang (Yangming).
大柴胡湯——少陽＋陽明症的混合：小柴胡湯（少陽）＋小承氣湯（陽明）的合方。
Ge Gen Huang Qin Huang Lian Tang — Gegen addresses the exterior pattern, unblocking the Yangming Stomach; Huangqin and Huanglian address the interior pattern; together they treat combined interior-exterior heat, with tenesmus (due to heat and dampness — severe abdominal pain and urgency to defecate, but the fullness/pain persists even after passing stool).
葛根黃芩黃連湯——葛根處理表症通陽明胃，黃芩黃連是裡症，合起來是處理表裡熱，裡急後重的症狀（因為熱與濕，肚子巨痛跟滿急著排便，但上完後一樣覺得肚子巨痛跟塞滿）。
病機分類 Pathogenesis ClassificationEnglish中文
Fang Feng Tong Sheng San — Taiyang exterior with heat in Yangming.
防風通聖散——太陽表＋陽明熱。
Da Chai Hu Tang — Shaoyang exterior with heat in Yangming.
大柴胡湯——少陽表＋陽明熱。
Ge Gen Huang Qin Huang Lian Tang — Yangming exterior & interior damp-heat.
葛根黃芩黃連湯——陽明表裡濕熱。
Wu Ji San — a formula addressing five types of accumulation.
五積散——五種積症的對應方。

▍三種手足冷方劑鑑別 Indications of Three Formulas for Cold Limbs

English中文
Si Ni San (see Group 13) — chief herb: Chaihu. Cold limbs from Liver and Spleen qi stagnation.
四逆散（見第13組）——君藥：柴胡。肝脾氣滯導致的四肢冷。
Si Ni Tang (see Group 15) — chief herb: Fuzi. Cold limbs from Heart and Kidney yang deficiency.
四逆湯（見第15組）——君藥：附子。心腎陽虛導致的四肢冷。
Dang Gui Si Ni Tang — chief herb: Guizhi. A modified variant of Gui Zhi Tang; cold limbs from cold blocking the channels.
當歸四逆湯——君藥：桂枝。是桂枝湯的變方；寒邪阻滯經絡導致的四肢冷。

▍桂枝湯系列變方 Gui Zhi Tang Family of Variants

English中文
Gui Zhi Tang (see Group 10) — exterior wind.
桂枝湯（見第10組）——外感風邪。
Xiao Jian Zhong Tang (see Group 15) — general deficiency with abdominal pain; Gui Zhi Tang with doubled Baishao, plus maltose.
小建中湯（見第15組）——general虛症兼腹痛，桂枝湯的白芍加倍、加麥芽糖。
Dang Gui Si Ni Tang — cold limbs from blockage by cold + blood deficiency; Gui Zhi Tang plus Mutong, Xixin.
當歸四逆湯——寒阻及血虛導致的四肢冷，桂枝湯加木通、細辛。

▍大柴胡湯 vs 防風通聖散 Comparing Two Interior-Exterior Formulas

English中文
Da Chai Hu Tang — Shaoyang + Yangming.
大柴胡湯——少陽＋陽明。
Fang Feng Tong Sheng San — Taiyang + Yangming.
防風通聖散——太陽＋陽明。
Ge Gen Huang Qin Huang Lian Tang — Yangming exterior-interior heat (diarrhea).
葛根黃芩黃連湯——陽明表裡熱（腹瀉）。
Wu Ji San — the formula corresponding to the five types of accumulation.
五積散——五種積症的對應方。
▍EXAM FOCUS — 本組出題重點
  • 本組三個「表裡雙解」方（防風通聖散、大柴胡湯、葛根黃芩黃連湯）分別對應太陽+陽明、少陽+陽明、陽明表裡濕熱三種不同的表裡組合，是最核心的鑑別考點。
  • 當歸四逆湯是桂枝湯的變方（加當歸、木通、細辛），與四逆散（君藥柴胡）、四逆湯（君藥附子）三方鑑別是常考題——記住「三方各自的君藥」是最快的辨識方法。
  • 防風通聖散結構複雜，含五組藥（解表、清肺胃熱、清陽明、利尿、補氣血），孕婦忌服、脾虛便溏慎用。
  • 大柴胡湯＝小柴胡湯（去人蔘）＋小承氣湯（去厚朴）＋芍藥，是「合方去藥」邏輯的經典範例，考試常考「去了哪些藥、為什麼」。
  • 五積散因應「五積」（寒、濕、痰、氣、血）分別用藥，是全書中組成邏輯最清楚呈現「一藥治一積」的方劑，適合用來理解方劑設計思路。
Dang Gui Si Ni Tang
當歸四逆湯【血虛且寒阻塞脈】
Danggui Decoction for Frigid Extremities
組成
Ingredients
  • [A modified variant of Gui Zhi Tang] — Cinnamon Twigs (Guizhi) 9 g; White Peony Root (Baishao) 9 g; Licorice (Gancao) 5 g; Jujube (Dazao) 8 pieces
  • Chinese Angelica Root (Danggui) 12 g — tonifies blood and moves blood, warms; Clematis Stem (Mutong) 6 g — increases circulation through the channels; Asarum (Xixin) 1.5 g — a great-heat herb, aromatic, opens orifices, dispels wind and cold, promotes circulation
  • 【桂枝湯的變方】— 桂枝 9 g；白芍 9 g；甘草 5 g；大棗 8 枚
  • 當歸 12 g——補血、行血、溫；木通 6 g——增加經絡的循環；細辛 1.5 g——大熱的藥，香氣通竅、祛風散寒、促進循環
功效
Functions
Warm the channels and dispel cold; nourish blood and move blood. (People with cold limbs usually also have menstrual issues.)
溫經散寒；養血活血。四肢冷的人通常也都會有生理期問題。
📝 課堂筆記（原文僅中文）
四逆散：治長期的肝氣滯（實）犯脾導致四肢寒冷，通常有憂鬱傾向。四逆湯：治療急性的臟腑問題，因為陽脫導致的四肢寒冷。
主治
Indication
Cold hands and feet caused by blood deficiency and cold stagnation (not from yang collapse, but from blockage). Boosts blood, restores the pulse.
因血虛兼寒凝所致的手足冰冷（不是因為陽脫，是因為阻塞）。益血、復脈。

歌訣：當歸四逆桂枝芍，細辛甘草木通著，再加大棗治陰厥，脈細陽虛由血弱。內有久寒加薑茱，發表溫中通脈絡。不用附子及乾薑，助陽過劑陰反灼。治手足厥寒，脈細欲絕者，此方主之。

臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Cold limbs
  • Local black-and-blue marks
  • Limb pain and numbness
  • Aversion to cold with a preference for warmth
  • Pale tongue with a whitish coating
  • Deep, thready pulse
  • 四肢冷
  • 局部瘀青
  • 肢體疼痛麻木
  • 畏寒喜溫
  • 舌淡苔白
  • 脈沉細
加減
Modification
  • Dysmenorrhea → add Lindera Root (Wuyao) and Fennel Fruit (Xiaohuixiang)
  • Severe pain in the lower limb → add Niuxi, Spatholobus Stem (Jixueteng), Quince Fruit (Mugua — the only fruit that stops pain, a common exam item)
  • Abdominal pain and vomiting → add Evodia Fruit (Wuzhuyu) and Fresh Ginger (Shengjiang)
  • [Huangqi Guizhi Wuwu Tang] — for limb numbness with qi and blood deficiency, use Gui Zhi Tang with Gancao replaced by Huangqi (Guizhi warms the channels and stops pain; Rougui can tonify Kidney qi)
  • 經痛：加沒藥、小茴香（四肢冷的人通常也都會有生理期問題）
  • 下肢痛（嚴重）：加牛膝、雞血藤、木瓜（考試常考，唯一可以止痛的水果）
  • 腹痛嘔吐：加吳茱萸、生薑
  • 【黃耆桂枝五物湯】：肢體麻木兼氣血兩虛：用桂枝湯，甘草改黃耆（桂枝可溫經止痛，肉桂可以補腎氣）
Fang Feng Tong Sheng San
防風通聖散【太陽＋陽明】
Ledebouriella Powder that Sagely Unblocks
組成
Ingredients
  • [Five groups of herbs]
  • Release exterior — Fangfeng, Jingjie, Bohe, Shengjiang, Mahuang (for exterior wind-cold)
  • Clear heat from Lung and Stomach — Shigao, Huangqin, Lianqiao, Jiegeng
  • Clear heat from Yangming — Dahuang, Mangxiao
  • Promote urination — Zhizi, Huashi
  • Tonify qi and blood — Danggui, Baishao, Baizhu, Gancao
  • 【五組藥】
  • 解表 — 防風、荊芥、薄荷、生薑、麻黃（外感風寒）
  • 清肺胃熱 — 石膏、黃芩、連翹、桔梗
  • 清陽明熱 — 大黃、芒硝
  • 利尿 — 梔子、滑石
  • 補氣血 — 當歸、白芍、白朮、甘草
功效
Functions
A classic patent-medicine formula (Taiyang + Yangming). Releases the exterior and attacks the interior, disperses wind and clears heat. A formula that comprehensively treats interior-exterior, qi-blood, and the Triple Burner, with the effects of releasing the exterior, clearing heat, resolving toxin, and purgation. Sweating does not damage the exterior, purging does not damage the interior — a commonly used interior-exterior formula. Also treats sores, swelling toxin, intestinal wind, and hemorrhoid fistula.
經典的中成藥方（太陽＋陽明）。發表攻裡，疏風清熱。表裡、氣血、三焦通治之劑，具有解表、清熱、解毒、攻下之功效。汗不傷表，下之不傷裡，為表裡通治之常用方。亦治瘡瘍腫毒、腸風痔漏。
主治
Indication
Exterior heat with interior heat and constipation — treats combined exterior-interior excess-heat.
表熱兼裡熱便秘——治表裡實熱。

歌訣：防風通聖大黃硝，荊芥麻黃梔芍翹，甘桔芎歸膏滑石，薄荷芩朮力偏饒，表裏交攻陽熱盛，外科瘍毒總能消。

臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Wind-heat exuberance, both exterior and interior excess
  • Aversion to cold, fever
  • Sore throat, bitter and dry mouth
  • No sweating, vexation
  • Short, dark urine, constipation
  • Greasy, yellow tongue coating
  • Surging, rapid pulse
  • 風熱壅盛、表裡俱實證
  • 惡寒發熱
  • 咽痛、口苦而乾
  • 無汗心煩
  • 小便短赤、大便秘結
  • 舌苔黃膩
  • 脈洪數
加減
Modification

Caution: use cautiously in those with Spleen deficiency and loose stool; contraindicated in pregnancy.

⚠ 使用禁忌：脾虛便溏者慎用；孕婦忌服。

Da Chai Hu Tang
大柴胡湯【少陽＋陽明】
Major Bupleurum Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • [Xiao Chai Hu Tang, omitting Renshen] — Chaihu, Huangqin, Banxia, Gancao, Shengjiang, Dazao
  • [Xiao Cheng Qi Tang, omitting Houpo] — Zhishi, Dahuang
  • Baishao
  • 【小柴胡湯去人蔘】— 柴胡、黃芩、半夏、甘草、生薑、大棗
  • 【小承氣湯去厚朴】— 枳實、大黃
  • 芍藥
功效
Functions
Relieve Shaoyang and drain heat from Yangming.
和解少陽、內瀉陽明熱結。
主治
Indication
Shaoyang syndrome mixed with Yangming syndrome and constipation — releases the exterior, attacks the interior.
少陽症兼陽明症與便秘——發表、攻裡。

歌訣：大柴胡湯用大黃，枳實芩夏白芍將，煎加薑棗表兼裏，妙法內攻併外攘。柴胡芒硝義亦爾，仍有桂枝大黃湯。治太陽病未解便傳入陽明，大便不通，熱實心煩，或寒熱往來，其脈沉實者，以此方下之。

臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Alternating chills and fever
  • Chest/hypochondriac bitter fullness, unceasing vomiting
  • Bitter mouth
  • Depressed, slightly vexed
  • Fullness/pain or stuffiness/hardness below the Heart
  • Unresolved bowel movement, or heat-associated diarrhea
  • Thick, yellow tongue coating
  • Wiry, forceful pulse
  • 往來寒熱
  • 胸脅苦滿，嘔不止
  • 口苦
  • 鬱鬱微煩

Ge Gen Huang Qin Huang Lian Tang
葛根黃芩黃連湯【陽明表裡濕熱】
Kudzu, Scutellaria and Coptis Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Pueraria (Gegen)
  • Coptis (Huanglian)
  • Scutellaria (Huangqin)
  • Licorice (Gancao)
  • 葛根
  • 黃連
  • 黃芩
  • 甘草
功效
Functions
Clear exterior heat and interior damp-heat. Resolves both exterior and interior, clears heat and stops diarrhea.
清表熱與裡濕熱；表裡兩解，清熱止利。
主治
Indication
Exterior heat with diarrhea — treats Taiyang and Yangming patterns together, releasing the exterior while clearing the interior.
表熱兼下利——治太陽、陽明證，解表、清裡。

歌訣：葛根黃芩黃連湯，甘草四般治二陽，解表清裏兼和胃，喘汗自利保平康。治太陽病桂枝證，醫反下之，利遂不止，脈促者，表未解也，喘而汗出者，此湯主之。

  • Heat pathogen entering the interior, body heat

  • Panting with sweating
  • Dry mouth and thirst
  • Yellow coating
  • Rapid pulse

Wu Ji San
五積散
Five Accumulation Powder
組成
Ingredients
  • [For cold] — Mahuang, Baizhi, Ganjiang, Rougui
  • [For dampness] — Cangzhu, Houpo, Fuling
  • [For phlegm] — Banxia
  • [For qi stagnation] — Jiegeng (enters the Lung channel to the throat); Zhishi (breaks qi); Chenpi (moves qi)
  • [For blood stasis] — Danggui, Chuanxiong, Chishao
  • 【治寒】— 麻黃、白芷、乾薑、肉桂
  • 【治濕】— 蒼朮、厚朴、茯苓
  • 【治痰】— 半夏
  • 【治氣滯】— 桔梗（走肺經至喉嚨）；枳實（破氣）；陳皮（行氣）
  • 【治血瘀】— 當歸、川芎、赤芍
功效
Functions
Relieve exterior and interior cold. Releases the exterior, warms the middle, regulates qi and resolves phlegm, moves blood and resolves accumulation.
解表兼溫裡寒。發表溫中、順氣化痰、活血消積。
主治
Indication
Addresses how "Five Accumulations" (cold, dampness, phlegm, qi, blood) relate to the concept of stagnation — releases the exterior, warms the interior.
Five Accumulation是如何治stagnation的概念——發表、溫裡。

歌訣：五積散治五般積，麻黃蒼芷芍歸芎，枳桔桂薑甘茯朴，陳皮半夏加薑蔥。除桂枳陳餘略炒，熟料尤增溫散功。

臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Body heat without sweating
  • Generalized body aches, headache, stiffness of the neck/back/low back/thigh
  • Poor appetite (indigestion, reduced appetite, often a sensation of fullness)
  • Vomiting, abdominal pain, chest fullness, aversion to food
  • Irregular menstruation

Also for exterior wind-cold with internal damage from raw/cold food, body heat without sweating, vomiting and abdominal pain; for women's abdominal pain and irregular menstruation belonging to a cold-congealing, qi-stagnation pattern.

  • 身熱無汗
  • 全身痠痛、頭痛身疼、項背腰股拘急
  • 納呆（消化不良、食慾不振，常有飽滯感）
  • 嘔吐腹痛、胸滿惡食
  • 月經不調

外感風寒、內傷生冷之證，身熱無汗，嘔吐腹痛；婦女心腹疼痛，月經不調等屬寒凝氣滯者。

17 · Tonifying 補益劑

▍補脾氣病機與對應藥物 Spleen Qi Deficiency Aspects and Herbs

English中文
Transformation of food → Dangshen (also can substitute Renshen)
運化食物 → 黨蔘（也可替換成人蔘）
Transformation of water → Baizhu: moving upward becomes phlegm, moving downward becomes diarrhea
運化水濕 → 白朮：往上會變痰，往下會變腹瀉
Governing the muscles → Huangqi: fatigue
主肌肉 → 黃耆：疲勞
Controlling blood circulation → bleeding and blood stasis
統血 → 流血跟瘀血
Yin and yang deficiency (Spleen yang deficiency = Spleen qi deficiency + cold) → Shanyao: cold limbs
陰陽兩虛（脾陽虛＝脾氣虛＋寒） → 山藥：四肢冷

▍補氣常用藥 Herbs for Tonifying Qi

English中文
Ginseng (Renshen) — addresses all issues.
人蔘——處理所有問題。
Pilose Asiabell Root (Dangshen).
黨蔘。
Astragalus Root (Huangqi) — governs the muscles, treats prolapse.
黃耆——掌控肌肉，治療脫垂。
White Atractylodes (Baizhu) — helps the Spleen remove dampness, for Spleen deficiency with water.
白朮——助脾去濕，脾虛伴隨水濕。
Chinese Yam (Shanyao) — tonifies yin, calms the spirit.
山藥——補陰，安神。

Qi deficiency stems from yang deficiency — yang deficiency includes all types of qi deficiency, but yang deficiency additionally includes cold. E.g. lower-back pain is Kidney qi deficiency, frequent urination is Kidney qi deficiency; cold limbs + lower-back pain = Kidney yang deficiency. Diarrhea with undigested food = Spleen yang deficiency; plain diarrhea = Spleen qi deficiency.
氣虛源自陽虛，陽虛包含所有種類的氣虛，但陽虛會再包含寒。例如：下背痛是腎氣虛、頻尿是腎氣虛，四肢冷＋下背痛＝腎陽虛。腹瀉伴隨未消化食物＝脾陽虛，單純腹瀉＝脾氣虛。

▍補氣代表方家族 The Qi-Tonifying Formula Family

English中文
Si Jun Zi Tang — the basic Spleen-deficiency formula.
四君子湯——是基礎的脾虛藥方。
Shen Ling Bai Zhu San — mainly used for diarrhea/watery stool, because Spleen qi deficiency weakens the ability to handle dampness, and excess water causes diarrhea; Fuling handles the water problem.
蔘苓白朮散——主要是腹瀉、水瀉使用，因為脾氣虛導致處理濕氣能力變弱，過多水份造成腹瀉，茯苓用來處理水份問題。
Bu Zhong Yi Qi Tang — mainly addresses more severe Spleen qi deficiency: chronic fatigue, prolapse, fever, surging pulse; can strengthen muscle power, is a stronger qi-tonic. The chief herb is Huangqi.
補中益氣湯——主要處理較嚴重的脾氣虛問題，脾虛的慢性疲勞、脫垂、發燒、洪脈的問題，可增強肌肉的力量，較強力的補氣，君藥是黃耆。
Sheng Mai San — mainly treats yin and qi deficiency together, including chronic fatigue and night sweats; commonly used during a recovery period, the last stage of qi deficiency.
生脈散——主要治療陰虛跟氣虛，包含長期疲勞、盜汗的狀況，常用在恢復期，氣虛的最後階段。

COVID's early stage has excess heat with the "4 Bigs" (high fever, thirst, sweating, surging pulse) — use Zhuye Shigao Tang. During the post-illness recovery period, when yin has been damaged by the excess heat, use Sheng Mai Yin to tonify the qi/blood deficiency.
Covid初期會有實熱有4 Big高熱口渴流汗洪脈，用竹葉石膏湯。等到疫後恢復期時，體內陰被實熱傷到，要用生脈飲補氣血的虛。

＆＆補充比較：陳皮、木香跟香附的差別？都是走氣，都會止痛，但木香走脾胃經（走脾胃經身體中間），香附走肝經（走肝膽經身體側邊）。陳皮走氣幫助消化系統平衡但沒有止痛作用。

▍EXAM FOCUS — 本組出題重點（18方，分五類）
  • 四君子湯（補氣底方）與四物湯（補血底方）是全書補益劑的兩大基礎：八珍湯＝兩方合方；十全大補湯＝八珍湯＋黃耆肉桂；人蔘養榮湯＝十全大補湯去川芎、加陳皮遠志五味子——這條「公式鏈」是最常考的架構題。
  • 補中益氣湯 vs 四君子湯：補中益氣湯是脾虛更嚴重、出現脫垂／發熱／洪脈時使用，藥效比四君子湯強，君藥為黃耆。
  • 當歸補血湯的黃耆：當歸＝5：1是必考比例，用於「有形之血生於無形之氣」的急證補血。
  • 六味地黃丸的「三補三瀉」結構（熟地補腎、山茱萸補肝、山藥補脾／澤瀉瀉腎、丹皮瀉肝、茯苓瀉脾）是全書補陰方的核心架構，其五個變方（杞菊、知柏、麥味、都氣、左慈地黃丸）常考「哪個症狀對應哪個變方」。
  • 大補陰丸 vs 左歸丸 vs 六味地黃丸：三者都補腎陰，但程度遞增——六味地黃丸（基礎，腎陰虛＋虛熱）→大補陰丸（虛熱較重，加知母黃柏瀉相火）→左歸丸（純補真陰，不瀉火，加鹿角膠龜板峻補精髓）。
  • 腎氣丸／濟生腎氣丸／右歸丸三方補陽程度遞增，且腎氣丸是六味地黃丸的變方（加桂枝附子）——常考「腎氣丸與六味地黃丸的關係」。
  • 左歸丸（龜板，補陰）與右歸丸（鹿茸，補陽）是動物藥材對舉的經典例子，常考「哪個用龜板、哪個用鹿茸」。
Si Jun Zi Tang
四君子湯【基礎補脾氣】
Four Gentleman Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Ginseng (Renshen) 10 g — benefits the Spleen, helps digest food (≈ ST36)
  • White Atractylodes (Baizhu) 9 g — benefits the Spleen and transforms water, prevents fluid accumulation (≈ SP9)
  • Poria (Fuling) 9 g — helps Ginseng digest the water within food (≈ ST40)
  • Baked Licorice (Zhigancao) 6 g

The basic Spleen-deficiency formula — whenever there is any other special symptom, Si Junzi Tang alone is not used; it is always combined with other herbs.

  • 人蔘 10 g——益脾，幫助消化食物（＝ST36）
  • 白朮 9 g——益脾轉化水份，預防水份的累積（＝SP9）
  • 茯苓 9 g——幫助人蔘消化食物中的水份（＝ST40）
  • 炙甘草 6 g

是基礎的脾虛藥方，只要有其他特殊症狀就不會單用四君子，四君子湯一定都會搭配其他的藥。

功效
Functions
Tonify qi and strengthen the Spleen. (A basic formula for qi deficiency; simultaneously prevents and treats; a very safe formula. Men's qi deficiency usually begins with Spleen deficiency and develops into overall qi deficiency; women's usually begins with blood deficiency and develops into qi deficiency.)
補氣健脾。基本的脾氣虛藥方，同時預防＆解決，很安全的藥。男人通常氣虛會從脾虛開始，發展至氣虛；女人通常從血虛開始發展至氣虛。

【脾虛四大症狀】沒胃口；疲勞；腹脹；腹瀉水瀉。

主治
Indication
Spleen qi deficiency with poor transformation (Spleen deficiency accompanied by dampness) — assists yin, tonifies qi.
脾氣虛伴隨濕氣問題——助陰、補氣。

歌訣：四君子湯中和義，蔘朮茯苓甘草比，益以夏陳名六君，袪痰補氣陽虛餌，除卻半夏名異功，或加香砂胃寒使。脾氣虛四大特徵：沒食慾、疲勞（因肌肉無力）、腹瀉（無法消化食物中的水份）、腹脹滿。補中益氣湯（會有薄黃苔）藥效比較強，且不需要處理濕氣的問題。

臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Pale complexion
  • Low, weak voice
  • Shortness of breath and lassitude
  • Poor appetite
  • Abdominal fullness
  • Loose stool
  • Pale tongue with white coating
  • Weak pulse
  • 面色蒼白
  • 聲音低弱
  • 氣短倦怠
  • 食慾不振
  • 腹脹
  • 大便溏薄
  • 舌淡苔白
  • 脈弱
加減
Modification
  • [Yigong San] — safer for children than Si Junzi Tang, increases appetite, helps tonify qi and strengthen the Spleen, moves Spleen qi to resolve stagnation. For children with abdominal fullness → add Tangerine Peel (Chenpi)
  • [Liujunzi Tang] — if dampness has reached the Lung causing accompanying phlegm, add the Chenpi/Banxia herb-pair (Chenpi ≈ CV12, Banxia ≈ LU1) → for cough with profuse, thin, white sputum add Chenpi and Banxia
  • [Xiangsha Liujunzi Tang] — adds aromatic herbs to eliminate abdominal fullness, often used for early-pregnancy morning sickness → for abdominal fullness add Costus Root (Muxiang), Amomum Fruit (Sharen)
  • [Bazhen Tang] — commonly used for women's general qi-and-blood insufficiency → for qi and blood deficiency add Danggui, Baishao, Shudihuang, Chuanxiong
  • [Bao Yuan Tang] — for qi deficiency that has developed into yang deficiency, uses Huangqi and Rougui to warm — compare with Si Junzi Tang → add Huangqi, Rougui, Ganjiang
  • 【異功散】：比四君子湯更安全讓小孩用，增加食慾，幫助小孩益氣健脾，行氣化滯：小孩腹滿：加陳皮
  • 【六君子湯】：如果濕氣已經到肺，導致伴隨痰的出現，就要加陳皮半夏（陳皮半夏對藥，陳皮對應穴道CV12，半夏對應穴道LU1）：咳嗽痰稀白量多：加陳皮、半夏
  • 【香砂六君子湯】：增加香氣的草藥，去消除肚子滿脹氣的症狀，常常用於懷孕早期的孕吐症狀：腹滿：加木香、砂仁
  • 【八珍湯】：常常用於女性的general氣血不足：氣血兩虛：加當歸、白芍、熟地、川芎
  • 【保元湯】：因為陽虛而引起的氣虛，用黃耆、肉桂溫暖，要跟四君子湯比較：加黃耆、肉桂、乾薑
Shen Ling Bai Zhu San
蔘苓白朮散【止水瀉】
Ginseng, Poria, and Atractylodes Powder
組成
Ingredients
  • [Tonify Spleen, stop diarrhea] — Ginseng (Renshen), Baizhu, Fuling, Baked Licorice (tonify Spleen qi); Chinese Yam (Shanyao), Lotus Seed (Lianzi) (tonify Spleen and stop diarrhea)
  • [Disinhibit dampness, move qi, promote drainage] — Hyacinth Bean (Biandou), Coix Seed (Yiyiren) (eliminate dampness, promote urination); Amomum Fruit (Sharen) (moves qi for fullness, promotes urination)
  • [Ascend to the Lung] — Platycodon Root (Jiegeng) (ascends to the Lung)
  • 【補脾止瀉】— 人蔘、白朮、茯苓、炙甘草（補脾氣）；山藥、蓮子（補脾止瀉）
  • 【利濕走氣促進排水】— 扁豆、薏苡仁（去濕，促進排尿）；砂仁（行氣除滿，促進排尿）
  • 【上升至肺】— 桔梗（上浮走肺）
功效
Functions
Tonify Spleen qi and transform dampness; boost qi, strengthen the Spleen, harmonize the Stomach and drain dampness. (Spleen-Stomach qi deficiency with accompanying dampness, hence diarrhea/watery stool.)
益氣健脾，和胃滲濕。脾胃氣虛夾濕證，因此會腹瀉水瀉。
主治
Indication
Spleen qi deficiency with dampness — tonifies the Spleen.
脾氣虛兼濕——補脾。

歌訣：蔘苓白朮扁豆陳，山藥甘蓮砂薏仁，桔梗上浮兼保肺，棗湯調服益脾神。

臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Weak limbs
  • Emaciation
  • Undigested food, or vomiting or diarrhea, chest/epigastric distension
  • Sallow complexion
  • Pale tongue with white, greasy coating
  • Empty, moderate pulse
  • 四肢乏力
  • 形體消瘦
  • 飲食不化或吐或瀉，胸脘悶脹
  • 面色萎黃
  • 舌淡苔白膩
  • 脈虛緩
加減
Modification

Bu Zhong Yi Qi Tang
補中益氣湯【脾虛嚴重】
Tonify the Middle and Augment the Qi Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Astragalus Root (Huangqi) 15 g
  • Ginseng (Renshen) 10 g
  • White Atractylodes (Baizhu) 10 g
  • Licorice (Gancao) 5 g
  • Chinese Angelica Root (Danggui) 10 g
  • Tangerine Peel (Chenpi) 6 g
  • Cimicifuga (Shengma) 3 g
  • Bupleurum Root (Chaihu) 3 g
  • 黃耆 15 g
  • 人蔘 10 g
  • 白朮 10 g
  • 甘草 5 g
  • 當歸 10 g
  • 陳皮 6 g
  • 升麻 3 g
  • 柴胡 3 g
功效
Functions
Tonify qi, uplift the Spleen qi. (When tonifying the Spleen, Si Junzi Tang is usually used for lack of appetite, fatigue, watery diarrhea, abdominal distension. When qi deficiency has progressed to the "4 Bigs" [high fever, thirst, sweating, strong pulse], this indicates Spleen deficiency has reached a severe state — use Bu Zhong Yi Qi Tang instead. Its effect is stronger than Si Junzi Tang's.)
補氣，升提脾氣。當補脾時，一般狀況用四君子湯，沒食慾、疲勞、水瀉、腹脹；因氣虛引起4 Big（高溫口渴流汗強脈）出現時，代表脾虛到了嚴重狀況，要用補中益氣湯；補中益氣湯的效果比四君子湯強。
主治
Indication
1. Spleen qi deficiency. 2. Sinking of qi — only appears with severe Spleen deficiency, causing prolapse. 3. Fever with qi deficiency — also only appears with severe Spleen deficiency. Tonifies qi, raises yang.
1. 脾氣虛。2. 氣陷——嚴重的脾虛才出現，會有脫垂的狀況。3. 氣虛發熱——嚴重的脾虛才出現。補氣、升陽。

歌訣：補中益氣耆朮陳，升柴蔘草當歸身，虛勞內傷功獨擅，亦治陽虛外感因。木香蒼朮易歸朮，調中益氣暢脾神。

【重用黃耆的方劑】補中益氣湯；當歸補血湯（緊急補血）；補陽還五湯（見第04組）。

臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Poor appetite
  • Lassitude
  • Loose stools
  • Fever
  • Spontaneous sweating
  • Thirst with a preference for hot drinks
  • Or prolapsed rectum or uterus
  • Chronic diarrhea
  • Pale, large tongue with a thin, whitish coating
  • 食慾不振
  • 倦怠
  • 大便溏薄
  • 發熱
  • 自汗
  • 口渴喜熱飲
  • 或脫肛、子宮脫垂
  • 慢性腹瀉
  • 舌淡胖，苔薄白
加減
Modification
（原始資料未列加減內容）
（原始資料未列加減內容）
Sheng Mai San
生脈散【氣＆陰虛】
Generate the Pulse Powder
組成
Ingredients
  • Ginseng (Renshen) 10 g
  • Ophiopogon Root (Maidong) 15 g — nourishes yin
  • Schisandra Fruit (Wuweizi) 6 g — astringes, prevents night sweats
  • 人蔘 10 g
  • 麥冬 15 g——滋陰
  • 五味子 6 g——收斂，防止盜汗
功效
Functions
Tonify qi and promote body fluids; astringe yin and arrest perspiration.
補氣生津；斂陰止汗。
主治
Indication
Deficiency of qi and yin — preserves the Lung, clears the Heart.
氣陰兩虛——保肺、清心。

歌訣：生脈麥味與人蔘，保肺清心治暑淫，氣少汗多兼口渴，病危脈絕急煎斟。

📝 課堂筆記（原文僅中文）
白虎湯（見第14組）：4 Big，剛開始發燒時。竹葉石膏湯（見第14組）：病情進展到第二階，氣跟陰都虛。生脈飲：病情結束後修復階段。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Lassitude
  • Profuse sweating (qi deficiency)
  • Shortness of breath, fatigue (yang deficiency)
  • Dry throat and thirst (yin deficiency)
  • Or protracted cough with small amounts of sticky sputum
  • Dry, reddened tongue with little coating (yin deficiency)
  • Feeble, rapid pulse
  • 倦怠
  • 大汗（氣虛）
  • 氣短乏力（陽虛）
  • 咽乾口渴（陰虛）
  • 或久咳痰少而黏
  • 舌紅乾少苔（陰虛）
  • 脈微數
加減
Modification
  • Severe damage of yin and body fluids → add Scrophularia (Xuanshen), Fresh Rehmannia Root (Shengdihuang)
  • Prolonged cough → add Stemona Root (Baibu): moistens the Lung, stops cough, kills parasites
  • Palpitations → add Wild Jujube Seed (Suanzaoren), Dragon's Bone (Longgu), Oyster Shell (Muli)
  • Qi and yin deficiency with more heat → replace Renshen with American Ginseng (Xiyangshen)
  • 津液虧損：加玄蔘、生地
  • 慢性咳嗽：加百部（潤肺止咳，殺蟲）
  • 心悸：加酸棗仁、龍骨、牡蠣
  • 氣陰虛而虛火旺：去人蔘，加西洋蔘
Si Wu Tang
四物湯【血虛基礎方】
Four-Substance Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Chief — Prepared Rehmannia Root (Shudihuang) 12 g: tonifies essence to tonify blood
  • Chinese Angelica Root (Danggui) 10 g — helps Shudihuang tonify blood
  • White Peony Root (Baishao) 12 g — helps Shudihuang tonify Liver blood
  • Chuanxiong Rhizome (Chuanxiong) 8 g — moves blood, increases blood circulation

Tonifies blood while moving blood.

  • 君 — 熟地黃 12 g：補精以補血
  • 當歸 10 g——幫助熟地黃補血
  • 白芍 12 g——幫熟地補肝血，補血
  • 川芎 8 g——走血，增加血循環

補血走氣，補血同時要走血。

功效
Functions
Nourish and regulate blood. (A basic blood-deficiency formula, also called "Four Lady Decoction." Tonifying blood must be combined with moving blood, otherwise blood stasis easily results. Si Wu Tang simultaneously tonifies and moves blood; Xiao Yao San addresses Liver invading Spleen.)
補血調血。基本的血虛藥方，也叫Four Lady Decoction。補血的同時要走血，不然容易造成血滯。四物湯是同時補血跟走血，逍遙散是肝犯脾。
主治
Indication
Liver blood deficiency and stagnation — a comprehensive nourish-blood formula.
肝血虛＆滯——養血通劑。

歌訣：四物當歸地芍芎，血家百病此方通，八珍合入四君子，氣血雙療功獨崇，再加黃耆與肉桂，十全大補補方雄，十全除卻耆地草，加粟煎之名胃風。

臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Palpitations
  • Insomnia
  • Dizziness
  • Pale complexion
  • Menstrual disorders (including delayed cycle)
  • Scanty menstruation
  • Or anemia with pains in the lower abdomen
  • Pale tongue, thready pulse
  • 心悸
  • 失眠
  • 頭暈
  • 面色蒼白
  • 月經不調（包含遲經）
  • 經量少
  • 或貧血兼小腹疼痛
  • 舌淡脈細
加減
Modification
  • [Tao Hong Si Wu Tang] — for severe stagnant blood, dysmenorrhea and blood clots → add Peach Seed (Taoren: breaks blood) and Safflower (Honghua: moves blood) (a herb-pair that breaks/moves blood and can induce abortion, analogous to Zhishi/Houpo which break/move qi)
  • Qi stagnation manifested as abdominal distension → add Cyperus Tuber (Xiangfu), Lindera Root (Wuyao), Muxiang (Muxiang and Xiangfu both move qi: Muxiang enters the Spleen-Stomach channel, Xiangfu enters the Liver channel)
  • Qi deficiency manifested as shortness of breath and lassitude → add Renshen, Huangqi
  • Uterine bleeding due to threatened abortion → add Donkey Hide Gelatin (Ejiao), Mugwort Leaf (Aiye)
  • Serious cold manifestations → add Cinnamon Bark (Rougui), Baked Ginger (Paojiang)
  • 【桃紅四物湯】：因為血滯，有經痛跟血塊，加桃仁與紅花（桃仁紅花對藥——破血走血可墮胎，如同枳實厚朴——破氣走氣）
  • 氣滯而腹緊：加香附、烏藥、木香（木香、香附都是走氣：木香走脾胃經，香附走肝經）
  • 氣虛而氣短疲倦：加人蔘、黃耆
  • 卵巢出血而流產：加阿膠、艾葉
  • 身體有寒氣：加肉桂、炮乾薑
Dang Gui Bu Xue Tang
當歸補血湯【急性血虛4大】
Tangkuei Decoction to Tonify Blood
組成
Ingredients
  • Chief — Astragalus Root (Huangqi) 30 g
  • Chinese Angelica Root (Danggui) 6 g

Ratio of Huangqi : Danggui = 5:1 (Huangqi is 5 times Danggui).

  • 君 — 黃耆 30 g
  • 當歸 6 g

黃耆：當歸比例5：1（黃耆是當歸的5倍）。

功效
Functions
Used acutely, tonifies qi to promote blood production. Tangible blood is generated from intangible qi, so the formula heavily uses Huangqi to greatly tonify Spleen-Lung qi, enriching the source of blood generation; Danggui is added to boost blood and harmonize the Ying, so that yang generates and yin grows, qi flourishes and blood is generated.
急性使用，補氣生血。有形之血生於無形之氣，故方中重用黃耆大補脾肺之氣，以裕生血之源；更用當歸益血和營，以使陽生陰長，氣旺血生。
📝 課堂筆記（原文僅中文）
1．緊急嚴重血虛，大失血需輸血或是糖化血色素6以下。
2．因嚴重血虛引起4 Big（高溫口渴流汗孔脈）出現時，感覺高溫但不是實際高溫，雖口渴但無法吞嚥太多水，舌頭蒼白＋孔脈，自發的虛汗，手心是冷的（氣虛的人陽虛所以四肢也是冷的，陰虛的人晚上睡覺時會發熱，血虛從陰虛發展過來），代表血虛到了嚴重狀況，要用當歸補血湯。
主治
Indication
Blood deficiency marked by a feverish body and flushed face, thirsty with a desire for drink, a large but empty pulse.
血虛，身體發燒、臉色潮紅，口渴想喝水，洪脈但伴隨孔脈。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Overwork-induced internal damage
  • Weak qi, blood deficiency
  • Yang floating outward
  • Feverish muscles, flushed face
  • Vexing thirst with desire to drink
  • Also for women's menstrual issues
  • Postpartum blood deficiency with fever and headache
  • Sores/ulcers that fail to heal for a long time
  • Surging, large yet empty pulse ("hollow" pulse)
  • 勞倦內傷
  • 氣弱血虛
  • 陽浮外越
  • 肌熱面赤
  • 煩渴欲飲
  • 以及婦人經行
  • 產後血虛發熱頭痛
  • 瘡瘍潰後，久不癒合
  • 脈洪大而虛、孔脈
加減
Modification
（原始資料未列加減內容）
（原始資料未列加減內容）
Gui Pi Tang
歸脾湯【用腦而心脾兩虛】
Restore the Spleen Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • [Si Junzi Tang] — Ginseng (Renshen) 15 g; White Atractylodes (Baizhu) 30 g; Poria with Wood Spirit (Fushen) 30 g; Baked Licorice (Zhigancao) 8 g
  • [Dang Gui Bu Xue Tang, ratio 5:1, tonifies Heart blood and Spleen qi] — Astragalus Root (Huangqi) 30 g; Chinese Angelica Root (Danggui) 3 g
  • [Calm Shen] — Longan Aril (Longyanrou) 30 g; Wild Jujube Seed (Suanzaoren) 30 g; Polygala Root (Yuanzhi) 3 g
  • [Moves qi, to balance the tonics above] — Costus Root (Muxiang) 15 g
  • 【四君子湯】— 人蔘 15 g；白朮 30 g；茯神 30 g；炙甘草 8 g
  • 【當歸補血湯，比例5:1，補心血脾氣】— 黃耆 30 g；當歸 3 g
  • 【安神】— 龍眼肉 30 g；酸棗仁 30 g；遠志 3 g
  • 【走氣，以平衡上面補藥】— 木香 15 g
功效
Functions
Tonify qi and nourish blood; invigorate the Spleen and Heart. (Heart blood deficiency; overthinking causes combined Heart-Spleen blood deficiency, leading to poor memory. Prone to bruising / breathing unease are also symptoms. Treats inability to stop bleeding, causing continuous menstruation and easy bruising. This formula's effect targets internal organs directly.)
補氣養血；健脾養心。心血虛。過度用腦造成心脾血兩虛，造成記憶力減退的症狀，容易瘀血、呼吸不順暢也是症狀之一。治無法止血，造成月經不止、容易瘀血。藥性走向內臟。
📝 課堂筆記（原文僅中文，治療流血比較）
理中丸（見第15組）；槐花散（見第04組）；歸脾湯——治療氣虛的流血。

理中丸也可以治療月經量過多（脾陽虛血深，脾氣虛血淡）。歸脾湯主要是理氣，不是溫中，理中丸溫中。
主治
Indication
Blood deficiency and bleeding — guides blood back to the Spleen.
血虛及出血——引血歸脾。

歌訣：歸脾湯用朮蔘耆，歸草茯神遠志隨，酸棗木香龍眼肉，煎加薑棗益心脾，怔忡健忘俱可卻，腸風崩漏總能醫。

臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Fatigue due to overthinking
  • Palpitations
  • Poor memory
  • Insomnia
  • Poor appetite, or metrorrhagia
  • Blood in the stool
  • Increased menstrual volume of light color
  • Or dribbling menstruation
  • Pale tongue with a thin, whitish coating
  • Thready, weak pulse
  • 因思慮過度而疲勞
  • 心悸
  • 健忘
  • 失眠
  • 食慾不振或崩漏
  • 便血
  • 經量增多而色淡
  • 或經水淋漓
  • 舌淡苔薄白
  • 脈細弱
加減
Modification
  • Lower-back weakness → add Mulberry Mistletoe (Sangjisheng), Wolfberry Fruit (Gouqizi)
  • Indigestion → add Medicated Leaven (Shenqu), Germinated Barley (Maiya), Hawthorn Fruit (Shanzha)
  • Prolonged bleeding → add Donkey Hide Gelatin (Ejiao), Mugwort Leaf (Aiye)
  • 下背虛：加桑寄生、枸杞子
  • 消化不良：加神曲、麥芽、山楂
  • 長期失血：加阿膠、艾葉
Zhi Gan Cao Tang
炙甘草湯【虛勞補陰陽氣血】
Honey-Fried Licorice Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • [Tonify qi] — Baked Licorice (Zhigancao) 12 g; Ginseng (Renshen) 6 g
  • [Tonify blood] — Donkey Hide Gelatin (Ejiao) 9 g; Jujube (Dazao) 10 pieces
  • [Tonify yin] — Fresh Rehmannia Root (Shengdihuang) 30 g; Ophiopogon Root (Maidong) 10 g; Cannabis Seed (Maziren) 10 g
  • [Warm yang] — Cinnamon Twigs (Guizhi) 9 g; Fresh Ginger (Shengjiang) 9 g
  • 【補氣】— 炙甘草 12 g；人蔘 6 g
  • 【補血】— 阿膠 9 g；大棗 10 枚
  • 【補陰】— 生地黃 30 g；麥冬 10 g；麻仁 10 g
  • 【溫陽】— 桂枝 9 g；生薑 9 g
功效
Functions
Tonify qi, blood, yin, yang, and restore the pulse. (Arrhythmia is mainly due to Heart qi deficiency; an unsettled Heart-spirit is due to Heart blood deficiency, hence the memory decline.)
補陰陽氣血、復脈。心律不整主要因心氣虛，心神不寧是因心血虛所以記憶力減退。
主治
Indication
(1) Mainly used for arrhythmia (with a choppy/intermittent pulse); palpitations and an intermittent pulse. (2) Vacuity taxation (Xu Lao) — all five Zang-organs deficient (Lung atrophy divides into two types: vacuity taxation and yin deficiency). Also treats Cold-damage pattern with a knotted-intermittent pulse and palpitations — also called "Restore the Pulse Decoction"; treats vacuity taxation and Lung atrophy.
(1) 主要使用：心律不整（有結代脈）；心悸伴脈結代。(2) 虛勞，五臟俱虛（肺痿分兩種：虛勞、陰虛）。治傷寒脈結代，心動悸者主之——一名復脈湯，治虛勞、肺痿。

歌訣：炙甘草湯蔘薑桂，麥冬生地大麻仁，大棗阿膠加酒服，虛勞肺痿效如神。

臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Intermittent pulse
  • Lassitude
  • Shortness of breath and protracted cough from qi and blood deficiency (Xu Lao)
  • Dry, reddened tongue with little coating
  • 斷斷續續的脈（結代）
  • 倦怠
  • 因氣血虛造成的氣短＆慢性咳嗽（虛勞）
  • 舌乾紅少苔
加減
Modification
（原始資料未列加減內容）
（原始資料未列加減內容）
Ba Zhen Tang
八珍湯【氣血兩補】
Eight Treasure Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Prepared Rehmannia Root (Shudihuang) 12 g
  • Chinese Angelica Root (Danggui) 10 g
  • White Peony Root (Baishao) 10 g
  • Chuanxiong Rhizome (Chuanxiong) 10 g
  • Ginseng (Renshen) 10 g
  • White Atractylodes (Baizhu) 8 g
  • Poria (Fuling) 8 g
  • Baked Licorice (Zhigancao) 6 g
  • 熟地黃 12 g
  • 當歸 10 g
  • 白芍 10 g
  • 川芎 10 g
  • 人蔘 10 g
  • 白朮 8 g
  • 茯苓 8 g
  • 炙甘草 6 g
老師補充 ＆＆
十全大補湯＝八珍湯加黃耆＋肉桂。人蔘養榮湯＝八珍湯加黃耆＋肉桂，再加遠志＋五味子。
功效
Functions
Tonify both qi and blood. (Ba Zhen Tang = Si Wu Tang [Shudi, Danggui, Baishao, Chuanxiong] + Si Junzi Tang [Renshen, Baizhu, Fuling, Zhigancao]. Its action does not target specific internal organs [unlike Gui Pi Tang, which does] — symptoms determine which formula to use.)
同時補氣血。八珍湯＝四物湯（熟地當歸白芍川芎）＋四君子湯（人蔘白朮茯苓炙甘草）。藥性不走向內臟（歸脾湯走向內臟），要用症狀來決定要用哪個。
主治
Indication
Blood deficiency and stagnation. (Si Junzi tonifies qi, Si Wu tonifies blood.)
血滯血虛。（四君補氣，四物補血）
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Tiredness
  • Poor appetite
  • Palpitations
  • Insomnia
  • Dizziness
  • Pale complexion
  • Scanty menstruation
  • Or anemia with pains in the lower abdomen
  • Pale tongue, thready pulse
  • 倦怠
  • 食慾不振
  • 心悸
  • 失眠
  • 頭暈
  • 臉色蒼白
  • 月經量少
  • 或貧血兼小腹疼痛
  • 舌淡脈細
加減
Modification
  • [Shi Quan Da Bu Tang] — for severe qi deficiency → add Astragalus Root (Huangqi) and Cinnamon Bark (Rougui)
  • [Tai Shan Pan Shi San] ⚠ — for threatened abortion due to qi and blood deficiency and dysfunction of the Chong and Ren Meridians: replace Fuling with Huangqi (tonifies Spleen qi, secures the fetus, prevents miscarriage), Himalayan Teasel Root (Xuduan, tonifies the Kidney and secures the fetus), Huangqin (clears heat to prevent miscarriage), and Sharen (stops vomiting)
  • 【十全大補湯】：氣虛較重：加黃耆跟肉桂
  • 【泰山磐石散】⚠：氣血虧虛、衝任經功能失調引起先兆性流產：以黃耆（補脾氣安胎預防流產）、續斷（補腎安胎）、黃芩（清熱預防流產）、砂仁（止嘔）取代茯苓
Shi Quan Da Bu Tang
十全大補湯
All-Inclusive Great Tonifying Decoction
組成
Ingredients
  • Ginseng; White Atractylodes; Poria; Licorice
  • Chinese Angelica Root; Chuanxiong Rhizome; Prepared Rehmannia Root; White Peony Root
  • Astragalus Root; Cinnamon Bark; Fresh Ginger; Jujube
  • 人蔘；白朮；茯苓；甘草
  • 當歸；川芎；熟地黃；白芍
  • 黃耆；肉桂；生薑；大棗
老師補充 ＆＆
十全大補湯＝八珍湯加黃耆＋肉桂。人蔘養榮湯＝八珍湯加黃耆＋肉桂，再加遠志＋五味子。
功效
Functions
Warmly tonify qi and blood, nourish yin and raise yang. For anemia, adjustment after illness or surgery. Tonifies blood, corrects and reduces anemia, strengthens immune function. (Ba Zhen Tang + Huangqi to help support yang and secure the exterior, + Rougui to guide fire back to its source.)
溫補氣血，滋陰升陽。貧血、病後或術後衰弱之調理。補血，糾正和減輕貧血，增強免疫功能。八珍湯加黃耆助陽固衛，加肉桂引火歸元。
主治
Indication
A greater tonifying formula than Ba Zhen Tang, for more pronounced qi-and-blood deficiency with additional yang deficiency.
較八珍湯更強力的補方，用於氣血兩虛且兼陽虛較明顯者。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Numerous deficiency-related damages
  • Sallow, pale complexion
  • Dizziness, blurred vision
  • Emaciation, poor appetite
  • Weak legs/knees and other deficiency symptoms
  • Empty, weak pulse
  • 諸虛百損
  • 面色蒼白萎黃
  • 頭暈目眩
  • 消瘦納呆
  • 足膝無力等諸虛弱證
  • 脈虛無力
加減
Modification
  • Poor appetite → add Sharen, Shanzha
  • Joint pain → add Niuxi, Xuduan
  • Insomnia with many dreams → add Suanzaoren, Yejiaoteng
  • Nocturnal emission → add Wuweizi, Shanzhuyu, Shanyao, Maidong
  • Heart deficiency with fright/palpitations → combine with Yang Xin Tang
  • Spleen deficiency with body fatigue → combine with Gui Pi Tang
  • 納呆：加砂仁、山查
  • 骨節疼痛：加牛膝、續斷
  • 失眠多夢：加酸棗仁、夜交藤
  • 夢遺：加五味子、山茱萸、山藥、麥冬
  • 心虛驚悸：合養心湯
  • 脾虛體倦：合歸脾湯
Ren Shen Yang Rong Tang

Qi and blood deficiency with anxiety — tonifies qi, nourishes blood.

Liu Wei Di Huang Wan
六味地黃丸【基礎腎陰虛】
Six-Ingredient Pill with Rehmannia
組成
Ingredients
  • [Kidney] — Prepared Rehmannia Root (Shudihuang) 24 g: tonifies blood; Alismatis Rhizome (Zexie) 9 g
  • [Liver] — Dogwood Fruit (Shanzhuyu) 12 g: tonifies Liver yin; Moutan Bark (Mudanpi) 9 g: cools Liver heat
  • [Spleen] — Chinese Yam (Shanyao) 12 g: tonifies Spleen qi and yin; Poria (Fuling) 9 g: clears Spleen heat

Spleen, Liver, and Kidney are each paired as a "tonify yin + clear heat" set — one must clear heat to make room to tonify.

  • 【腎】— 熟地黃 24 g：補血；澤瀉 9 g
  • 【肝】— 山茱萸 12 g：補肝陰；牡丹皮 9 g：涼肝熱
  • 【脾】— 山藥 12 g：補脾氣跟陰；茯苓 9 g：清脾熱

脾肝腎都是補陰跟清熱一組，必須要清熱才有空間可以補。

功效
Functions
Kidney yin deficiency + empty heat, a general pattern. (Liu Wei Di Huang Wan is a modified variant of Shen Qi Wan. Yin deficiency relates to three organs: Kidney, Liver, Spleen.)
腎陰虛＋虛熱，通治性藥方。六味地黃丸是腎氣丸的變方。陰虛關係到三個臟腑：腎、肝、脾。
主治
Indication
Kidney yin deficiency.
腎陰虛。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Soreness and weakness of the waist and knees
  • Vertigo
  • Tinnitus
  • Deafness
  • Night sweats
  • Seminal emissions
  • Feverish sensation in the palms and soles
  • Dry tongue and sore throat
  • Pain in the heels
  • Dribbling urination
  • Reddened tongue with little coating
  • Weak, rapid pulse
  • 腰膝痠軟
  • 眩暈
  • 耳鳴
  • 耳聾
  • 盜汗
  • 遺精
  • 手足心熱
  • 舌乾咽痛
  • 足跟痛
  • 小便淋漓
  • 舌紅少苔
  • 脈弱數
加減
Modification
  • [Qiju Dihuang Wan] — Kidney yin deficiency damaging the Liver, affecting the eyes: for severe dizziness and dry eyes → add Gouqizi, Juhua
  • [Zhibai Dihuang Wan] — more severe Kidney yin deficiency (affecting hormones) causing night sweats and hot flashes, commonly used for women; very cold/cooling, only for short-term treatment of hot flashes — once the fever recedes, return to Liu Wei Di Huang Wan; long-term use easily causes feeling cold → for severe hot flashes and night sweating add Zhimu, Huangbai
  • [Maiwei Dihuang Wan] — Spleen yin deficiency with coughing blood, diabetic patients (qi & yin deficiency, mainly Spleen-Stomach) → for cough with blood add Maidong, Wuweizi
  • [Duqi Wan] — Kidney yin deficiency, Kidney fails to receive qi, unable to transmit sufficient qi to the Lung → for Kidney failing to receive qi add Wuweizi
  • [Zuoci Wan (Erlong Zuoci Wan)] — Kidney yin deficiency tinnitus → for severe ear ringing add Wuweizi, Shichangpu, Cishi
  • 【杞菊地黃丸】：腎陰虛損到肝，影響到眼睛：頭暈眼乾嚴重：加枸杞子、菊花
  • 【知柏地黃丸】：腎陰虛較嚴重（影響到賀爾蒙）造成夜汗潮熱，常用於女性，很寒涼只能短期使用治療潮熱，一但退熱就要退回六味地黃丸，長期服用容易感覺身冷：潮熱盜汗嚴重：加知母、黃柏
  • 【麥味地黃丸】：脾陰虛咳血、糖尿病患者（氣＆陰虛，主要是脾胃）：咳血：加麥冬、五味子
  • 【都氣丸】：腎陰虛，腎不納氣，無法傳足夠的氣給肺：腎不納氣：加五味子
  • 【左慈丸（耳聾左慈丸）】：腎陰虛耳鳴：耳鳴嚴重：加五味子、石菖蒲、磁石

Great Tonify the Yin Pill

老師補充 ＆＆ 製法
將諸藥研末，與豬脊髓蒸熟，蜜糖為丸。
功效
Functions
Kidney yin deficiency with more empty heat, a stronger formula. Nourishes yin essence and drains ministerial fire. Heavily uses Shudi and Guiban to nourish yin and submerge yang, bolstering water to control fire. Huangbai and Zhimu, bitter-cold, drain fire to preserve true yin. Pork spinal cord and honey, sweet and moistening, both nourish essence-marrow to cultivate the root, and moderate the excessive bitter-dryness of Zhimu and Huangbai.
腎陰虛＋虛熱較多，藥效較強。滋陰精而瀉相火。重用熟地、龜板滋陰潛陽，壯水制火；黃柏、知母苦寒泄火以保真陰；更以豬脊髓、蜂蜜甘潤之品，既能滋補精髓以培其本，又能制約知、柏過於苦燥。
主治
Indication
Yin deficiency with more empty heat.
陰虛兼虛熱較多。

Zuo Gui Wan
左歸丸【真陰不足腎陰虛較多】
Restore the Left Pill
組成
Ingredients
  • Prepared Rehmannia Root (Shudihuang) 24 g
  • Dogwood Fruit (Shanzhuyu) 12 g
  • Chinese Yam (Shanyao) 12 g
  • Wolfberry Fruit (Gouqizi) 12 g
  • Tortoise Plastron (Biejia, per source; commonly Guiban) 12 g
  • Cyathula Root (Niuxi) 10 g

Tortoise plastron and deer-antler glue: powerfully tonify essence-marrow — tortoise plastron leans toward tonifying yin, deer antler leans toward tonifying yang. Dodder Seed and Niuxi: benefit the Liver and Kidney, strengthen the low back and knees, strengthen the sinews and bones.

  • 熟地黃 24 g
  • 山茱萸 12 g
  • 山藥 12 g
  • 枸杞子 12 g
  • 龜板（原稿寫作鱉甲）12 g
  • 牛膝 10 g

【龜板、鹿角膠，峻補精髓】龜板偏於補陰，鹿角偏於補陽。【菟絲子、牛膝】益肝腎，強腰膝，健筋骨。

功效
Functions
More severe Kidney yin deficiency + empty heat, a stronger formula. Nourishes yin, tonifies the Kidney, fills essence and boosts marrow. For true yin insufficiency, essence-marrow depletion pattern.
腎陰虛較多＋虛熱，藥效較強。滋陰補腎，填精益髓。真陰不足，精髓虧損證。
主治
Indication
Pure yin deficiency.
純陰虛證。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • True yin insufficiency
  • Dizziness, blurred vision
  • Sore low back and knees
  • Seminal emission/leakage
  • Spontaneous and night sweats
  • Tinnitus, deafness
  • Red tongue with little coating
  • Thready, rapid pulse
  • 真陰不足
  • 頭暈目眩
  • 腰膝痠軟
  • 遺精滑泄
  • 自汗盜汗
  • 耳鳴耳聾
  • 舌紅少苔
  • 脈細數
加減
Modification
  • Weak Spleen-Stomach transportive function → add Chenpi, Sharen
  • Qi deficiency → add Dangshen, Fuling, Baizhu
  • Empty heat → add Shengdi, Mudanpi, Huangbai, Zhimu
  • Excessive sweating → add Floating Wheat (Fuxiaomai)
  • Insomnia → combine with Tian Wang Bu Xin Dan (see Group 01)
  • 脾胃運化乏力：加陳皮、砂仁
  • 氣虛：加黨蔘、茯苓、白朮
  • 虛熱：加生地、丹皮、黃柏、知母
  • 汗多：加浮小麥
  • 不眠：合天王補心丹（見第01組）
Shen Qi Wan
腎氣丸【腎陽虛】
Kidney Qi Pill
組成
Ingredients
  • Prepared Rehmannia (Shudihuang) 240 g
  • Chinese Yam (Shanyao) 120 g
  • Dogwood Fruit (Shanzhuyu) 120 g
  • Alismatis Rhizome (Zexie) 90 g
  • Poria (Fuling) 90 g
  • Moutan Bark (Mudanpi) 90 g
  • Cinnamon Twigs (Guizhi) 30 g
  • Prepared Aconite Root (Fuzi) 30 g
  • 熟地黃 240 g
  • 山藥 120 g
  • 山茱萸 120 g
  • 澤瀉 90 g
  • 茯苓 90 g
  • 牡丹皮 90 g
  • 桂枝 30 g
  • 附子 30 g
老師補充 ＆＆
古代都只用桂枝，但肉桂才比較補腎。
功效
Functions
Tonify Kidney yang. A basic, general formula for treating Kidney yang deficiency; well-balanced, treats chronic problems, safe for long-term use. (Liu Wei Di Huang Wan is a modified variant of Shen Qi Wan.)
補腎陽。基礎＆通治性藥方，治療腎陽虛，藥性較平衡，治療長期問題，可以安全長期使用。（六味地黃丸是腎氣丸的變方）
主治
Indication
Kidney yang deficiency: 1. fatigue, cold body; 2. urination problems; 3. reproductive problems.
腎陽虛：1. 疲勞、身冷；2. 排尿問題；3. 生殖問題。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Soreness and weakness of the waist and knees
  • Edema
  • Cold limbs
  • Dysuria or polyuria
  • Pale, large tongue
  • Weak pulse
  • 腰膝痠軟
  • 水腫
  • 四肢冷
  • 排尿困難或多尿
  • 舌淡胖
  • 脈弱
加減
Modification
  • [Ji Sheng Shenqi Wan] — for edema and dysuria → add Cheqianzi and Niuxi
  • For concomitant impotence → add Yinyanghuo, Buguzhi, and Bajitian
  • 【濟生腎氣丸】：治水腫排尿不順：加牛膝、車前子
  • 陽痿：加淫羊藿、補骨脂、巴戟天
Ji Sheng Shen Qi Wan
濟生腎氣丸【水腫】
Kidney Qi Pill to Aid the Living
組成
Ingredients
  • [Shen Qi Wan base] — Shudihuang; Shanyao; Shanzhuyu; Zexie; Fuling; Mudanpi; Rougui; Fuzi
  • [Added herbs] — Niuxi; Cheqianzi
  • 【腎氣丸底】— 熟地黃；山藥；山茱萸；澤瀉；茯苓；牡丹皮；肉桂；附子
  • 【加味】— 牛膝；車前子
功效
Functions
Tonify Kidney yang, mainly addressing urination and edema (edema from Kidney yang insufficiency). Tonifies the Kidney and assists yang, disinhibits water and reduces swelling, restores normal qi-transformation function; can also disinhibit water and unblock stranguria, letting damp-water leave via the urine so symptoms resolve on their own. Also treats Xiao Ke with a pattern of drinking then urinating an equal amount. (Kidney yang weakness, disordered qi transformation, unable to regulate the waterways — Kidney deficiency causing inability to produce urine. Compare with Zhen Wu Tang: Zhen Wu Tang is a Shaoyin Heart-Kidney deficiency pattern, with palpitations on lying down.)
補腎陽，主要處理排尿、水腫問題（腎陽不足引起之水腫）。補腎助陽，利水消腫，使氣化機能復常，並能利水通淋，使水濕從小便去，則諸症自癒。亦治消渴，飲一溲一。腎陽虛弱、氣化失常、無力通調水道之證，腎虛導致無法產生尿液。相較於真武湯，真武湯是少陰心腎虛，躺下時會心悸。
主治
Indication
Kidney yang insufficiency leading to inability to urinate.
腎陽不足導致無法排尿。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Heaviness in the low back, swollen feet
  • Dysuria
  • Aversion to cold, cold limbs
  • Sore low back and knees
  • Pale tongue with white coating
  • Deep pulse
  • 腰重腳腫
  • 小便不利
  • 畏寒肢冷
  • 腰膝痠軟
  • 舌淡苔白
  • 脈沉
加減
Modification
  • More severe edema → add Zhuling, Zexie
  • Dribbling urination → add Mutong, Bianxu
  • Severe low-back pain → add Duzhong
  • Qi deficiency with body fatigue → add Renshen, Huangqi
  • 水腫較甚：加豬苓、澤瀉
  • 小便淋漓：加木通、萹蓄
  • 腰痛甚：加杜仲
  • 氣虛體倦：加人蔘、黃耆
You Gui Wan
右歸丸【命門火衰】
Restore the Right Pill
組成
Ingredients
  • Prepared Rehmannia (Shudihuang) 240 g
  • Chinese Yam (Shanyao) 120 g
  • Dogwood Fruit (Shanzhuyu) 120 g
  • Wolfberry Fruit / Cibot Rhizome (per source: Gouji) 30 g
  • Cinnamon Bark (Rougui) 30 g
  • Prepared Aconite Root (Fuzi) 30 g
  • Pilose Antler (Lurong) 30 g
  • Eucommia Bark (Duzhong) 30 g
  • Dodder Seed (Tusizi) 30 g
  • Angelica Root (Danggui) 30 g
  • 熟地黃 240 g
  • 山藥 120 g
  • 山茱萸 120 g
  • 枸杞（原稿標示為狗脊）30 g
  • 肉桂 30 g
  • 附子 30 g
  • 鹿茸 30 g
  • 杜仲 30 g
  • 菟絲子 30 g
  • 當歸 30 g
功效
Functions
Tonifies Kidney yang, for more severe symptoms, specifically addressing reproductive-system problems. (Zuo Gui Wan uses Guiban [tortoise plastron]; You Gui Wan uses Lurong [pilose antler] — both contain animal-derived materials.) Warmly tonifies Kidney yang, fills essence and tonifies blood, to nourish the yang origin within the Kidney. For Kidney yang insufficiency, Life Gate fire decline.
補腎陽，較嚴重的症狀，專門處理生殖系統問題。左歸丸（龜板）跟右歸丸（鹿茸）都有動物性藥材。溫補腎陽，填精補血，以收培補腎中元陽之效。腎陽不足，命門火衰。
主治
Indication
Kidney yang deficiency accompanied by declined sexual function.
腎陽虛伴隨性功能低下問題。
臨床表現
Manifestation
  • Chronic illness with yang decline, sore low back and knees
  • Chronic illness with yang decline, fatigued qi and spirit
  • Aversion to cold, cold limbs
  • Impotence, spermatorrhea, or turbid vaginal discharge, loose stool
  • Pale tongue with white coating
  • Thready, deep, slow pulse
  • 久病陽衰腰膝痠軟
  • 久病陽衰氣衰神疲
  • 畏寒肢冷
  • 陽萎滑精或帶濁便溏
  • 舌淡苔白
  • 脈細沉遲
加減
Modification
  • Concomitant qi deficiency → add Renshen, Baizhu, Fuling, Huangqi
  • Yang deficiency with spermatorrhea or turbid discharge/loose stool → add Buguzhi
  • Predawn diarrhea → add Wuweizi, Roudoukou
  • Spleen-Stomach deficiency-cold → add Ganjiang, Wuzhuyu
  • Sore low back and knees → add Hetaorou (walnut meat)
  • Impotence → add Bajitian, Roucongrong, Haigoushen
  • 兼氣虛者：加人蔘、白朮、茯苓、黃耆
  • 陽虛滑精或帶濁便溏：加補骨脂
  • 五更泄瀉：加五味子、肉豆蔻
  • 脾胃虛寒：加乾薑、吳茱萸
  • 腰膝痠痛：加胡桃肉
  • 陽萎：加巴戟天、肉蓯蓉、海狗腎

四君子湯
Si Jun Zi Tang		脾氣虛（基礎）脾胃氣虛，運化無力補氣健脾面白氣短、食少便溏、脈弱補益劑基礎方；一定搭配其他藥使用
蔘苓白朮散
Shen Ling Bai Zhu San		脾虛夾濕（腹瀉）脾胃氣虛，濕滯不化益氣健脾、滲濕止瀉四肢乏力、飲食不化、苔白膩四君子湯加止瀉滲濕藥
補中益氣湯
Bu Zhong Yi Qi Tang		脾虛氣陷（嚴重）中氣下陷，清陽不升補中益氣、升陽舉陷脫肛子宮脫垂、氣虛發熱黃耆為君；效力強於四君子湯
生脈散
Sheng Mai San		氣陰兩虛（恢復期）暑熱傷氣傷津益氣生津、斂陰止汗汗多氣短、咽乾、脈虛數用於病後恢復期；與竹葉石膏湯對比
四物湯
Si Wu Tang		血虛血滯（基礎）肝血不足，血行不暢補血調血面色蒼白、心悸眩暈、脈細補血劑基礎方；補血須兼行血
當歸補血湯
Dang Gui Bu Xue Tang		血虛（急重症）血虛陽浮，虛陽外越補氣生血肌熱面赤、脈洪大而虛黃耆當歸5:1；急證補血代表方
歸脾湯
Gui Pi Tang		心脾兩虛（慢性）思慮傷脾，心血暗耗補氣養血、健脾養心健忘心悸、崩漏便血、脈細弱藥性走向內臟；治氣虛型出血
炙甘草湯
Zhi Gan Cao Tang		陰陽氣血俱虛（虛勞）氣血陰陽俱不足，心失所養益氣滋陰、補血復脈心悸脈結代、舌乾少苔又名復脈湯；兼治肺痿
八珍湯
Ba Zhen Tang		氣血兩虛氣血俱虛，無明顯臟腑偏向益氣補血面色蒼白、心悸失眠、脈細四君子湯＋四物湯合方
十全大補湯
Shi Quan Da Bu Tang		氣血陽俱虛（更重）氣血兩虛兼陽氣不足溫補氣血諸虛百損、脈虛無力八珍湯加黃耆肉桂
人蔘養榮湯
Ren Shen Yang Rong Tang		氣血兩虛兼神志不安氣血不足，心神失養補氣血、安神氣血兩虛兼心悸失眠健忘十全大補湯去川芎加遠志五味子
六味地黃丸
Liu Wei Di Huang Wan		腎陰虛（基礎）肝脾腎三陰不足滋補腎陰腰膝痠軟、盜汗遺精、脈細數三補三瀉結構；五個常用變方
一貫煎
Yi Guan Jian		肝腎陰虛兼氣滯陰虛血燥，肝失條達滋陰疏肝脅痛吞酸、舌乾紅、脈細弦滋陰藥中佐一味川楝子行氣
大補陰丸
Da Bu Yin Wan		腎陰虛（虛熱較重）陰虛火旺，相火妄動滋陰降火骨蒸盜汗、咳血、脈細數較六味地黃丸藥力更強，瀉相火
左歸丸
Zuo Gui Wan		真陰不足（純補不瀉）真陰虧損，精髓不足滋陰補腎、填精益髓頭暈耳鳴、遺精滑泄、脈細數用龜板；純補不清熱，與大補陰丸區分
腎氣丸
Shen Qi Wan		腎陽虛（基礎）腎陽不足，氣化失司補腎助陽腰膝痠軟、畏寒肢冷、脈弱六味地黃丸加桂附而成
濟生腎氣丸
Ji Sheng Shen Qi Wan		腎陽虛水腫腎陽不足，氣化不利，水濕內停補腎助陽、利水消腫腰重腳腫、小便不利、脈沉腎氣丸加牛膝車前子
右歸丸
You Gui Wan		腎陽虛（命門火衰，重症）命門火衰，精血不足溫補腎陽、填精補血畏寒肢冷、陽萎滑精、脈細沉遲用鹿茸；專治生殖系統問題
